    forall([m, s, p], is-seminorm-on(m, s, p) iff p in fun(s, rr) and forall([snx_ in s], 0 <= p(snx_)) and forall([snx_ in s, sny_ in s], p((vadd(m))(snx_, sny_)) <= p(snx_) + p(sny_)) and forall([snl_ in rr, snx_ in s], p((act(m))(snl_, snx_)) = abs(snl_) * p(snx_)))

### is-sent

    forall([ssp_, ssn_], is-sent(ssp_, ssn_) iff node-dir(ssp_, ssn_) = sent)

### is-separable

    forall([s], is-separable(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forsome([dseq in fun(nn, pts(s))], forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], (dist(s))(x, dseq(n)) < eps))))

### is-separate-trace

    forall([sasp_, sas_], is-separate-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sal_ in tuples(msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_)))], length(sas_) = succ(length(sal_)) and nth(1, sas_) = [received, lst(sal_)] and forall([sai_ in nn], 1 <= sai_ implies sai_ <= length(sal_) implies nth(succ(sai_), sas_) = [sent, nth(sai_, sal_)])))

### is-separating-character

    forall([sck_, scl_], is-separating-character(sck_, scl_) iff is-add-character(sck_, scl_) and forall([scx_ in carr(sck_)], scl_(scx_) = 1 iff scx_ in nv-integers(sck_)))

### is-sesquilinear-form

    forall([v, sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) iff sqf in fun(cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), cc) and forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v), z_ in vec(v)], sqf((vadd(v))(x_, y_), z_) = sqf(x_, z_) + sqf(y_, z_)) and forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], sqf((cact(v))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * sqf(x_, y_)) and forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], sqf(y_, x_) = conjugate(sqf(x_, y_))))

### is-sieve

    forall([s, sv], is-sieve(s, sv) iff sv in fun(nstar, power(pts(s))) and forall([svz_ in fun(nn, nn)], forall([sve_], pos-rr(sve_) implies forsome([svb_ in nn], forall([svn_ in nn], svb_ <= svn_ implies diam(s, sv(restrict(svz_, ord-segment(svn_)))) <= sve_))) and forall([svn_ in nn], sv(restrict(svz_, ord-segment(succ(svn_)))) subset sv(restrict(svz_, ord-segment(svn_)))) and forsome([svp_], forall([svn_ in nn], svp_ in sv(restrict(svz_, ord-segment(svn_)))))))

### is-sigma-algebra

    forall([omega, ca], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) iff omega in set and ca subset power(omega) and omega in ca and forall([a_ in ca], complement-in(omega, a_) in ca) and forall([f_ in fun(nn, ca)], big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_)) in ca))

### is-sigma-finite

    forall([omega, ca, mu], is-sigma-finite(omega, ca, mu) iff forsome([sff_ in fun(nn, ca)], forall([sfn_ in nn], mu(sff_(sfn_)) in rr) and big-union(sfk_, nn, sff_(sfk_)) = omega))

### is-sigma-union-of

    forall([cb, es], is-sigma-union-of(cb, es) iff forsome([sug_ in fun(nn, cb)], es = big-union(sun_, nn, sug_(sun_))))

### is-signed-measure

    forall([omega, ca, nu], is-signed-measure(omega, ca, nu) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and nu in fun(ca, rr-star) and nu(empty-set) = 0 and not(forsome([sma_ in ca], nu(sma_) = pos-inf) and forsome([smb_ in ca], nu(smb_) = neg-inf)) and forall([smf_ in fun(nn, ca)], forall([smi_, smj_], smi_ in nn implies smj_ in nn implies not(smi_ = smj_) implies intersection(smf_(smi_), smf_(smj_)) = empty-set) implies ext-series-converges-to(vnb-lambda(smn_, nn, nu(smf_(smn_))), nu(big-union(smn_, nn, smf_(smn_))))))

### is-simple-fn

    forall([omega, ca, f], is-simple-fn(omega, ca, f) iff is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) and card(image(f, omega)) in nn and forall([x_ in omega], f(x_) in rr))

### is-simple-msg

    forall([sbst_, sbsk_, sbsm_], is-simple-msg(sbst_, sbsk_, sbsm_) iff not(forsome([sbsl_ in tuples(msg-set(sbst_, sbsk_))], sbsm_ = lst(sbsl_))))

### is-sn-open

    forall([m, ix, pf, u], is-sn-open(m, ix, pf, u) iff u subset vec(m) and forall([snx_ in u], forsome([snf_ in set], card(snf_) in nn and snf_ subset ix and forsome([sne_], pos-rr(sne_) and sn-ball(m, pf, snf_, snx_, sne_) subset u))))

### is-souslin

    forall([s, e], is-souslin(s, e) iff forsome([ssv_], is-sieve(s, ssv_) and souslin-set(s, ssv_) = e))

### is-square-minus-id

    forall([v, jo], is-square-minus-id(v, jo) iff forall([x_ in vec(v)], jo(jo(x_)) = (vneg(v))(x_)))

### is-standard-borel

    forall([xs, ca], is-standard-borel(xs, ca) iff forsome([sbs_], is-polish(sbs_) and forsome([sbf_ in bijection(xs, pts(sbs_))], is-measurable-map(xs, ca, pts(sbs_), borel-sets(sbs_), sbf_) and forall([sba_ in ca], image(sbf_, sba_) in borel-sets(sbs_)))))

### is-step-fn

    forall([f, a, b], is-step-fn(f, a, b) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and forsome([n_, p_], is-partition(p_, n_, a, b) and forall([i_], i_ in interval(0, n_) and i_ < n_ implies forsome([c_ in rr], forall([t_], t_ in rr and p_(i_) < t_ and t_ < p_(succ(i_)) implies f(t_) = c_)))))

### is-step-map

    forall([omega, ca, s, g], is-step-map(omega, ca, s, g) iff is-measurable-map(omega, ca, pts(s), borel-sets(s), g) and forsome([stv_ in fun(nn, pts(s))], forall([stx_ in omega], forsome([stk_ in nn], g(stx_) = stv_(stk_)))))

### is-strictly-below

    forall([porel, x, y], is-strictly-below(porel, x, y) iff [x, y] in porel and not([y, x] in porel))

### is-strictly-increasing-on

    forall([f, s], is-strictly-increasing-on(f, s) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and s subset rr and forall([u in s, v in s], u < v implies f(u) < f(v)))

### is-subfield-of-cc

    forall([kk], is-subfield-of-cc(kk) iff is-subring-of-cc(kk) and forall([ansf_ in kk], not(ansf_ = 0) implies recip(ansf_) in kk))

### is-subgroup

    forall([sgg_, sgh_], is-subgroup(sgg_, sgh_) iff sgh_ subset carr(sgg_) and iden(sgg_) in sgh_ and forall([sga_ in sgh_, sgb_ in sgh_], (opr(sgg_))(sga_, sgb_) in sgh_) and forall([sga_ in sgh_], (inv(sgg_))(sga_) in sgh_))

### is-sublinear

    forall([m, p], is-sublinear(m, p) iff p in fun(vec(m), rr) and forall([hsx_ in vec(m), hsy_ in vec(m)], p((vadd(m))(hsx_, hsy_)) <= p(hsx_) + p(hsy_)) and forall([hst_ in rr], 0 <= hst_ implies forall([hsx_ in vec(m)], p((act(m))(hst_, hsx_)) = hst_ * p(hsx_))))

### is-submodule

    forall([m, s], is-submodule(m, s) iff s subset vec(m) and vzero(m) in s and forall([x_ in s, y_ in s], (vadd(m))(x_, y_) in s) and forall([x_ in s], (vneg(m))(x_) in s) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in s], (act(m))(r_, x_) in s))

### is-subnet

    forall([d, x, e, y], is-subnet(d, x, e, y) iff is-directed-set(d) and is-directed-set(e) and forsome([snh_], is-cofinal-monotone(e, d, snh_) and forall([snj_ in pts(e)], y(snj_) = x(snh_(snj_)))))

### is-subring-of-cc

    forall([aa], is-subring-of-cc(aa) iff is-cc-subring(aa) and forall([ansn_ in aa], -ansn_ in aa))

### is-subsequence

    forall([s, y, f], is-subsequence(s, y, f) iff f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and y = subseq(f, phi)))

### is-subspace

    forall([m, s], is-subspace(m, s) iff is-submodule(m, s))

### is-subterm

    forall([smt_, smk_, sma_, smb_], is-subterm(smt_, smk_, sma_, smb_) iff [sma_, smb_] in subterm-rel(smt_, smk_))

### is-subterm-closed

    forall([smt_, smk_, smr_], is-subterm-closed(smt_, smk_, smr_) iff forall([sma_ in msg-set(smt_, smk_)], [sma_, sma_] in smr_) and forall([sma_, sml_, smi_], sml_ in tuples(msg-set(smt_, smk_)) implies smi_ in nn implies 1 <= smi_ implies smi_ <= length(sml_) implies [sma_, nth(smi_, sml_)] in smr_ implies [sma_, lst(sml_)] in smr_) and forall([sma_, smg_, smy_], smg_ in msg-set(smt_, smk_) implies smy_ in smk_ implies [sma_, smg_] in smr_ implies [sma_, encr(smg_, smy_)] in smr_))

### is-summable

    forall([grp, f], is-summable(grp, f) iff forsome([r in carr(grp)], sums-to(grp, f, r)))

### is-symmetric-subset

    forall([m, cs], is-symmetric-subset(m, cs) iff cs subset vec(m) and forall([syx_ in cs], (act(m))(-1, syx_) in cs))

### is-taylor-family

    forall([d, n, a, b], is-taylor-family(d, n, a, b) iff n in nn and 1 <= n and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and d(n) in fun(ooint(a, b), rr) and forall([tk_ in nn], tk_ < n implies d(tk_) in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) and is-continuous-on(d(tk_), ccint(a, b)) and forall([tx_ in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(d(tk_), tx_, (d(succ(tk_)))(tx_)))))

### is-tee-trace

    forall([sasp_, sas_], is-tee-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sag_ in msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_))], sas_ = [[received, sag_], [sent, sag_], [sent, sag_]]))

### is-text-trace

    forall([sasp_, sap_, sas_], is-text-trace(sasp_, sap_, sas_) iff forsome([saa_ in sap_], sas_ = [[sent, saa_]]))

### is-token-set

    forall([smt_], is-token-set(smt_) iff smt_ in set and forall([smx_ in smt_], not(smx_ in tuples(set))))

### is-top-closed

    forall([t, a], is-top-closed(t, a) iff a subset pts(t) and complement-in(pts(t), a) in opens(t))

### is-top-family

    forall([ix, tf], is-top-family(ix, tf) iff ix in set and forall([tpi_ in ix], is-top-space(tf(tpi_))))

### is-totally-bounded-in

    forall([tbm_, tba_], is-totally-bounded-in(tbm_, tba_) iff forall([tbr_], pos-rr(tbr_) implies forsome([tbf_], card(tbf_) in nn and tbf_ subset pts(tbm_) and forall([tbp_ in tba_], forsome([tbc_ in tbf_], (dist(tbm_))(tbc_, tbp_) < tbr_)))))

### is-tprod-open

    forall([ix, tf, u], is-tprod-open(ix, tf, u) iff u subset tprod-pts(ix, tf) and forall([tpw_ in u], forsome([tpf_], tpf_ subset tprod-constr(ix, tf) and card(tpf_) in nn and tpw_ in tprod-box(ix, tf, tpf_) and tprod-box(ix, tf, tpf_) subset u)))

### is-tvs

    forall([fld, m, t], is-tvs(fld, m, t) iff is-normed-field(fld) and is-module(m) and scal(m) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(fld) and is-hausdorff(t) and pts(t) == vec(m) and forall([tvx_, tvy_, tvu_], tvx_ in vec(m) implies tvy_ in vec(m) implies tvu_ in opens(t) implies (vadd(m))(tvx_, tvy_) in tvu_ implies forsome([tvv_ in opens(t)], tvx_ in tvv_ and forsome([tvw_ in opens(t)], tvy_ in tvw_ and forall([tva_ in tvv_, tvb_ in tvw_], (vadd(m))(tva_, tvb_) in tvu_)))) and forall([tvr_, tvx_, tvu_], tvr_ in carr(scal(m)) implies tvx_ in vec(m) implies tvu_ in opens(t) implies (act(m))(tvr_, tvx_) in tvu_ implies forsome([tvd_], pos-rr(tvd_) and forsome([tvw_ in opens(t)], tvx_ in tvw_ and forall([tvs_ in carr(scal(m))], (fnrm(fld))((add(scal(m)))(tvs_, (neg(scal(m)))(tvr_))) < tvd_ implies forall([tva_ in tvw_], (act(m))(tvs_, tva_) in tvu_))))))

### is-ufd

    forall([gus_], is-ufd(gus_) iff is-integral-domain(gus_) and forall([gua_ in carr(gus_)], not(gua_ = zero(gus_)) implies forsome([guu_], is-unit(gus_, guu_) and forsome([gun_ in nn, gup_], is-prime-seq(gus_, gup_, gun_) and gua_ = (mul(gus_))(guu_, prod-ring(gus_, gup_, interval(1, gun_)))))) and forall([gun_ in nn, gup_], is-prime-seq(gus_, gup_, gun_) implies forall([gum_ in nn, guq_], is-prime-seq(gus_, guq_, gum_) implies forall([guu_], is-unit(gus_, guu_) implies forall([guv_], is-unit(gus_, guv_) implies (mul(gus_))(guu_, prod-ring(gus_, gup_, interval(1, gun_))) = (mul(gus_))(guv_, prod-ring(gus_, guq_, interval(1, gum_))) implies gun_ = gum_ and forsome([gus2_ in bijection(interval(1, gun_), interval(1, gum_))], forall([gui_ in interval(1, gun_)], associated(gus_, gup_(gui_), guq_(gus2_(gui_))))))))))

### is-ultrametric

    forall([ums_], is-ultrametric(ums_) iff is-metric-space(ums_) and forall([umx_ in pts(ums_), umy_ in pts(ums_), umz_ in pts(ums_)], (dist(ums_))(umx_, umy_) <= max((dist(ums_))(umx_, umz_), (dist(ums_))(umz_, umy_))))

### is-unif-cauchy

    forall([s, fam], is-unif-cauchy(s, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k], k in nn and cap <= k implies forall([l], l in nn and cap <= l implies forall([x in pts(s)], abs((fam(k))(x) - (fam(l))(x)) < eps))))))

### is-unif-limit-on

    forall([fam, g, a, b], is-unif-limit-on(fam, g, a, b) iff fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)) and g in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and forall([eps_], pos-rr(eps_) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_], k_ in nn and n_ <= k_ implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], abs(g(x_) - (fam(k_))(x_)) <= eps_)))))

### is-uniformly-continuous

    forall([s, t, f], is-uniformly-continuous(s, t, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([delta], pos-rr(delta) and forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], (dist(s))(a, b) <= delta implies (dist(t))(f(a), f(b)) <= eps))))

### is-unit

    forall([gus_, guu_], is-unit(gus_, guu_) iff guu_ in carr(gus_) and forsome([guv_ in carr(gus_)], (mul(gus_))(guu_, guv_) = one(gus_)))

### is-upper-bound

    forall([grd, porel, ch, b], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch, b) iff b in grd and forall([x in ch], [x, b] in porel))

### is-upper-triangular

    forall([a, n, d], is-upper-triangular(a, n, d) iff forall([ltu_, ltv_], ltu_ in interval(1, n) implies ltv_ in interval(1, n) implies ltv_ < ltu_ implies entry(d, ltu_, ltv_) = zero(a)))

### little-o-at

    forall([g, a], little-o-at(g, a) iff g in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and forsome([eps in fun(rr, rr)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, eps, a) and eps(a) = 0 and forall([x in rr], g(x) = eps(x) * (x - a))))

### mat-equiv

    forall([a, m, n, c, d], mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, d) iff forsome([u], is-invertible-mat(a, m, u) and forsome([v], is-invertible-mat(a, n, v) and d = matmul(a, matmul(a, u, c), v))))

### meromorphic-on

    forall([mru_, mrf_], meromorphic-on(mru_, mrf_) iff is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), mru_) and forsome([mrs_], mrs_ subset mru_ and holomorphic-on(difference(mru_, mrs_), mrf_) and forall([mrx_ in mrs_], is-removable-singularity(mrf_, mrx_) or forsome([mrn_], is-pole(mrf_, mrx_, mrn_)))))

### net-converges-to

    forall([t, d, x, a], net-converges-to(t, d, x, a) iff is-top-space(t) and is-directed-set(d) and x in fun(pts(d), pts(t)) and a in pts(t) and forall([ncu_ in opens(t)], a in ncu_ implies is-eventually-in(d, x, ncu_)))

### net-unif-conv-on

    forall([d, p, f, k], net-unif-conv-on(d, p, f, k) iff forall([uce_], pos-rr(uce_) implies forsome([uci_ in pts(d)], forall([ucj_ in pts(d)], [uci_, ucj_] in dleq(d) implies forall([ucy_ in k], abs((p(ucj_))(ucy_) - f(ucy_)) < uce_)))))

### npe

    forall([m, s, f, t, g], npe(m, s, f, t, g) iff is-submodule(m, t) and s subset t and is-linear-functional-on(m, t, g) and extends-on(s, g, f) and forall([w_ in t], abs(g(w_)) <= dual-norm-on(m, s, f) * (vnrm(m))(w_)))

### null-homotopic

    forall([hmu_, hmg_, hma_, hmb_], null-homotopic(hmu_, hmg_, hma_, hmb_) iff hmg_(hmb_) = hmg_(hma_) and homotopic(hmu_, hmg_, vnb-lambda(hmx_, ccint(hma_, hmb_), hmg_(hma_)), hma_, hmb_))

### null-rr-seq

    forall([rad], null-rr-seq(rad) iff rad in fun(nn, rr) and forall([k in nn], pos-rr(rad(k))) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([k], k in nn and n <= k implies rad(k) <= eps))))

### occurs-in-space

    forall([ssp_, ssu_], occurs-in-space(ssp_, ssu_) iff forsome([ssn_ in nodes(ssp_)], is-subterm(tokens(ssp_), keys(ssp_), ssu_, node-content(ssp_, ssn_))))

### odd

    forall([x], odd(x) iff forsome([k in zz], x = 2 * k + 1))

### opg-rep

    forall([rpg_, rpa_, rpp_, rpt_, rpy_], opg-rep(rpg_, rpa_, rpp_, rpt_, rpy_) iff 0 <= rpt_ and forsome([rpn_ in nn, rpr_ in rr], 0 <= rpr_ and rpr_ < rpa_ and rpt_ = rpn_ * rpa_ + rpr_ and rpy_ = (opr(rpg_))(gpow(rpg_, rpp_(rpa_), rpn_), rpp_(rpr_))) or rpt_ < 0 and forsome([rpn_ in nn, rpr_ in rr], 0 <= rpr_ and rpr_ < rpa_ and 0 - rpt_ = rpn_ * rpa_ + rpr_ and rpy_ = (inv(rpg_))((opr(rpg_))(gpow(rpg_, rpp_(rpa_), rpn_), rpp_(rpr_)))))

### originates

### padic-frac-rep

    forall([cpp_, cpx_, cpr_], padic-frac-rep(cpp_, cpx_, cpr_) iff cpr_ in qq and forsome([cpz_ in zz, cpm_ in nn], cpr_ * cpp_ ^ cpm_ = cpz_) and (padic-abs(cpp_))(cpx_ + -cpr_) <= 1)

### padic-rel

    forall([cpp_, cpx_, cpv_], padic-rel(cpp_, cpx_, cpv_) iff cpv_ in rr and (cpx_ = 0 and cpv_ = 0 or not(cpx_ = 0) and forsome([cpa_ in zz, cpb_ in zz], not(cpb_ = 0) and cpx_ * cpb_ = cpa_ and forsome([cpi_ in nn, cpj_ in nn], zz-ord-is(cpp_, cpa_, cpi_) and zz-ord-is(cpp_, cpb_, cpj_) and cpv_ * 2 ^ cpi_ = 2 ^ cpj_))))

### pointwise-bounded

### pos-rr

    forall([r], pos-rr(r) iff r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r))

### positively-oriented

    forall([poa_, pob_, poc_], positively-oriented(poa_, pob_, poc_) iff forall([pou_ in interior(cc-ms, conv3(poa_, pob_, poc_))], angle(pob_ - pou_, poa_ - pou_) < pi and angle(poc_ - pou_, pob_ - pou_) < pi and angle(poa_ - pou_, poc_ - pou_) < pi))

### pred-code

### prod-abs-converges

### prod-converges

### prod-converges-to

    forall([pcf_, pcl_], prod-converges-to(pcf_, pcl_) iff pcf_ in fun(nn, cc) and forsome([pcm_ in nn], forall([pck_ in nn], pcm_ <= pck_ implies not(pcf_(pck_) = 0)) and forsome([pcz_ in cc], not(pcz_ = 0) and converges-to(cc-ms, vnb-lambda(pcn_, nn, pprod(vnb-lambda(pcj_, nn, pcf_(pcm_ + pcj_)), pcn_)), pcz_) and pcl_ = pprod(pcf_, pcm_) * pcz_)))

### same-strand-before

    forall([ssp_, ssn_, ssm_], same-strand-before(ssp_, ssn_, ssm_) iff ssn_ in nodes(ssp_) and ssm_ in nodes(ssp_) and nth(2, ssn_) = nth(2, ssm_) and nth(1, ssn_) < nth(1, ssm_))

### same-strand-upto

    forall([ssp_, ssn_, ssm_], same-strand-upto(ssp_, ssn_, ssm_) iff ssn_ in nodes(ssp_) and ssm_ in nodes(ssp_) and nth(2, ssn_) = nth(2, ssm_) and nth(1, ssn_) <= nth(1, ssm_))

### sends-to

    forall([ssp_, ssn_, ssm_], sends-to(ssp_, ssn_, ssm_) iff ssn_ in nodes(ssp_) and ssm_ in nodes(ssp_) and is-sent(ssp_, ssn_) and is-received(ssp_, ssm_) and node-content(ssp_, ssn_) = node-content(ssp_, ssm_))

### seq-compact

    forall([s], seq-compact(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s))], forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and forsome([l in pts(s)], converges-to(s, subseq(f, phi), l)))))

### series-converges

    forall([f], series-converges(f) iff converges(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, series-partial-sum(f, k))))

### series-converges-to

    forall([f, l], series-converges-to(f, l) iff converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, series-partial-sum(f, k)), l))

### simply-connected

    forall([hmu_], simply-connected(hmu_) iff is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), hmu_) and is-connected(cc-ms, hmu_) and forall([hma_, hmb_, hmg_], is-path(hmg_, hma_, hmb_) implies trace(hmg_, hma_, hmb_) subset hmu_ implies hmg_(hmb_) = hmg_(hma_) implies null-homotopic(hmu_, hmg_, hma_, hmb_)))

### smith-staircase

    forall([a, m, n, d, k], smith-staircase(a, m, n, d, k) iff is-diagonal(a, m, n, d) and k <= m and k <= n and forall([i_ in interval(1, k)], not(entry(d, i_, i_) = zero(a))) and forall([i_ in interval(1, m)], not(i_ <= k) implies forall([j_ in interval(1, n)], entry(d, i_, j_) = zero(a))))

### spans

    forall([md, n, u, sm], spans(md, n, u, sm) iff forall([j_ in interval(1, n)], entry(u, j_, 1) in sm) and forall([x_ in sm], forsome([c_ in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md)))], x_ = lincomb(md, n, c_, u))))

    forall([md7af_, md7at_], strict-atom(md7af_, md7at_) iff forsome([md7ax_, md7ay_], (md7af_ = enc-in(md7ax_, md7ay_) or md7af_ = enc-eq(md7ax_, md7ay_)) and (md7at_ in strict-reach(md7ax_) or md7at_ in strict-reach(md7ay_))) or forsome([md7pl_, md7pk_, md7pi_], md7af_ = eapp(10, md7pl_) and nth(1, md7pl_) = econst(md7pk_) and pred-code(md7pk_) and md7pi_ in nn and 2 <= md7pi_ and md7pi_ <= length(md7pl_) and md7at_ in strict-reach(nth(md7pi_, md7pl_))))

### strict-closed

### strict-head

    forall([mchd_], strict-head(mchd_) iff 7 <= mchd_ and mchd_ <= 8 or 11 <= mchd_ and mchd_ <= 19 or 30 <= mchd_ and mchd_ <= 31)

### strict-step

    forall([md7s_, md7t_], strict-step(md7s_, md7t_) iff forsome([md7h_, md7l_, md7i_], md7s_ = eapp(md7h_, md7l_) and strict-head(md7h_) and md7i_ in nn and 1 <= md7i_ and md7i_ <= length(md7l_) and md7t_ = nth(md7i_, md7l_)) or forsome([md7k_, md7a_, md7b_], (md7k_ = 22 or md7k_ = 24 or md7k_ = 26) and md7s_ = ebind(md7k_, 1, [md7a_], md7b_) and md7t_ = md7a_))

### strictly-mono-nn

    forall([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) iff phi in fun(nn, nn) and forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], m < n_ implies phi(m) < phi(n_)))

### subsqn

    forall([y, f], subsqn(y, f) iff forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and y = subseq(f, phi)))

### summable-weight

    forall([w], summable-weight(w) iff w in fun(nn, rr) and forall([n in nn], 0 < w(n)) and series-converges(w))

### sums-to

    forall([grp, f, r], sums-to(grp, f, r) iff forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([fin in set], card(fin) in nn and fin subset dom(f) and forall([ext], ext in set and card(ext) in nn and fin subset ext and ext subset dom(f) implies (nrm(grp))((opr(grp))(r, (inv(grp))(finsum(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(grp), f, ext)))) < eps))))

### taylor-differentiable

    forall([f, a, x, n], taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) iff forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t in ccint(a, x)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, nth-deriv(f, k), t))) and forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t], a < t and t < x implies is-diff-at(nth-deriv(f, k), t, (nth-deriv(f, succ(k)))(t)))))

### taylor-differentiable-v

    forall([m, f, a, x, n], taylor-differentiable-v(m, f, a, x, n) iff forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t in ccint(a, x)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, nvs-metric-space(m), nth-deriv-v(m, f, k), t))) and forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t], a < t and t < x implies is-diff-at-v(m, nth-deriv-v(m, f, k), t, (nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(k)))(t)))))

### top-converges-to

    forall([t, f, l], top-converges-to(t, f, l) iff f in fun(nn, pts(t)) and l in pts(t) and forall([ut_ in opens(t)], l in ut_ implies forsome([nt_ in nn], forall([mt_ in nn], nt_ <= mt_ implies f(mt_) in ut_))))

### total-head

    forall([mchd_], total-head(mchd_) iff 11 <= mchd_ and mchd_ <= 14 or mchd_ = 19 or 30 <= mchd_ and mchd_ <= 31)

### totally-bounded

    forall([s], totally-bounded(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies forsome([f], card(f) in nn and is-r-net(s, f, pts(s), r))))

### true-closed

    forall([md7v_], true-closed(md7v_) iff forall([md7m_, md7n_], md7m_ in md7v_ implies true-step(md7m_, md7n_) implies md7n_ in md7v_))

### true-step

    forall([md7f_, md7g_], true-step(md7f_, md7g_) iff forsome([md7c_, md7d_], md7f_ = enc-and(md7c_, md7d_) and (md7g_ = md7c_ or md7g_ = md7d_)) or forsome([md7y_, md7o_, md7r_], md7f_ = enc-in(md7y_, enc-sep(md7o_, md7r_)) and md7g_ = expr-open(md7r_, 0, md7y_)))

### unif-on-compacta

    forall([u, fam, f], unif-on-compacta(u, fam, f) iff forall([wcq_], wcq_ subset u implies is-compact(subspace-ms(cc-ms, wcq_)) implies forall([wce_], pos-rr(wce_) implies forsome([wcn_ in nn], forall([wck_ in nn], wcn_ <= wck_ implies forall([wcy_ in wcq_], magnitude((fam(wck_))(wcy_) - f(wcy_)) <= wce_))))))

### uniquely-originates

    forall([ssp_, ssu_], uniquely-originates(ssp_, ssu_) iff forsome([ssn_], originates(ssp_, ssu_, ssn_) and forall([ssm_], originates(ssp_, ssu_, ssm_) implies ssm_ = ssn_)))

### wf-fml

    forall([mce_], wf-fml(mce_) iff 2 in expr-wks(mce_))

### wf-term

    forall([mce_], wf-term(mce_) iff 1 in expr-wks(mce_))

### zeros-accumulate-at

    forall([u, f, a], zeros-accumulate-at(u, f, a) iff forall([acr_], pos-rr(acr_) implies forsome([acx_ in u], acx_ in ball(cc-ms, a, acr_) and not(acx_ = a) and f(acx_) = 0)))

### zz-coprime

    forall([a, b], zz-coprime(a, b) iff zz-is-gcd(a, b, 1))

### zz-divides

    forall([a, b], zz-divides(a, b) iff a in zz and b in zz and forsome([c_ in zz], b = a * c_))

### zz-is-gcd

    forall([a, b, d], zz-is-gcd(a, b, d) iff zz-divides(d, a) and zz-divides(d, b) and forall([e_ in zz], zz-divides(e_, a) implies zz-divides(e_, b) implies zz-divides(e_, d)))

### zz-is-gcd-set

    forall([s, d], zz-is-gcd-set(s, d) iff d in zz and forall([gsx_ in s], zz-divides(d, gsx_)) and forall([gse_ in zz], forall([gsx_ in s], zz-divides(gse_, gsx_)) implies zz-divides(gse_, d)))

### zz-ord-is

    forall([cpp_, cpn_, cpk_], zz-ord-is(cpp_, cpn_, cpk_) iff zz-divides(cpp_ ^ cpk_, cpn_) and not(zz-divides(cpp_ ^ succ(cpk_), cpn_)))

### zz-prime

    forall([cpp_], zz-prime(cpp_) iff cpp_ in zz and 1 < cpp_ and forall([cpd_ in zz], zz-divides(cpd_, cpp_) implies cpd_ = 1 or cpd_ = -1 or cpd_ = cpp_ or cpd_ = -cpp_))

## Recursively-defined functoids

Defined by primitive recursion on `NN` (`def-by-nn-recursion`): a `name-zero` base and a `name-succ` step.  The extra parameters range over a class (a ring, a monoid, ...), so each is a **functoid**, not a function.

### cc-partial-prod

    forall([cpf_], cc-partial-prod(cpf_, 0) == 1)

    forall([cpf_, cpn_ in nn], cc-partial-prod(cpf_, succ(cpn_)) == cc-partial-prod(cpf_, cpn_) * cpf_(cpn_))

### comb-kk

    forall([r, x, y], comb-kk(r, x, y, 0) == vnb-lambda(k, zz, if(k = 0, one(r), zero(r))))

    forall([r, x, y, m in nn], comb-kk(r, x, y, succ(m)) == vnb-lambda(k, zz, (add(r))((mul(r))(x, (comb-kk(r, x, y, m))(k - 1)), (mul(r))(y, (comb-kk(r, x, y, m))(k)))))

### conv-level

    forall([cvm_, cve_], conv-level(cvm_, cve_, 0) == cve_)

    forall([cvm_, cve_, cvn_ in nn], conv-level(cvm_, cve_, succ(cvn_)) == {cvz_ in vec(cvm_): forsome([cvx_ in conv-level(cvm_, cve_, cvn_), cvy_ in cve_, cvt_ in rr], 0 <= cvt_ and cvt_ <= 1 and cvz_ = (vadd(cvm_))((act(cvm_))(1 - cvt_, cvx_), (act(cvm_))(cvt_, cvy_)))})

### count-hits

    forall([chs_, chz_], count-hits(chs_, chz_, 0) == 0)

    forall([chs_, chz_, chn_ in nn], count-hits(chs_, chz_, succ(chn_)) == if(chs_(chn_) = chz_, succ(count-hits(chs_, chz_, chn_)), count-hits(chs_, chz_, chn_)))

### dc-iter

    forall([s, a, nxt], dc-iter(s, a, nxt, 0) == a)

    forall([s, a, nxt, n in nn], dc-iter(s, a, nxt, succ(n)) == choice({y_ in s: y_ in nxt(n, dc-iter(s, a, nxt, n))}))

### det

    forall([r, a], det(r, 0, a) == one(r))

    forall([r, n, a], is-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(succ(n), succ(n), carr(r)) implies det(r, succ(n), a) == finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, succ(n)), (mul(r))(mpow(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r), (neg(r))(one(r)), succ(j)), (mul(r))(entry(a, 1, j), det(r, n, minor(a, 1, j, n))))), interval(1, succ(n))))

### enc-ande

    forall([mcl_, mca_], enc-ande(mcl_, mca_, 0) == enc-truth)

    forall([mcl_, mca_, mcn_ in nn], enc-ande(mcl_, mca_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-and(enc-ande(mcl_, mca_, mcn_), enc-in(nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_), mca_)))

### enc-andl

    forall([mcx_, mcl_], enc-andl(mcx_, mcl_, 0) == enc-truth)

    forall([mcx_, mcl_, mcn_ in nn], enc-andl(mcx_, mcl_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-and(enc-andl(mcx_, mcl_, mcn_), enc-in(mcx_, nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_))))

### enc-cartl

    forall([mcx_, mcl_], enc-cartl(mcx_, mcl_, 0) == enc-truth)

    forall([mcx_, mcl_, mcn_ in nn], enc-cartl(mcx_, mcl_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-and(enc-cartl(mcx_, mcl_, mcn_), enc-in(enc-nth(enc-num(succ(mcn_)), mcx_), nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_))))

### enc-cch

    forall([mix_, mia_, min_], enc-cch(mix_, mia_, min_, 0) == enc-eq(mix_, enc-list(idx-list(min_))))

    forall([mix_, mia_, min_, mik_ in nn], enc-cch(mix_, mia_, min_, succ(mik_)) == enc-ex(enc-and(enc-in(ebvar(0), nth(min_ - mik_, mia_)), enc-cch(mix_, mia_, min_, mik_))))

### enc-eql

    forall([mcl_, mca_], enc-eql(mcl_, mca_, 0) == enc-truth)

    forall([mcl_, mca_, mcn_ in nn], enc-eql(mcl_, mca_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-and(enc-eql(mcl_, mca_, mcn_), enc-eq(nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_), nth(succ(mcn_), mca_))))

### enc-orl

    forall([mcx_, mcl_], enc-orl(mcx_, mcl_, 0) == enc-falsity)

    forall([mcx_, mcl_, mcn_ in nn], enc-orl(mcx_, mcl_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-or(enc-orl(mcx_, mcl_, mcn_), enc-in(mcx_, nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_))))

### enc-tcj

    forall([mia_, mib_, min_], enc-tcj(mia_, mib_, min_, 0) == enc-truth)

    forall([mia_, mib_, min_, mik_ in nn], enc-tcj(mia_, mib_, min_, succ(mik_)) == if(mik_ = 0, enc-eq(nth(min_, mia_), nth(min_, mib_)), enc-and(enc-eq(nth(min_ - mik_, mia_), nth(min_ - mik_, mib_)), enc-tcj(mia_, mib_, min_, mik_))))

### expr-stage

    expr-stage(0) == union(cartesian(pair(0, 0), nn), union(cartesian(pair(1, 1), nn), cartesian(pair(2, 2), nn)))

    forall([mxn_ in nn], expr-stage(succ(mxn_)) == union(expr-stage(mxn_), union(cartesian(pair(3, 3), nn, tuples(expr-stage(mxn_))), cartesian(pair(4, 4), nn, nn, tuples(expr-stage(mxn_)), expr-stage(mxn_)))))

### factorial

    factorial(0) = succ(0)

    forall([k in nn], factorial(succ(k)) = succ(k) * factorial(k))

### falling

    forall([n], falling(n, 0) == succ(0))

    forall([n, k in nn], falling(n, succ(k)) == (n - k) * falling(n, k))

### fv-stage

    fv-stage(0) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(0), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 0, pair(nth(2, mxe_), nth(2, mxe_)), empty-set))

    forall([mxn_ in nn], fv-stage(succ(mxn_)) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(succ(mxn_)), if(mxe_ in expr-stage(mxn_), (fv-stage(mxn_))(mxe_), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 3, big-union(mxw_, make-set(nth(3, mxe_)), (fv-stage(mxn_))(mxw_)), union(big-union(mxw_, make-set(nth(4, mxe_)), (fv-stage(mxn_))(mxw_)), (fv-stage(mxn_))(nth(5, mxe_)))))))

### gpow

    forall([gpg_, gpx_], gpow(gpg_, gpx_, 0) == iden(gpg_))

    forall([gpg_, gpx_, gpn_ in nn], gpow(gpg_, gpx_, succ(gpn_)) == (opr(gpg_))(gpx_, gpow(gpg_, gpx_, gpn_)))

### group-famprod

    forall([gfam_], group-famprod(gfam_, 0) == triv-group)

    forall([gfam_, gfn_ in nn], group-famprod(gfam_, succ(gfn_)) == group-prod(group-famprod(gfam_, gfn_), gfam_(succ(gfn_))))

### group-famtuple

    forall([xfam_], group-famtuple(xfam_, 0) == 0)

    forall([xfam_, xfn_ in nn], group-famtuple(xfam_, succ(xfn_)) == [group-famtuple(xfam_, xfn_), xfam_(succ(xfn_))])

### ideal-stage

    forall([sbrt_, sbrk_, sbrks_, sbrs_], ideal-stage(sbrt_, sbrk_, sbrks_, sbrs_, 0) == sbrs_)

    forall([sbrt_, sbrk_, sbrks_, sbrs_, sbrn_ in nn], ideal-stage(sbrt_, sbrk_, sbrks_, sbrs_, succ(sbrn_)) == {sbrm_ in msg-set(sbrt_, sbrk_): forsome([sbrg_ in key-ideal-gen(sbrt_, sbrk_, empty-set, ideal-stage(sbrt_, sbrk_, sbrks_, sbrs_, sbrn_)), sbry_ in sbrks_], sbrm_ = encr(sbrg_, sbry_))})

### lcd-stage

    lcd-stage(0) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(0), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 1, {mxd_ in nn: nth(2, mxe_) < mxd_}, nn))

    forall([mxn_ in nn], lcd-stage(succ(mxn_)) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(succ(mxn_)), if(mxe_ in expr-stage(mxn_), (lcd-stage(mxn_))(mxe_), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 3, {mxd_ in nn: forall([mxw_ in make-set(nth(3, mxe_))], mxd_ in (lcd-stage(mxn_))(mxw_))}, {mxd_ in nn: forall([mxw_ in make-set(nth(4, mxe_))], mxd_ in (lcd-stage(mxn_))(mxw_)) and mxd_ + nth(3, mxe_) in (lcd-stage(mxn_))(nth(5, mxe_))}))))

### mpow

    forall([m, x], mpow(m, x, 0) == iden(m))

    forall([m, x, n in nn], mpow(m, x, succ(n)) == (opr(m))(x, mpow(m, x, n)))

### mprod

    forall([mpm_, mpu_], mprod(mpm_, mpu_, 0) == iden(mpm_))

    forall([mpm_, mpu_, mpn_ in nn], mprod(mpm_, mpu_, succ(mpn_)) == (opr(mpm_))(mprod(mpm_, mpu_, mpn_), mpu_(mpn_)))

### msg-stage

    forall([smt_, smk_], msg-stage(smt_, smk_, 0) == smt_)

    forall([smt_, smk_, smn_ in nn], msg-stage(smt_, smk_, succ(smn_)) == union(msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smn_), union(cartesian(pair(1, 1), tuples(msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smn_))), cartesian(pair(2, 2), msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smn_), smk_))))

### nth-deriv

    forall([f], nth-deriv(f, 0) == f)

    forall([f, n in nn], nth-deriv(f, succ(n)) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, deriv(nth-deriv(f, n), x)))

### nth-deriv-on

    forall([ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_], nth-deriv-on(ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_, 0) == ndvf_)

    forall([ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_, ndvn_ in nn], nth-deriv-on(ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_, succ(ndvn_)) == vnb-lambda(ndvz_, ndvu_, deriv-on(ndvk_, ndvu_, nth-deriv-on(ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_, ndvn_), ndvz_)))

### nth-deriv-v

    forall([m, f], nth-deriv-v(m, f, 0) == f)

    forall([m, f, n in nn], nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, deriv-v(m, nth-deriv-v(m, f, n), x)))

### op-iter

    forall([goi-md_, goi-t_, goi-w_], op-iter(goi-md_, goi-t_, goi-w_, 0) == goi-w_)

    forall([goi-md_, goi-t_, goi-w_, m in nn], op-iter(goi-md_, goi-t_, goi-w_, succ(m)) == goi-t_(op-iter(goi-md_, goi-t_, goi-w_, m)))

### open-stage

    forall([mxu_], open-stage(mxu_, 0) == vnb-lambda([mxe_, mxk_], cartesian(expr-stage(0), nn), if(mxe_ = [1, mxk_], mxu_, mxe_)))

    forall([mxu_, mxn_ in nn], open-stage(mxu_, succ(mxn_)) == vnb-lambda([mxe_, mxk_], cartesian(expr-stage(succ(mxn_)), nn), if(mxe_ in expr-stage(mxn_), (open-stage(mxu_, mxn_))(mxe_, mxk_), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 3, [3, nth(2, mxe_), maplk(open-stage(mxu_, mxn_), nth(3, mxe_), mxk_)], [4, nth(2, mxe_), nth(3, mxe_), maplk(open-stage(mxu_, mxn_), nth(4, mxe_), mxk_), (open-stage(mxu_, mxn_))(nth(5, mxe_), mxk_ + nth(3, mxe_))]))))

### openl

    forall([mcob_, mcol_], openl(mcob_, mcol_, 0) == mcob_)

    forall([mcob_, mcol_, mcon_ in nn], openl(mcob_, mcol_, succ(mcon_)) == expr-open(openl(mcob_, mcol_, mcon_), mcon_, nth(succ(mcon_), mcol_)))

### poch

    forall([pcz_], poch(pcz_, 0) == 1)

    forall([pcz_, pcn_ in nn], poch(pcz_, succ(pcn_)) == poch(pcz_, pcn_) * (pcz_ + pcn_))

### poly-der

    forall([hmt_, hmx_], poly-der(hmt_, hmx_, 0) == aff-der(hmx_(0), hmx_(succ(0)), hmt_(0), hmt_(succ(0))))

    forall([hmt_, hmx_, hmm_ in nn], poly-der(hmt_, hmx_, succ(hmm_)) == juxta(poly-der(hmt_, hmx_, hmm_), aff-der(hmx_(succ(hmm_)), hmx_(succ(succ(hmm_))), hmt_(succ(hmm_)), hmt_(succ(succ(hmm_)))), hmt_(0), hmt_(succ(hmm_)), hmt_(succ(succ(hmm_)))))

### poly-road

    forall([hmt_, hmx_], poly-road(hmt_, hmx_, 0) == aff-seg(hmx_(0), hmx_(succ(0)), hmt_(0), hmt_(succ(0))))

    forall([hmt_, hmx_, hmm_ in nn], poly-road(hmt_, hmx_, succ(hmm_)) == juxta(poly-road(hmt_, hmx_, hmm_), aff-seg(hmx_(succ(hmm_)), hmx_(succ(succ(hmm_))), hmt_(succ(hmm_)), hmt_(succ(succ(hmm_)))), hmt_(0), hmt_(succ(hmm_)), hmt_(succ(succ(hmm_)))))

### pprod

    forall([ppf_], pprod(ppf_, 0) == 1)

    forall([ppf_, ppn_ in nn], pprod(ppf_, succ(ppn_)) == pprod(ppf_, ppn_) * ppf_(ppn_))

### prefix-prod

    forall([ppa_, ppx_], prefix-prod(ppa_, ppx_, 0) == 1)

    forall([ppa_, ppx_, ppn_ in nn], prefix-prod(ppa_, ppx_, succ(ppn_)) == prefix-prod(ppa_, ppx_, ppn_) * ppa_(ppx_(ppn_)))

### prod-ord

    forall([m, f], prod-ord(m, f, 0) == iden(m))

    forall([m, f, n in nn], prod-ord(m, f, succ(n)) == (opr(m))(prod-ord(m, f, n), f(n)))

### reduce

    forall([op, f], reduce(op, f, 1) == f(0))

    forall([op, f, n], n in nn and 1 <= n implies reduce(op, f, succ(n)) == op(reduce(op, f, n), f(n)))

### ring-prod-n

    forall([f], ring-prod-n(f, 0) == zero-ring)

    forall([f, n in nn], ring-prod-n(f, succ(n)) == ring-prod(ring-prod-n(f, n), f(n)))

### rn-maxabs

    forall([rnmx_], rn-maxabs(rnmx_, 0) == 0)

    forall([rnmx_, rnmk_ in nn], rn-maxabs(rnmx_, succ(rnmk_)) == max(rn-maxabs(rnmx_, rnmk_), abs(rnmx_(succ(rnmk_)))))

### set-power

    forall([sqg_, sqv_], set-power(sqg_, sqv_, 0) == singleton(iden(sqg_)))

    forall([sqg_, sqv_, sqk_ in nn], set-power(sqg_, sqv_, succ(sqk_)) == set-prod(sqg_, set-power(sqg_, sqv_, sqk_), sqv_))

### snd-iter

    forall([sim_], snd-iter(sim_, 0) == sim_)

    forall([sim_, sin_ in nn], snd-iter(sim_, succ(sin_)) == nnsnd(snd-iter(sim_, sin_)))

### subst-stage

    forall([mxm_, mxu_], subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, 0) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(0), if(mxe_ = [0, mxm_], mxu_, mxe_)))

    forall([mxm_, mxu_, mxn_ in nn], subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, succ(mxn_)) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(succ(mxn_)), if(mxe_ in expr-stage(mxn_), (subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, mxn_))(mxe_), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 3, [3, nth(2, mxe_), mapl(subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, mxn_), nth(3, mxe_))], [4, nth(2, mxe_), nth(3, mxe_), mapl(subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, mxn_), nth(4, mxe_)), (subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, mxn_))(nth(5, mxe_))]))))

### sum

    forall([r, f], sum(r, f, 0) == zero(r))

    forall([r, f, n in nn], sum(r, f, succ(n)) == (add(r))(sum(r, f, n), f(n)))

### sum-ag

    forall([ag, f], sum-ag(ag, f, 0) == iden(ag))

    forall([ag, f, n in nn], sum-ag(ag, f, succ(n)) == (opr(ag))(sum-ag(ag, f, n), f(n)))

### taylor-poly-v

    forall([m, f, a, x], taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, 0) == (act(m))((x - a) ^ 0 * recip(factorial(0)), (nth-deriv-v(m, f, 0))(a)))

    forall([m, f, a, x, n in nn], taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, succ(n)) == (vadd(m))(taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n), (act(m))((x - a) ^ succ(n) * recip(factorial(succ(n))), (nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)))(a))))

### trinum

    trinum(0) == 0

    forall([n_ in nn], trinum(succ(n_)) == trinum(n_) + succ(n_))

### wks-stage

    wks-stage(0) == vnb-lambda(mce_, expr-stage(0), pair(1, 1))

    forall([mcn_ in nn], wks-stage(succ(mcn_)) == vnb-lambda(mce_, expr-stage(succ(mcn_)), if(mce_ in expr-stage(mcn_), (wks-stage(mcn_))(mce_), if(nth(1, mce_) = 3, {mckk_ in nn: mckk_ = hd-res(nth(2, mce_)) and app-ar-ok(nth(2, mce_), length(nth(3, mce_))) and forall([mcw_ in make-set(nth(3, mce_))], hd-argk(nth(2, mce_)) in (wks-stage(mcn_))(mcw_))}, {mckk_ in nn: mckk_ = hd-bres(nth(2, mce_)) and bind-sig(nth(2, mce_), nth(3, mce_), length(nth(4, mce_))) and forall([mcw_ in make-set(nth(4, mce_))], 1 in (wks-stage(mcn_))(mcw_)) and hd-body(nth(2, mce_)) in (wks-stage(mcn_))(nth(5, mce_))}))))

### zen

    forall([x], zen(x, 0) == choice(x))

    forall([x, alpha in ord], zen(x, succ_ord(alpha)) == choice({y_ in x: forall([c_ in ord], ord-le(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(x, c_) = y_))}))

    forall([x, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zen(x, lam) == choice({y_ in x: forall([c_ in ord], ord-lt(c_, lam) implies not(zen(x, c_) = y_))}))

### zkept

    forall([phi, grd, porel], zkept(phi, grd, porel, 0) == empty-set)

    forall([phi, grd, porel, alpha in ord], zkept(phi, grd, porel, succ_ord(alpha)) == union(zkept(phi, grd, porel, alpha), keep-set(phi, grd, porel, zkept(phi, grd, porel, alpha), alpha)))

    forall([phi, grd, porel, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zkept(phi, grd, porel, lam) == big-union(beta, ord-segment(lam), zkept(phi, grd, porel, beta)))

### zup

    forall([grd, porel], zup(grd, porel, 0) == choice(grd))

    forall([grd, porel, alpha in ord], zup(grd, porel, succ_ord(alpha)) == choice({y_ in grd: is-strictly-below(porel, zup(grd, porel, alpha), y_)}))

    forall([grd, porel, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zup(grd, porel, lam) == choice({y_ in grd: forall([c_ in ord], ord-lt(c_, lam) implies [zup(grd, porel, c_), y_] in porel)}))

## Constants & other defined terms

`def-constant` terms fixed by characterizing axioms.

### ax-class-ext

    ax-class-ext == enc-all(enc-all(enc-imp(enc-all(enc-iff(enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(2)), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(1)))), enc-eq(ebvar(1), ebvar(0)))))

### ax-fun-app

    ax-fun-app == enc-all(enc-all(enc-all(enc-imp(enc-in(ebvar(1), enc-fun1(ebvar(2))), enc-iff(enc-eq(enc-apply(ebvar(1), ebvar(0)), enc-apply(ebvar(1), ebvar(0))), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(2)))))))

### ax-limit-iff

    ax-limit-iff == enc-all(enc-iff(enc-limit(ebvar(0)), enc-and(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-ordc), enc-and(enc-not(enc-eq(ebvar(0), enc-num(0))), enc-not(enc-ex(enc-and(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-ordc), enc-eq(ebvar(1), enc-succo(ebvar(0))))))))))

### ax-mem-set

    ax-mem-set == enc-all(enc-all(enc-imp(enc-in(ebvar(1), ebvar(0)), enc-in(ebvar(1), enc-setc))))

### ax-nn-ind

    ax-nn-ind == enc-all(enc-imp(enc-and(enc-in(enc-num(0), ebvar(0)), enc-all(enc-imp(enc-and(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-nnc), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(1))), enc-in(enc-succ(ebvar(0)), ebvar(1))))), enc-all(enc-imp(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-nnc), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(1))))))

### ax-ord-succ-above

    ax-ord-succ-above == enc-all(enc-imp(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-ordc), enc-ordlt(ebvar(0), enc-succo(ebvar(0)))))

### ax-succ-nn

    ax-succ-nn == enc-all(enc-imp(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-nnc), enc-in(enc-succ(ebvar(0)), enc-nnc)))

### ax-tfi

    ax-tfi == enc-all(enc-imp(enc-all(enc-imp(enc-and(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-ordc), enc-all(enc-imp(enc-ordlt(ebvar(0), ebvar(1)), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(2))))), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(1)))), enc-all(enc-imp(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-ordc), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(1))))))

### ax-zero-nn

    ax-zero-nn == enc-in(enc-num(0), enc-nnc)

### baire-ms

    baire-ms == ultra-seq-ms(nn, vnb-lambda(bwk_, nn, 2))

### cantor-ms

    cantor-ms == product-metric(vnb-lambda(ctk_, nn, rm-two-ms))

### cayley

    cayley == lft(1, -1i, 1, 1i)

### cc-coords

    cc-coords == vnb-lambda(zv_, cc, [real-part(zv_), imag-part(zv_)])

### cc-hat

    cc-hat == union(cc, singleton(cc-inf))

### cc-hat-opens

    cc-hat-opens == {uh_ in power(cc-hat): uh_ subset cc and is-open(cc-ms, uh_) or forsome([kh_ in power(cc)], kh_ subset cc and is-compact(subspace-ms(cc-ms, kh_)) and uh_ = difference(cc-hat, kh_))}

### cc-hat-top

    cc-hat-top = [cc-hat, cc-hat-opens]

### cc-matrices

    cc-matrices == {cmq_ in mat(2, 2, rr): forsome([cma_ in rr, cmb_ in rr], cmq_ = cc-matrix-of(cma_, cmb_))}

### cc-matrix-rep

    cc-matrix-rep == vnb-lambda(cmz_, cc, cc-matrix-of(real-part(cmz_), imag-part(cmz_)))

### cc-ms

    cc-ms = [cc, vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(cc, cc), magnitude(x - y))]

### cc-normed-field

    cc-normed-field = [cc, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, cc, -x_), 0, 1, vnb-lambda(x_, cc, magnitude(x_))]

### cc-of-pair

    cc-of-pair == vnb-lambda(pv_, cartesian(rr, rr), nth(1, pv_) + nth(2, pv_) * 1i)

### cc2-sphere

    cc2-sphere == {hsw_ in cartesian(cc, cc): magnitude(nth(1, hsw_)) * magnitude(nth(1, hsw_)) + magnitude(nth(2, hsw_)) * magnitude(nth(2, hsw_)) = 1}

### clf

    clf == {mce_ in expr-set: wf-fml(mce_) and expr-lc(mce_)}

### closed-intervals

    closed-intervals == {oci_ in power(rr): oci_ = empty-set or forsome([oca_ in rr, ocb_ in rr], oca_ <= ocb_ and oci_ = ccint(oca_, ocb_))}

### clt

    clt == {mce_ in expr-set: wf-term(mce_) and expr-lc(mce_)}

### ctx-set

    ctx-set == {mcg_ in power(clf): card(mcg_) in nn}

### cx-c

    cx-c = [cx-sp, nodes(cx-sp), {cxe_ in cartesian(nodes(cx-sp), nodes(cx-sp)): cxe_ = [[1, 1], [1, 2]] or cxe_ = [[2, 2], [1, 5]] or cxe_ = [[2, 5], [1, 3]] or cxe_ = [[3, 5], [1, 4]] or cxe_ = [[2, 3], [3, 2]] or cxe_ = [[4, 2], [1, 6]] or cxe_ = [[2, 4], [1, 7]] or cxe_ = [[2, 7], [1, 8]] or cxe_ = [[3, 7], [2, 8]] or cxe_ = [[3, 8], [2, 1]]}]

### cx-isp

    cx-isp = [cx-sp, interval(5, 8), empty-set]

### cx-orp

    cx-orp = [cx-isp, interval(10, 11), vnb-lambda(cxn_, interval(10, 11), cxn_ + 20)]

### cx-sp

    cx-sp = [nn, interval(1, 8), interval(30, 33), vnb-lambda(cxi_, interval(1, 8), nth(cxi_, [[[sent, lst([22, 10, 11, encr(lst([20, 22, 10, 11]), 30)])], [received, lst([22, encr(lst([20, 33]), 30)])]], [[received, lst([22, 10, 11, encr(lst([20, 22, 10, 11]), 30)])], [sent, lst([22, 10, 11, encr(lst([20, 22, 10, 11]), 30), encr(lst([21, 22, 10, 11]), 31)])], [received, lst([22, encr(lst([20, 32]), 30), encr(lst([21, 32]), 31)])], [sent, lst([22, encr(lst([20, 32]), 30)])]], [[received, lst([22, 10, 11, encr(lst([20, 22, 10, 11]), 30), encr(lst([21, 22, 10, 11]), 31)])], [sent, lst([22, encr(lst([20, 32]), 30), encr(lst([21, 32]), 31)])]], [[received, lst([22, 10, 11, encr(lst([20, 22, 10, 11]), 30), encr(lst([21, 22, 10, 11]), 31)])], [sent, lst([22, encr(lst([20, 33]), 30), encr(lst([21, 33]), 31)])]], [[received, lst([22, 10, 11, encr(lst([20, 22, 10, 11]), 30), encr(lst([21, 22, 10, 11]), 31)])], [sent, lst([22, 10, 11, encr(lst([20, 22, 10, 11]), 30), encr(lst([21, 22, 10, 11]), 31)])], [sent, lst([22, 10, 11, encr(lst([20, 22, 10, 11]), 30), encr(lst([21, 22, 10, 11]), 31)])]], [[received, lst([22, encr(lst([20, 32]), 30)])]], [[received, lst([22, encr(lst([20, 33]), 30), encr(lst([21, 33]), 31)])], [sent, 22], [sent, encr(lst([20, 33]), 30)], [sent, encr(lst([21, 33]), 31)]], [[received, 22], [received, encr(lst([20, 33]), 30)], [sent, lst([22, encr(lst([20, 33]), 30)])]]])), vnb-lambda(cxk_, interval(30, 33), cxk_)]

### ell-one

    ell-one = {x_ in sqn(cc): series-converges(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude(x_(k_))))}

### ell-two

    ell-two = {x_ in sqn(cc): series-converges(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude(x_(k_)) * magnitude(x_(k_))))}

### enc-falsity

    enc-falsity == eapp(1, [])

### enc-nnc

    enc-nnc == econst(3)

### enc-ordc

    enc-ordc == econst(5)

### enc-setc

    enc-setc == econst(1)

### enc-truth

    enc-truth == eapp(0, [])

### euler-gamma

    euler-gamma == 1 - series-limit(vnb-lambda(egk_, nn, log(succ(succ(egk_))) - log(succ(egk_)) - recip(succ(succ(egk_)))))

### expr-set

    expr-set == big-union(mxn_, nn, expr-stage(mxn_))

### fam-of-list

    forall([l, i], i in nn and succ(i) <= length(l) implies (fam-of-list(l))(i) = nth(succ(i), l))

### gamma-product

    gamma-product == vnb-lambda(egw_, {egu_ in cc: forall([egn_ in nn], not(egu_ = -egn_))}, recip(recip-gamma(egw_)))

### hopf-quotient-map

    hopf-quotient-map == vnb-lambda(hqp_, unit-sphere-cr, homog-point(nth(1, hqp_), 1 - nth(2, hqp_)))

### lft-group

    lft-group = [lfts, vnb-lambda([lfg_, lfh_], cartesian(lfts, lfts), compose(lfg_, lfh_)), id-fun(cc-hat), vnb-lambda(lfg_, lfts, inverse-bij(lfg_, cc-hat, cc-hat))]

### lfts

    lfts == {lff_ in fun(cc-hat, cc-hat): forsome([lfa_ in cc, lfb_ in cc, lfc_ in cc, lfd_ in cc], not(lfa_ * lfd_ - lfb_ * lfc_ = 0) and lff_ = lft(lfa_, lfb_, lfc_, lfd_))}

### nn-add-monoid

    nn-add-monoid = [nn, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(nn, nn), x_ + y_), 0]

### nn-directed

    nn-directed = [nn, {ndp_ in cartesian(nn, nn): nth(1, ndp_) <= nth(2, ndp_)}]

### nn-minus

    forall([k in nn, l in nn], nn-minus(k, l) = if(l <= k, k - l, 0))

### nnreal

    nnreal == {mhr_ in rr: 0 <= mhr_}

### nstar

    nstar == big-union(nsk_, nn, fun(ord-segment(nsk_), nn))

### pi

    pi == iota(cekp_, cekp_ in rr and 0 < cekp_ and forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 iff forsome([cekk_ in zz], cekz_ = cekk_ * ((2 * cekp_) * 1i))))

### qq-field

    qq-field = [qq, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_), 0, 1, difference(qq, singleton(0)), vnb-lambda(x_, difference(qq, singleton(0)), recip(x_))]

### qq-line

    qq-line = [field-as-integral-domain(qq-field), qq, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_), 0, vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_), vnb-lambda([r_, x_], cartesian(qq, qq), r_ * x_)]

### qq-ring

    qq-ring = [qq, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_), 0, 1]

### received

    received = 1

### recip-gamma

    recip-gamma == vnb-lambda(egz_, cc, (egz_ * cc-exp(euler-gamma * egz_)) * (prod-fun(cc, vnb-lambda(c6k_, nn, vnb-lambda(c6x_, cc, (1 - c6x_ * recip((vnb-lambda(c6gk_, nn, -succ(c6gk_)))(c6k_))) * cc-exp(cc-series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(clhk_, nn, recip-star(clhk_) * (c6x_ * recip((vnb-lambda(c6gk_, nn, -succ(c6gk_)))(c6k_))) ^ clhk_), succ((vnb-lambda(c6gk_, nn, 1))(c6k_)))) - 1))))(egz_))

### rm-two

    rm-two == pair(0, 1)

### rm-two-ms

    rm-two-ms == subspace-ms(rr-ms, rm-two)

### rr-bounded-ms

    rr-bounded-ms == bdd-metric(rr-ms)

### rr-ext-down

    rr-ext-down == union(rr, singleton(neg-inf))

### rr-ext-up

    rr-ext-up == union(rr, singleton(pos-inf))

### rr-field

    rr-field = [rr, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_), 0, 1, difference(rr, singleton(0)), vnb-lambda(x_, difference(rr, singleton(0)), recip(x_))]

### rr-ms

    rr-ms = [rr, vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(rr, rr), abs(x - y))]

### rr-normed-field

    rr-normed-field = [rr, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_), 0, 1, vnb-lambda(x_, rr, abs(x_))]

### rr-nvs

    rr-nvs = [normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), rr, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_), 0, vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_), vnb-lambda([r_, x_], cartesian(rr, rr), r_ * x_), vnb-lambda(x_, rr, abs(x_))]

### rr-pos-star-add-monoid

    rr-pos-star-add-monoid = [rr-pos-star, eplus, 0]

### rw-univ

    rw-univ == cartesian(nn, ctx-set, expr-set, expr-set)

### sent

    sent = 0

### seq-set

    seq-set == cartesian(ctx-set, clf)

### triv-group

    triv-group = [pair(0, 0), vnb-lambda([tgp_, tgq_], cartesian(pair(0, 0), pair(0, 0)), 0), 0, vnb-lambda(tgp_, pair(0, 0), 0)]

### unit-circle

    unit-circle == {ucz_ in cc: magnitude(ucz_) = 1}

### unit-sphere-cr

    unit-sphere-cr == {hsp_ in cartesian(cc, rr): magnitude(nth(1, hsp_)) * magnitude(nth(1, hsp_)) + nth(2, hsp_) * nth(2, hsp_) = 1}

### upper-half-plane

    upper-half-plane == {hpz_ in cc: 0 < imag-part(hpz_)}

### zero-ring

    zero-ring = [{0}, vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian({0}, {0}), 0), vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian({0}, {0}), 0), vnb-lambda(p, {0}, 0), 0, 0]

### zz-ring

    zz-ring = [zz, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, zz, -x_), 0, 1]

## Unfold-only constructors (functoids)

`def-functoid` heads, written `name(params) := body`: the macete rewrites `name(args)` to that body, and there is no theorem — which is why these are absent from the `def-*` registry above.  `FUNCTORS.md` lists the same set split into set/structure-valued and value functoids.

TRAP worth knowing before citing one: `mac` unfolds a functoid in a GOAL, but `mac-h` **cannot** unfold it in an assumption — `def-functoid` installs no theorem for it to look up, so `mac-h` warns `unknown theorem/macete` and the driver continues with the hypothesis untouched.  A constructor whose members get read out of the CONTEXT therefore also carries a membership `iff`, stamped `definitional`, in the section below — that `iff` is a consequence of the definition here, not the definition.

### abs-fn

    abs-fn(omega, f) := vnb-lambda(afx_, omega, abs(f(afx_)))

### adj

    adj(r, n, a) := matof(succ(n), succ(n), vnb-lambda([adu_, adv_], cartesian(interval(1, succ(n)), interval(1, succ(n))), (mul(r))(mpow(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r), (neg(r))(one(r)), adu_ + adv_), det(r, n, minor(a, adv_, adu_, n)))))

### ae-rel

    ae-rel(omega, ca, mu, sf) := {aep_ in cartesian(sf, sf): forsome([aen_ in set], is-null-set(omega, ca, mu, aen_) and forall([aex_ in omega], not(aex_ in aen_) implies (nth(1, aep_))(aex_) = (nth(2, aep_))(aex_)))}

### ae-setoid

    ae-setoid(omega, ca, mu, sf) := [sf, ae-rel(omega, ca, mu, sf)]

### aff-der

    aff-der(hmp_, hmq_, hmc_, hmd_) := vnb-lambda(hmz_, ccint(hmc_, hmd_), recip(hmd_ - hmc_) * (hmq_ - hmp_))

### aff-seg

    aff-seg(hmp_, hmq_, hmc_, hmd_) := vnb-lambda(hmz_, ccint(hmc_, hmd_), hmp_ + ((hmz_ - hmc_) * recip(hmd_ - hmc_)) * (hmq_ - hmp_))

### ag-zz-module

    ag-zz-module(qag_) := [zz-ring, carr(qag_), opr(qag_), iden(qag_), inv(qag_), vnb-lambda([azk_, aza_], cartesian(zz, carr(qag_)), zz-act(qag_, azk_, aza_))]

### agree-length

    agree-length(alx_, aly_) := iota(alk_, alk_ in nn and not(alx_(alk_) = aly_(alk_)) and forall([alj_ in nn], alj_ < alk_ implies alx_(alj_) = aly_(alj_)))

### algebraic-numbers-over

    algebraic-numbers-over(kk) := {anth_ in cc: is-algebraic-over-field(kk, anth_)}

### angle

    angle(ang1_, ang2_) := pi + imag-part(choice({angz_ in cc: -pi <= imag-part(angz_) and imag-part(angz_) < pi and cc-exp(angz_) = -(ang1_ * conjugate(ang2_))}))

### annulus

    annulus(anla_, anlr_, anls_) := {anlz_ in cc: anlr_ < magnitude(anlz_ - anla_) and magnitude(anlz_ - anla_) < anls_}

### antiderivable-fns

    antiderivable-fns(ada_, adb_) := {adp_ in fun(rr, rr): is-antiderivable(adp_, ada_, adb_)}

### atoms

    atoms(ca) := {mat_ in ca: is-atom(ca, mat_)}

### baire-code

    baire-code(bcm_) := vnb-lambda(bck_, nn, nnfst(snd-iter(bcm_, bck_)))

### ball

    ball(s, c, r) := {y in pts(s): (dist(s))(c, y) <= r and not((dist(s))(c, y) = r)}

### ball-cover

    ball-cover(s, r) := image(vnb-lambda(c, pts(s), ball(s, c, r)), pts(s))

### bdd-metric

    bdd-metric(s) := [pts(s), vnb-lambda([u, v], cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), (/)((dist(s))(u, v), 1 + (dist(s))(u, v)))]

### bernstein-basis

    bernstein-basis(n, x) := comb-kk(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), x, 1 - x, n)

### bernstein-poly

    bernstein-poly(f, n, x) := series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(l_, nn, f((/)(l_, n)) * (bernstein-basis(n, x))(l_)), succ(n))

### bichar-of

    bichar-of(chk_, chc_, cha_) := vnb-lambda([chu_, chv_], cartesian(carr(chk_), carr(chk_)), chc_((mul(chk_))(cha_, (mul(chk_))(chu_, chv_))))

### bidual-eval

    bidual-eval(m, x) := vnb-lambda(bdef_, dual-vec(m), bdef_(x))

### bk-f

    bk-f(bfg_, bfv_, bfx_) := subadd-reg(grp-monoid(bfg_), vnb-lambda(bfz_, carr(bfg_), bk-gauge(bfv_, bfz_)), bfx_)

### bk-gauge

    bk-gauge(bgv_, bgx_) := einf({bgr_ in rr: forsome([bgk_ in nn], bgx_ in bgv_(bgk_) and bgr_ = num(1/2) ^ bgk_)})

### bk-metric

    bk-metric(bmg_, bmv_) := [carr(bmg_), vnb-lambda([bmx_, bmy_], cartesian(carr(bmg_), carr(bmg_)), bk-f(bmg_, bmv_, (opr(bmg_))((inv(bmg_))(bmy_), bmx_)) + bk-f(bmg_, bmv_, (opr(bmg_))((inv(bmg_))(bmx_), bmy_)))]

### block

    block(p, k, l) := matof(k, l, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, l)), entry(p, i, j)))

### border

    border(a, b, m, p, q) := matof(succ(p), succ(q), vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, succ(p)), interval(1, succ(q))), if(i = 1, if(j = 1, b, zero(a)), if(j = 1, zero(a), entry(m, nn-minus(i, 1), nn-minus(j, 1))))))

### borel-sets

    borel-sets(m) := sigma-generated(pts(m), {mou_ in power(pts(m)): is-open(m, mou_)})

### c-int

    c-int(phi, a, b) := iota(v_, v_ in rr and forsome([f_], is-antiderivative(f_, phi, a, b) and v_ = f_(b) - f_(a)))

### c-int-or

    c-int-or(phi, a, b) := if(is-antiderivable(phi, a, b), c-int(phi, a, b), if(is-antiderivable(phi, b, a), -c-int(phi, b, a), 0))

### c-metric

    c-metric(s) := c-metric-w(s, vnb-lambda(n, nn, (/)(1, 2 ^ (n + 1))))

### c-metric-w

    c-metric-w(s, w) := [pts(s), vnb-lambda([u, v], cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), iota(l, l in rr and series-converges-to(vnb-lambda(k, nn, w(k) * min(1, ((dists(s))(k))(u, v))), l)))]

### c-pseudo-family

    c-pseudo-family(s) := vnb-lambda(k, nn, [pts(s), (dists(s))(k)])

### cact

    cact(v) := vnb-lambda([cxz_, cxx_], cartesian(cc, vec(v)), (vadd(v))((act(v))(real-part(cxz_), cxx_), (act(v))(imag-part(cxz_), (jop(v))(cxx_))))

### caratheodory-sets

    caratheodory-sets(omega, mu) := {es_ in power(omega): is-caratheodory-measurable(omega, mu, es_)}

### card

    card(a_) := iota(alpha, alpha in ord and forsome([phi], phi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(alpha))) and forall([beta], ord-lt(beta, alpha) implies not(forsome([psi], psi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(beta))))))

### cauchy-setoid

    cauchy-setoid(m) := [cseq(m), crel(m)]

### cc-cont-fns

    cc-cont-fns(u) := {ccf_ in fun(u, cc): forall([ccy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), ccf_, ccy_))}

### cc-cos

    cc-cos(z) := cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cek_, nn, (num(-1) ^ cek_ * recip(factorial(2 * cek_))) * z ^ (2 * cek_)))

### cc-exhaust

    cc-exhaust(u, n) := {cxz_ in cc: magnitude(cxz_) <= n and ball(cc-ms, cxz_, recip(n + 1)) subset u}

### cc-exhaust-seminorms

    cc-exhaust-seminorms(u, v) := vnb-lambda(cfk_, nn, vnb-lambda(cfa_, v, cc-sup-norm(cfa_, cc-exhaust(u, cfk_))))

### cc-exp

    cc-exp(z) := cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cek_, nn, recip(factorial(cek_)) * z ^ cek_))

### cc-fn-module

    cc-fn-module(u, v) := [normed-field-as-commutative-ring(cc-normed-field), v, vnb-lambda([cfa_, cfb_], cartesian(v, v), vnb-lambda(cfz_, u, cfa_(cfz_) + cfb_(cfz_))), vnb-lambda(cfz_, u, 0), vnb-lambda(cfa_, v, vnb-lambda(cfz_, u, -cfa_(cfz_))), vnb-lambda([cfc_, cfa_], cartesian(cc, v), vnb-lambda(cfz_, u, cfc_ * cfa_(cfz_)))]

### cc-fraction-field

    cc-fraction-field(aa) := {anfq_ in cc: forsome([anfa_ in aa, anfb_ in aa], not(anfb_ = 0) and anfq_ * anfb_ = anfa_)}

### cc-hol-fns

    cc-hol-fns(u) := {ccf_ in fun(u, cc): holomorphic-on(u, ccf_)}

### cc-int

    cc-int(pphi_, a, b) := pw-int(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) + pw-int(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) * 1i

### cc-lincomb

    cc-lincomb(n, b, g) := finsum(ring-additive-ag(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(cc-normed-field)), vnb-lambda(almj_, interval(1, n), b(almj_) * g(almj_)), interval(1, n))

### cc-line

    cc-line(clnp_, clnd_) := {clnz_ in cc: imag-part((clnz_ - clnp_) * conjugate(clnd_)) = 0}

### cc-log

    cc-log(clgw_) := iota(clgz_, clgz_ in cc and -pi < imag-part(clgz_) and imag-part(clgz_) < pi and cc-exp(clgz_) = clgw_)

### cc-matrix-of

    cc-matrix-of(a, b) := [[a, b], [-b, a]]

### cc-mid

    cc-mid(cmx_, cmy_) := (cmx_ + cmy_) * recip(2)

### cc-series-limit

    cc-series-limit(f) := iota(cpsl_, cc-series-converges-to(f, cpsl_))

### cc-series-partial-sum

    cc-series-partial-sum(f, k) := sum-ag(normed-field-additive-ag(cc-normed-field), f, k)

### cc-sin

    cc-sin(z) := cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cek_, nn, (num(-1) ^ cek_ * recip(factorial(succ(2 * cek_)))) * z ^ succ(2 * cek_)))

### cc-sup-norm

    cc-sup-norm(u, cpsxs_) := esup(image(vnb-lambda(cpsx_, cpsxs_, magnitude(u(cpsx_))), cpsxs_))

### ccint

    ccint(a, b) := {x in rr: a <= x and x <= b}

### centre-set

    centre-set(s, r, f) := image(vnb-lambda(b, f, choice(centres(s, b, r))), f)

### centres

    centres(s, b, r) := {c in pts(s): ball(s, c, r) = b}

### cert-set

    cert-set(md12t_, md12cg_, md12cw_) := {md12zz_ in expr-set: forall([md12v_ in power(expr-set)], cert-closed(md12t_, md12cg_, md12cw_, md12v_) implies md12zz_ in md12v_)}

### cf-cons

    cf-cons(b, c) := vnb-lambda(pfk_, nn, b * 0 ^ pfk_ + (1 - 0 ^ pfk_) * c(nn-minus(pfk_, 1)))

### char-mat

    char-mat(f, a, n) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([gmi_, gmj_], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), (add(poly(f)))(if(gmi_ = gmj_, monomial(f, one(f), 1), zero(poly(f))), (neg(poly(f)))(monomial(f, entry(a, gmi_, gmj_), 0)))))

### char-poly

    char-poly(f, a, n) := det(poly(f), n, char-mat(f, a, n))

### choose

    choose(n, m) := card(choose-set(n, m))

### choose-set

    choose-set(n, m) := {a in power(ord-segment(n)): card(a) = m}

### cips-j

    cips-j(w) := vnb-lambda(cxx_, vec(w), (act(w))(1i, cxx_))

### cips-ract

    cips-ract(w) := vnb-lambda([cxr_, cxx_], cartesian(rr, vec(w)), (act(w))(cxr_, cxx_))

### cips-real

    cips-real(w) := [normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), vec(w), vadd(w), vzero(w), vneg(w), cips-ract(w), cips-rip(w), cips-j(w)]

### cips-rip

    cips-rip(w) := vnb-lambda([cxx_, cxy_], cartesian(vec(w), vec(w)), real-part((ip(w))(cxx_, cxy_)))

### cips-rsip

    cips-rsip(w) := [normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), vec(w), vadd(w), vzero(w), vneg(w), cips-ract(w), cips-rip(w)]

### circle-deriv

    circle-deriv(wnc_, wnr_) := vnb-lambda(wnt_, ccint(0, 2 * pi), 1i * (wnr_ * cc-exp(1i * wnt_)))

### circle-path

    circle-path(wnc_, wnr_) := vnb-lambda(wnt_, ccint(0, 2 * pi), wnc_ + wnr_ * cc-exp(1i * wnt_))

### cl2-space

    cl2-space(omega, ca, mu) := complexify(l2-space(omega, ca, mu))

### clamp

    clamp(a, b, x) := min(max(x, a), b)

### class

    class(s, a) := {b in pts(s): related(s, a, b)}

### clopen-sets

    clopen-sets(s) := {cou_ in power(pts(s)): is-open(s, cou_) and is-closed(s, cou_)}

### closed-annulus

    closed-annulus(anla_, anlr_, anls_) := {anlz_ in cc: anlr_ <= magnitude(anlz_ - anla_) and magnitude(anlz_ - anla_) <= anls_}

### closed-ball

    closed-ball(s, c, r) := {y in pts(s): (dist(s))(c, y) <= r}

### closure

    closure(s, a) := {x in pts(s): forall([u], u in power(pts(s)) and is-open(s, u) and x in u implies forsome([y in u], y in a))}

### coint

    coint(a, b) := {osq_ in rr: a <= osq_ and osq_ < b}

### col-as-row

    col-as-row(gcr-a_, gcr-n_, gcr-j_) := matof(1, gcr-n_, vnb-lambda([gcri_, gcrk_], cartesian(interval(1, 1), interval(1, gcr-n_)), entry(gcr-a_, gcrk_, gcr-j_)))

### collapse-at

    collapse-at(k_) := vnb-lambda(z_, nn, if(k_ < z_, pred(z_), z_))

### companion

    companion(f, a, n) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([gci_, gcj_], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), if(gcj_ = n, (neg(f))(a(gci_)), if(gci_ = succ(gcj_), one(f), zero(f)))))

### completion

    completion(m) := [quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)), completion-dist(m)]

### completion-dist

    completion-dist(m) := vnb-lambda(p, cartesian(quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)), quotient(cauchy-setoid(m))), iota(dval, dval in rr and forsome([f, g], f in cseq(m) and g in cseq(m) and nth(1, p) = class(cauchy-setoid(m), f) and nth(2, p) = class(cauchy-setoid(m), g) and converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f, g), dval))))

### completion-measure

    completion-measure(omega, ca, mu) := res(method-i-outer(omega, ca, mu), completion-sigma(omega, ca, mu))

### completion-sigma

    completion-sigma(omega, ca, mu) := {cps_ in power(omega): forsome([cpa_ in ca, cpn_ in power(omega)], is-null-set(omega, ca, mu, cpn_) and cps_ = union(cpa_, cpn_))}

### complex-integral

    complex-integral(omega, ca, mu, u) := rintegral(omega, ca, mu, vnb-lambda(cix_, omega, real-part(u(cix_)))) + 1i * rintegral(omega, ca, mu, vnb-lambda(cix_, omega, imag-part(u(cix_))))

### complexify

    complexify(v) := [scal(v), cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), cx-vadd(v), [vzero(v), vzero(v)], cx-vneg(v), cx-act(v), cx-rip(v), cx-j(v)]

### compose

    compose(f, g) := vnb-lambda(z_, dom(g), f(g(z_)))

### conj-set

    conj-set(cjsa_) := {cjsz_ in cc: conjugate(cjsz_) in cjsa_}

### connected-component

    connected-component(ccs_, ccx_) := big-union(ccc_, {ccc_ in power(pts(ccs_)): is-connected-t(ccs_, ccc_) and ccx_ in ccc_}, ccc_)

### cont-fns

    cont-fns(s) := {cfh_ in fun(pts(s), rr): is-continuous(s, rr-ms, cfh_)}

### content-of

    content-of(ssd_) := nth(2, ssd_)

### conv-hull

    conv-hull(cvm_, cve_) := big-union(cvn_, nn, conv-level(cvm_, cve_, cvn_))

### conv-join

    conv-join(cjm_, cjc_, cja_) := {cjz_ in vec(cjm_): forsome([cjy_ in cjc_, cjt_ in rr], 0 <= cjt_ and cjt_ <= 1 and cjz_ = (vadd(cjm_))((act(cjm_))(1 - cjt_, cjy_), (act(cjm_))(cjt_, cja_)))}

### conv3

    conv3(cva_, cvb_, cvc_) := {cvz_ in cc: forsome([cvl_ in ccint(0, 1), cvm_ in ccint(0, 1), cvn_ in ccint(0, 1)], (cvl_ + cvm_) + cvn_ = 1 and cvz_ = (cvl_ * cva_ + cvm_ * cvb_) + cvn_ * cvc_)}

### coord-permute

    coord-permute(cpx_, cpg_) := vnb-lambda(cpw_, fun(nn, cpx_), vnb-lambda(cpk_, nn, (cpg_(cpk_))(cpw_(cpk_))))

### cover-sums

    cover-sums(omega, cls, pm, es) := {cvv_ in rr-pos-star: forsome([cvs_ in fun(nn, cls)], es subset big-union(cvn_, nn, cvs_(cvn_)) and cvv_ = esum(vnb-lambda(cvk_, nn, pm(cvs_(cvk_)))))}

### cps-radius

    cps-radius(cf) := esup({cpsr_ in rr: 0 <= cpsr_ and series-converges(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, magnitude(cf(cpsk_)) * cpsr_ ^ cpsk_))})

### crel

    crel(m) := {p in cartesian(cseq(m), cseq(m)): cseq-equiv(m, nth(1, p), nth(2, p))}

### crip

    crip(v) := vnb-lambda([cxx_, cxy_], cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), (rip(v))(cxx_, cxy_) - 1i * (rip(v))((jop(v))(cxx_), cxy_))

### cseq

    cseq(m) := {f in fun(nn, pts(m)): is-cauchy-seq(m, f)}

### csip-cips

    csip-cips(v) := [normed-field-as-commutative-ring(cc-normed-field), vec(v), vadd(v), vzero(v), vneg(v), cact(v), crip(v)]

### csip-qj

    csip-qj(v) := descend(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(v)), vnb-lambda(sqa_, vec(v), class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(v)), (jop(v))(sqa_))))

### csip-quotient

    csip-quotient(v) := [scal(v), quotient(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(v))), sip-qvadd(csip-as-sip(v)), class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(v)), vzero(v)), sip-qvneg(csip-as-sip(v)), sip-qact(csip-as-sip(v)), sip-qrip(csip-as-sip(v)), csip-qj(v)]

### ctx-add

    ctx-add(mcg_, mca_) := union(mcg_, pair(mca_, mca_))

### ctx-fv

    ctx-fv(mcg_) := big-union(mcw_, mcg_, expr-fv(mcw_))

### cx-act

    cx-act(v) := vnb-lambda([cr_, cp_], cartesian(carr(scal(v)), cartesian(vec(v), vec(v))), [(act(v))(cr_, nth(1, cp_)), (act(v))(cr_, nth(2, cp_))])

### cx-j

    cx-j(v) := vnb-lambda(cp_, cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), [(vneg(v))(nth(2, cp_)), nth(1, cp_)])

### cx-rip

    cx-rip(v) := vnb-lambda([cp_, cq_], cartesian(cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), cartesian(vec(v), vec(v))), (rip(v))(nth(1, cp_), nth(1, cq_)) + (rip(v))(nth(2, cp_), nth(2, cq_)))

### cx-sip

    cx-sip(v) := [scal(v), cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), cx-vadd(v), [vzero(v), vzero(v)], cx-vneg(v), cx-act(v), cx-rip(v)]

### cx-vadd

    cx-vadd(v) := vnb-lambda([cp_, cq_], cartesian(cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), cartesian(vec(v), vec(v))), [(vadd(v))(nth(1, cp_), nth(1, cq_)), (vadd(v))(nth(2, cp_), nth(2, cq_))])

### cx-vneg

    cx-vneg(v) := vnb-lambda(cp_, cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), [(vneg(v))(nth(1, cp_)), (vneg(v))(nth(2, cp_))])

### cyclic-subspace

    cyclic-subspace(gcs-md_, gcs-t_, gcs-v_) := {gcsz_ in vec(gcs-md_): forsome([gcsq_ in carr(poly(scal(gcs-md_)))], gcsz_ = poly-op(gcs-md_, gcs-t_, gcsq_, gcs-v_))}

### cyclic-sum

    cyclic-sum(l, a) := direct-sum(zz-ring, l, vnb-lambda(csi_, interval(1, l), zz-mod(a(csi_))))

### cyl

    cyl(cyw_, cyn_) := {cyz_ in fun(nn, nn): forall([cyj_ in nn], cyj_ < cyn_ implies cyz_(cyj_) = cyw_(cyj_))}

### dbi

    dbi(m, xs) := vnb-lambda(dbn_, nn, subspace-ms(rr-ms, ccint(-(vnrm(m))(xs(dbn_)), (vnrm(m))(xs(dbn_)))))

### dbj

    dbj(m, xs) := vnb-lambda(dbf_, dual-unit-ball(m), vnb-lambda(dbn_, nn, dbf_(xs(dbn_))))

### deg

    deg(a, p) := iota(n_, n_ in nn and deg-bound(a, p, n_) and forall([j_ in nn], deg-bound(a, p, j_) implies n_ <= j_))

### deriv

    deriv(f, a) := iota(l, is-diff-at(f, a, l))

### deriv-on

    deriv-on(k, u, f, a) := iota(l, is-diff-on(k, u, f, a, l))

### deriv-v

    deriv-v(m, f, a) := iota(l, is-diff-at-v(m, f, a, l))

### derivable

    derivable(mct_) := {mcq_ in seq-set: forall([mcs_ in power(seq-set)], core-closed(mct_, mcs_) implies mcq_ in mcs_)}

### descend

    descend(s, f) := vnb-lambda(c, quotient(s), iota(z, z in set and forsome([a in c], z = f(a))))

### descend2

    descend2(s, f) := vnb-lambda([c, d], cartesian(quotient(s), quotient(s)), iota(z, z in set and forsome([a in c, b in d], z = f(a, b))))

### diag-mat

    diag-mat(n, f) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([dga_, dgb_], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), (mul(zz-ring))(f(dga_), entry(identmat(zz-ring, n), dga_, dgb_))))

### diam

    diam(mhm_, mhs_) := esup({mhw_ in rr-pos-star: forsome([mhx_ in mhs_, mhy_ in mhs_], mhw_ = (dist(mhm_))(mhx_, mhy_))})

### difference

    difference(u_, b_) := complement-in(u_, b_)

### dir-deriv

    dir-deriv(m, f, a, eta) := deriv(seg-curve(m, f, a, eta), 0)

### dir-of

    dir-of(ssd_) := nth(1, ssd_)

### direct-sum

    direct-sum(s, n, f) := [s, dsum-vec(n, f), dsum-vadd(n, f), dsum-vzero(n, f), dsum-vneg(n, f), dsum-act(s, n, f)]

### directed

    directed(sst_, ssk_) := cartesian(pair(sent, received), msg-set(sst_, ssk_))

### discrete-ms

    discrete-ms(dsx_) := [dsx_, vnb-lambda([dsu_, dsv_], cartesian(dsx_, dsx_), if(dsu_ = dsv_, 0, 1))]

### discrete-space

    discrete-space(a_) := [a_, power(a_)]

### disjoint-sum-set

    disjoint-sum-set(ssx_) := {ssp_ in cartesian(nn, big-union(ssk_, nn, ssx_(ssk_))): nth(2, ssp_) in ssx_(nth(1, ssp_))}

### dist-seq

    dist-seq(m, f, g) := vnb-lambda(n, nn, (dist(m))(f(n), g(n)))

### dsum-act

    dsum-act(s, n, f) := vnb-lambda([dqr_, dsf_], cartesian(carr(s), dsum-vec(n, f)), vnb-lambda(dsi_, interval(1, n), (act(f(dsi_)))(dqr_, dsf_(dsi_))))

### dsum-vadd

    dsum-vadd(n, f) := vnb-lambda([dsf_, dsg_], cartesian(dsum-vec(n, f), dsum-vec(n, f)), vnb-lambda(dsi_, interval(1, n), (vadd(f(dsi_)))(dsf_(dsi_), dsg_(dsi_))))

### dsum-vec

    dsum-vec(n, f) := {dsv_ in fun(interval(1, n), big-union(dsk_, interval(1, n), vec(f(dsk_)))): forall([dsj_ in interval(1, n)], dsv_(dsj_) in vec(f(dsj_)))}

### dsum-vneg

    dsum-vneg(n, f) := vnb-lambda(dsf_, dsum-vec(n, f), vnb-lambda(dsi_, interval(1, n), (vneg(f(dsi_)))(dsf_(dsi_))))

### dsum-vzero

    dsum-vzero(n, f) := vnb-lambda(dsi_, interval(1, n), vzero(f(dsi_)))

### dual

    dual(m) := [normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), dual-vec(m), vnb-lambda([dlaf_, dlag_], cartesian(dual-vec(m), dual-vec(m)), vnb-lambda(dlax_, vec(m), dlaf_(dlax_) + dlag_(dlax_))), vnb-lambda(dlzx_, vec(m), 0), vnb-lambda(dlnf_, dual-vec(m), vnb-lambda(dlnx_, vec(m), -dlnf_(dlnx_))), vnb-lambda([dlsr_, dlsf_], cartesian(rr, dual-vec(m)), vnb-lambda(dlsx_, vec(m), dlsr_ * dlsf_(dlsx_))), vnb-lambda(dlvf_, dual-vec(m), dual-norm(m, dlvf_))]

### dual-ball-ms

    dual-ball-ms(m, xs) := pullback-ms(product-metric(dbi(m, xs)), dual-unit-ball(m), dbj(m, xs))

### dual-norm

    dual-norm(m, f) := iota(c_, c_ in rr and 0 <= c_ and forall([x_ in vec(m)], abs(f(x_)) <= c_ * (vnrm(m))(x_)) and forall([d_], d_ in rr and 0 <= d_ and forall([x_ in vec(m)], abs(f(x_)) <= d_ * (vnrm(m))(x_)) implies c_ <= d_))

### dual-norm-on

    dual-norm-on(m, s, f) := iota(c_, c_ in rr and 0 <= c_ and forall([x_ in s], abs(f(x_)) <= c_ * (vnrm(m))(x_)) and forall([d_], d_ in rr and 0 <= d_ and forall([x_ in s], abs(f(x_)) <= d_ * (vnrm(m))(x_)) implies c_ <= d_))

### dual-unit-ball

    dual-unit-ball(m) := {dbf_ in dual-vec(m): dual-norm(m, dbf_) <= 1}

### dual-vec

    dual-vec(m) := {f_ in fun(vec(m), rr): is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f_)}

### dual-weak-star-top

    dual-weak-star-top(m) := subspace-top(tproduct-top(vec(m), vnb-lambda(b6n_, vec(m), metric-top(rr-ms))), dual-vec(m))

### duality-map

    duality-map(chk_, chc_, cha_) := vnb-lambda(chx_, carr(chk_), chc_((mul(chk_))(cha_, chx_)))

### dynkin-generated

    dynkin-generated(omega, ce) := {dga_ in power(omega): forall([dgb_], dgb_ in power(power(omega)) and is-dynkin-system(omega, dgb_) and ce subset dgb_ implies dga_ in dgb_)}

### eapp

    eapp(mxh_, mxl_) := [3, mxh_, mxl_]

### ebind

    ebind(mxh_, mxn_, mxa_, mxb_) := [4, mxh_, mxn_, mxa_, mxb_]

### ebvar

    ebvar(mxk_) := [1, mxk_]

### econst

    econst(mxc_) := [2, mxc_]

### efvar

    efvar(mxn_) := [0, mxn_]

### einf

    einf(s) := esup(elower-bounds(s))

### elem-f

    elem-f(a, n, k, l) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), if(i = k and j = l or i = l and j = k or i = j and not(i = k) and not(i = l), one(a), zero(a))))

### elem-g

    elem-g(a, n, r, k, l) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), if(i = j, one(a), if(i = k and j = l, r, zero(a)))))

### elem-gen

    elem-gen(r, n) := intersection-of({egs_ in power(mat(n, n, carr(r))): identmat(r, n) in egs_ and forall([egx_ in egs_, ege_ in set], is-elementary(r, n, ege_) implies matmul(r, egx_, ege_) in egs_)})

### elem-h

    elem-h(a, n, r, k) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), if(i = j, if(i = k, r, one(a)), zero(a))))

### eliminf

    eliminf(f) := esup(image(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, einf(etail(f, n_))), nn))

### elimsup

    elimsup(f) := einf(image(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, esup(etail(f, n_))), nn))

### elower-bounds

    elower-bounds(s) := {b_ in rr-pos-star: forall([x_ in s], b_ <= x_)}

### embed

    embed(m) := vnb-lambda(u, pts(m), class(cauchy-setoid(m), embed-seq(m, u)))

### embed-seq

    embed-seq(m, u) := vnb-lambda(n, nn, u)

### enc-all

    enc-all(mcb_) := ebind(20, 1, [], mcb_)

### enc-and

    enc-and(mcp_, mcq_) := eapp(3, [mcp_, mcq_])

### enc-apply

    enc-apply(mcp_, mcq_) := eapp(18, [mcp_, mcq_])

### enc-bu

    enc-bu(mca_, mcb_) := ebind(24, 1, [mca_], mcb_)

### enc-cart

    enc-cart(mcl_) := eapp(13, mcl_)

### enc-comp

    enc-comp(mcb_) := ebind(23, 1, [], mcb_)

### enc-eq

    enc-eq(mcp_, mcq_) := eapp(8, [mcp_, mcq_])

### enc-ex

    enc-ex(mcb_) := ebind(21, 1, [], mcb_)

### enc-fun

    enc-fun(mcp_, mcq_) := eapp(19, [mcp_, mcq_])

### enc-fun1

    enc-fun1(mdp_) := eapp(19, [mdp_])

### enc-if

    enc-if(mcp_, mca_, mcb_) := ebind(27, 0, [mca_, mcb_], mcp_)

### enc-iff

    enc-iff(mcp_, mcq_) := eapp(6, [mcp_, mcq_])

### enc-imp

    enc-imp(mcp_, mcq_) := eapp(5, [mcp_, mcq_])

### enc-in

    enc-in(mcp_, mcq_) := eapp(7, [mcp_, mcq_])

### enc-inter

    enc-inter(mcl_) := eapp(12, mcl_)

### enc-iota

    enc-iota(mcb_) := ebind(25, 1, [], mcb_)

### enc-lam

    enc-lam(mca_, mcb_) := ebind(26, 1, [mca_], mcb_)

### enc-le

    enc-le(md10x_, md10y_) := eapp(10, [econst(11), md10x_, md10y_])

### enc-length

    enc-length(mcp_) := eapp(16, [mcp_])

### enc-limit

    enc-limit(mip_) := eapp(10, [econst(9), mip_])

### enc-list

    enc-list(mcl_) := eapp(17, mcl_)

### enc-lt

    enc-lt(md10x_, md10y_) := eapp(10, [econst(13), md10x_, md10y_])

### enc-not

    enc-not(mcp_) := eapp(2, [mcp_])

### enc-nth

    enc-nth(mcp_, mcq_) := eapp(15, [mcp_, mcq_])

### enc-num

    enc-num(mck_) := econst(2 * mck_)

### enc-or

    enc-or(mcp_, mcq_) := eapp(4, [mcp_, mcq_])

### enc-ordlt

    enc-ordlt(mip_, miq_) := eapp(10, [econst(7), mip_, miq_])

### enc-pred1

    enc-pred1(md10n_, md10p_) := eapp(10, [md10n_, md10p_])

### enc-qeq

    enc-qeq(mcp_, mcq_) := eapp(9, [mcp_, mcq_])

### enc-sep

    enc-sep(mca_, mcb_) := ebind(22, 1, [mca_], mcb_)

### enc-succ

    enc-succ(mip_) := eapp(30, [mip_])

### enc-succo

    enc-succo(mip_) := eapp(31, [mip_])

### enc-tuples

    enc-tuples(mcp_) := eapp(14, [mcp_])

### enc-union

    enc-union(mcl_) := eapp(11, mcl_)

### encr

    encr(smg_, smk_) := [2, smg_, smk_]

### entry

    entry(m, i, j) := nth(j, nth(i, m))

### enum-fam

    enum-fam(ag, f, phi, n) := vnb-lambda(i, nn, if(i in ord-segment(n), f(phi(i)), iden(ag)))

### ep-norm

    ep-norm(omega, ca, mu, epp_, epf_) := epow(integral(omega, ca, mu, ptwise-epow(omega, epf_, epp_)), recip(epp_))

### eplus-down

    eplus-down(edx_, edy_) := if(edx_ = neg-inf or edy_ = neg-inf, neg-inf, edx_ + edy_)

### eplus-up

    eplus-up(eux_, euy_) := if(eux_ = pos-inf or euy_ = pos-inf, pos-inf, eux_ + euy_)

### epow

    epow(ept_, epp_) := if(ept_ = pos-inf, pos-inf, rpow-star(ept_, epp_))

### eps-rel

    eps-rel(cuvs_, cuve_) := {cuvp_ in cartesian(pts(cuvs_), pts(cuvs_)): (dist(cuvs_))(nth(1, cuvp_), nth(2, cuvp_)) < cuve_}

### eps-setoid

    eps-setoid(cuvs_, cuve_) := [pts(cuvs_), eps-rel(cuvs_, cuve_)]

### eqv

    eqv(mrt_) := {mrx_ in rw-univ: eqv-ok(mrt_, mrx_)}

### esum

    esum(f_) := esup({v_ in rr-pos-star: forsome([t_], (t_ in set and card(t_) in nn and t_ subset dom(f_)) and v_ = finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, f_, t_))})

### esup

    esup(s_) := iota(bb_, bb_ in rr-pos-star and forall([x in s_], x <= bb_) and forall([b], b in rr-pos-star and forall([x in s_], x <= b) implies bb_ <= b))

### etail

    etail(f, n) := image(f, {p_ in nn: n <= p_})

### euclidean-gauges

    euclidean-gauges(s) := {dg in fun(carr(s), nn): has-div-remainder(s, dg)}

### euler-phi

    euler-phi(n) := card({ephk_ in interval(1, n): zz-coprime(ephk_, n)})

### ev-op

    ev-op(gev-md_, gev-t_) := vnb-lambda(gevp_, carr(poly(scal(gev-md_))), op-of(gev-md_, gev-t_, gevp_))

### eval-at-points

    eval-at-points(a, th, m) := vnb-lambda(gep_, poly-le(a, m), vnb-lambda(gei_, interval(1, succ(m)), poly-eval(a, gep_, th(gei_))))

### expr-fv

    expr-fv(mxe_) := (fv-stage(expr-rank(mxe_)))(mxe_)

### expr-lcd

    expr-lcd(mxe_) := (lcd-stage(expr-rank(mxe_)))(mxe_)

### expr-open

    expr-open(mxe_, mxk_, mxu_) := (open-stage(mxu_, expr-rank(mxe_)))(mxe_, mxk_)

### expr-rank

    expr-rank(mxe_) := iota(mxr_, mxr_ in nn and mxe_ in expr-stage(mxr_) and forall([mxj_ in nn], mxe_ in expr-stage(mxj_) implies mxr_ <= mxj_))

### expr-subst

    expr-subst(mxe_, mxm_, mxu_) := (subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, expr-rank(mxe_)))(mxe_)

### expr-wks

    expr-wks(mce_) := (wks-stage(expr-rank(mce_)))(mce_)

### ext-add

    ext-add(eax_, eay_) := if(eax_ = pos-inf or eay_ = pos-inf, pos-inf, if(eax_ = neg-inf or eay_ = neg-inf, neg-inf, eax_ + eay_))

### ext-neg

    ext-neg(enx_) := if(enx_ = pos-inf, neg-inf, if(enx_ = neg-inf, pos-inf, -enx_))

### ext-partial-sum

    ext-partial-sum(x, n) := if(forsome([epk_ in nn], epk_ < n and x(epk_) = pos-inf), pos-inf, if(forsome([epk_ in nn], epk_ < n and x(epk_) = neg-inf), neg-inf, series-partial-sum(x, n)))

### ext-sub

    ext-sub(esx_, esy_) := ext-add(esx_, ext-neg(esy_))

### extend-const

    extend-const(f, a, b) := vnb-lambda(ecx_, rr, f(clamp(a, b, ecx_)))

### fact-sums

    fact-sums(fsm_, fsg_, fsx_) := {fsr_ in rr: forsome([fsn_ in nn, fsu_ in fun(nn, carr(fsm_))], mprod(fsm_, fsu_, fsn_) = fsx_ and fsr_ = series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(fsi_, nn, fsg_(fsu_(fsi_))), fsn_))}

### fin-enum

    fin-enum(s) := choice(bijection(ord-segment(card(s)), s))

### fin-subset-monoid

    fin-subset-monoid(a_) := [fin-subsets(a_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(fin-subsets(a_), fin-subsets(a_)), union(x_, y_)), empty-set]

### fin-subsets

    fin-subsets(a_) := {t_ in power(a_): card(t_) in nn}

### finite-unions

    finite-unions(omega, ci) := {mfu_ in power(omega): forsome([mff_ in set], card(mff_) in nn and mff_ subset ci and mfu_ = big-union(mfw_, mff_, mfw_))}

### finprod

    finprod(m, f, s) := finsum(m, f, s)

### finsum

    finsum(ag, f, s) := sum-ag(ag, enum-fam(ag, f, fin-enum(s), card(s)), card(s))

### finsupp

    finsupp(a, m) := {f in fun(carr(m), carr(a)): card(supp(a, m, f)) in nn}

### fker

    fker(m, f) := {fkz_ in vec(m): f(fkz_) = 0}

### fn-x-section

    fn-x-section(ys, f, x) := vnb-lambda(fsy_, ys, f([x, fsy_]))

### fn-y-section

    fn-y-section(xs, f, y) := vnb-lambda(fsx_, xs, f([fsx_, y]))

### fp

    fp(qsp_) := ringoid-quotient-ring(fp-ringoid(qsp_))

### fp-ringoid

    fp-ringoid(qsp_) := [zz, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, zz, -x_), 0, 1, {fpn_ in zz: zz-divides(qsp_, fpn_)}]

### fr-ball

    fr-ball(m, fam, n, eps) := {x in vec(m): forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies (fam(k))(x) < eps)}

### fr-c-space

    fr-c-space(m, fam) := [vec(m), vnb-lambda(frk_, nn, vnb-lambda([fru_, frv_], cartesian(vec(m), vec(m)), (fam(frk_))((vadd(m))(fru_, (vneg(m))(frv_)))))]

### fr-metric

    fr-metric(m, fam) := dist(fr-metric-space(m, fam))

### fr-metric-space

    fr-metric-space(m, fam) := c-metric(fr-c-space(m, fam))

### gamma-ext

    gamma-ext(gez_) := gamma-function(gez_ + choice({gek_ in nn: 0 < real-part(gez_) + gek_})) * recip(poch(gez_, choice({gek_ in nn: 0 < real-part(gez_) + gek_})))

### gamma-function

    gamma-function(gmz_) := improper-int(gamma-integrand(gmz_))

### gamma-integrand

    gamma-integrand(gmz_) := vnb-lambda(gmw_, {gmu_ in cc: 0 < real-part(gmu_)}, cc-exp((gmz_ - 1) * cc-log(gmw_)) * cc-exp(-gmw_))

### gauge

    gauge(s) := choice(euclidean-gauges(s))

### generated-subgroup

    generated-subgroup(gcg_, gcx_) := {gcy_ in carr(gcg_): forall([gcs_ in power(carr(gcg_))], is-subgroup(gcg_, gcs_) implies gcx_ in gcs_ implies gcy_ in gcs_)}

### graph-ms

    graph-ms(s, a, g) := [a, vnb-lambda([gmu_, gmv_], cartesian(a, a), (dist(s))(gmu_, gmv_) + abs(g(gmu_) - g(gmv_)))]

### graph-of

    graph-of(gfx_, gfy_, gff_) := {grp_ in cartesian(gfx_, gfy_): gff_(nth(1, grp_)) = nth(2, grp_)}

### greedy-chain

    greedy-chain(phi, grd, porel) := zkept(phi, grd, porel, card(grd))

### group-prod

    group-prod(x, y) := [cartesian(carr(x), carr(y)), vnb-lambda([gpp_, gpq_], cartesian(cartesian(carr(x), carr(y)), cartesian(carr(x), carr(y))), [(opr(x))(nth(1, gpp_), nth(1, gpq_)), (opr(y))(nth(2, gpp_), nth(2, gpq_))]), [iden(x), iden(y)], vnb-lambda(gpp_, cartesian(carr(x), carr(y)), [(inv(x))(nth(1, gpp_)), (inv(y))(nth(2, gpp_))])]

### group-zpow

    group-zpow(g, x, k) := if(0 <= k, gpow(g, x, k), (inv(g))(gpow(g, x, 0 - k)))

### grp-monoid

    grp-monoid(gmg_) := [carr(gmg_), opr(gmg_), iden(gmg_)]

### hausdorff

    hausdorff(mhm_, mhl_) := method-ii(mhm_, power(pts(mhm_)), hausdorff-pm(mhm_, mhl_))

### hausdorff-phi

    hausdorff-phi(mhm_, mhf_) := method-ii(mhm_, power(pts(mhm_)), phi-pm(mhm_, mhf_))

### hausdorff-pm

    hausdorff-pm(mhm_, mhl_) := vnb-lambda(mhh_, power(pts(mhm_)), epow(diam(mhm_, mhh_), mhl_))

### hd-argk

    hd-argk(mch_) := if(mch_ <= 6, 2, 1)

### hd-body
