    hd-body(mch_) := if(mch_ = 24 or mch_ = 26, 1, 2)

### hd-bres

    hd-bres(mch_) := if(mch_ <= 21, 2, 1)

### hd-res

    hd-res(mch_) := if(mch_ <= 10, 2, 1)

### hom-abelian-group

    hom-abelian-group(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-abelian-group(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-bounded-linear

    hom-bounded-linear(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(vec(a), vec(b)): is-bounded-linear-arrow(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-bundle

    hom-bundle(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(bnodes(a), bnodes(b)): is-hom-bundle(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-c-continuous

    hom-c-continuous(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(pts(a), pts(b)): is-c-continuous-arrow(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-c-metric-space

    hom-c-metric-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(pts(a), pts(b)): is-hom-c-metric-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-comm-monoid

    hom-comm-monoid(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-comm-monoid(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-commutative-ring

    hom-commutative-ring(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-commutative-ring(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-complex-inner-product-space

    hom-complex-inner-product-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(vec(a), vec(b)): is-hom-complex-inner-product-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-complex-pre-hilbert-space

    hom-complex-pre-hilbert-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(vec(a), vec(b)): is-hom-complex-pre-hilbert-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-complex-semi-inner-product-space

    hom-complex-semi-inner-product-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(vec(a), vec(b)): is-hom-complex-semi-inner-product-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-continuous

    hom-continuous(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(pts(a), pts(b)): is-continuous-arrow(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-directed-set

    hom-directed-set(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(pts(a), pts(b)): is-hom-directed-set(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-euclidean-ring

    hom-euclidean-ring(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-euclidean-ring(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-field

    hom-field(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-field(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-field-ring

    hom-field-ring(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-field-ring(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-group

    hom-group(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-group(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-infiltrated-space

    hom-infiltrated-space(a, b) := {homp_ in cartesian(fun(adv-ids(a), adv-ids(b)), fun(adv-tokens(a), adv-tokens(b))): is-hom-infiltrated-space(a, b, nth(1, homp_), nth(2, homp_))}

### hom-integral-domain

    hom-integral-domain(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-integral-domain(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-lipschitz

    hom-lipschitz(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(pts(a), pts(b)): is-lipschitz-arrow(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-measurable-space

    hom-measurable-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(pts(a), pts(b)): is-hom-measurable-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-measure-space

    hom-measure-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(pts(a), pts(b)): is-hom-measure-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-metric-group

    hom-metric-group(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-metric-group(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-metric-space

    hom-metric-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(pts(a), pts(b)): is-hom-metric-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-metrizable-top-space

    hom-metrizable-top-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(pts(a), pts(b)): is-hom-metrizable-top-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-module

    hom-module(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(vec(a), vec(b)): is-hom-module(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-monoid

    hom-monoid(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-monoid(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-normed-ag

    hom-normed-ag(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-normed-ag(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-normed-field

    hom-normed-field(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-normed-field(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-normed-vector-space

    hom-normed-vector-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(vec(a), vec(b)): is-hom-normed-vector-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-pid

    hom-pid(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-pid(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-pk-space

    hom-pk-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(names(a), names(b)): is-hom-pk-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-pre-hilbert-space

    hom-pre-hilbert-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(vec(a), vec(b)): is-hom-pre-hilbert-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-pseudometric-space

    hom-pseudometric-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(pts(a), pts(b)): is-hom-pseudometric-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-ring

    hom-ring(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-ring(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-ringoid

    hom-ringoid(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-ringoid(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-semi-inner-product-space

    hom-semi-inner-product-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(vec(a), vec(b)): is-hom-semi-inner-product-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-semigroup

    hom-semigroup(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-semigroup(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-setoid

    hom-setoid(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(pts(a), pts(b)): is-hom-setoid(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-sk-space

    hom-sk-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(sk-names(a), sk-names(b)): is-hom-sk-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-top-group

    hom-top-group(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(carr(a), carr(b)): is-hom-top-group(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-top-space

    hom-top-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(pts(a), pts(b)): is-hom-top-space(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-uniformly-continuous

    hom-uniformly-continuous(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(pts(a), pts(b)): is-uniformly-continuous-arrow(a, b, homf_)}

### hom-vector-space

    hom-vector-space(a, b) := {homf_ in fun(vec(a), vec(b)): is-hom-vector-space(a, b, homf_)}

### homog-minorant

    homog-minorant(m, p) := vnb-lambda(hmx_, vec(m), inf(homog-set(m, p, hmx_)))

### homog-point

    homog-point(u, v) := if(v = 0, cc-inf, u * recip(v))

### homog-set

    homog-set(m, p, x) := {hmr_ in rr: forsome([hmt_ in rr], 0 < hmt_ and p((act(m))(hmt_, x)) <= hmt_ * hmr_)}

### hopf-map

    hopf-map(w) := [2 * (nth(1, w) * conjugate(nth(2, w))), magnitude(nth(1, w)) * magnitude(nth(1, w)) - magnitude(nth(2, w)) * magnitude(nth(2, w))]

### id-fun

    id-fun(a_) := vnb-lambda(idfx_, a_, idfx_)

### id-of

    id-of(ssn_) := nth(2, ssn_)

### identmat

    identmat(a, n) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), if(i = j, one(a), zero(a))))

### idx-list

    idx-list(min_) := iota(mir_, mir_ in tuples(set) and length(mir_) = min_ and forall([mii_ in nn], 1 <= mii_ implies mii_ <= min_ implies nth(mii_, mir_) = ebvar(min_ - mii_)))

### improper-int

    improper-int(ipf_) := iota(ipv_, improper-int-converges-to(ipf_, ipv_))

### index-of

    index-of(ssn_) := nth(1, ssn_)

### indicator

    indicator(omega, a) := vnb-lambda(x_, omega, if(x_ in a, 1, 0))

### inf

    inf(infs_) := -sup({infv_ in rr: forsome([infx_ in infs_], infv_ = -infx_)})

### inf-subsets

    inf-subsets(a) := {s in power(a): not(card(s) in nn)}

### insert-last

    insert-last(phi, x, n) := vnb-lambda(i, nn, if(i in ord-segment(n), phi(i), x))

### inst-reach

    inst-reach(md12d_, md12l_) := {md12z_ in expr-set: forall([md12w_ in power(expr-set)], md12d_ in md12w_ implies inst-closed(md12l_, md12w_) implies md12z_ in md12w_)}

### integral

    integral(omega, ca, mu, f) := esup({iny_ in rr-pos-star: forsome([ins_ in fun(omega, rr-pos-star)], is-simple-fn(omega, ca, ins_) and ptwise-le(omega, ins_, f) and iny_ = simple-integral(omega, mu, ins_))})

### interior

    interior(s, a) := {x in pts(s): forsome([u in power(pts(s))], is-open(s, u) and x in u and u subset a)}

### intersection-of

    intersection-of(c) := {iov_ in big-union(iow_, c, iow_): forall([iou_ in c], iov_ in iou_)}

### interval

    interval(a, b) := {i in nn: a <= i and i <= b}

### interval-length

    interval-length(s) := esup({odv_ in rr-pos-star: odv_ = 0 or forsome([odx_ in s, ody_ in s], odx_ <= ody_ and odv_ = ody_ - odx_)})

### inverse-bij

    inverse-bij(phi, x, y) := vnb-lambda(y_, y, choice({x_ in x: phi(x_) = y_}))

### ip-metric-space

    ip-metric-space(v) := nag-metric-space(ip-normed-ag(v))

### ip-norm

    ip-norm(v, x) := sqrt((ip(v))(x, x))

### ip-normed-ag

    ip-normed-ag(v) := [vec(v), vadd(v), vzero(v), vneg(v), vnb-lambda(x_, vec(v), ip-norm(v, x_))]

### isometry-group

    isometry-group(igs_) := {igf_ in bijection(pts(igs_), pts(igs_)): is-isometry(igs_, igs_, igf_)}

### isometry-group-grp

    isometry-group-grp(igs_) := [isometry-group(igs_), vnb-lambda([igu_, igv_], cartesian(isometry-group(igs_), isometry-group(igs_)), vnb-lambda(igx_, pts(igs_), igu_(igv_(igx_)))), vnb-lambda(igx_, pts(igs_), igx_), vnb-lambda(igi_, isometry-group(igs_), inverse-bij(igi_, pts(igs_), pts(igs_)))]

### isometry-group-ms

    isometry-group-ms(ims_) := [isometry-group(ims_), vnb-lambda([imu_, imv_], cartesian(isometry-group(ims_), isometry-group(ims_)), sup-dist(ims_, ims_, imu_, imv_))]

### j-cart

    j-cart(mit_) := {mix_ in rw-univ: forsome([mig_, miz_, mia_], miz_ in set and mia_ in set and mix_ = [2, mig_, enc-in(miz_, enc-cart(mia_)), enc-cch(miz_, mia_, length(mia_), length(mia_))] and mig_ in ctx-set and 1 <= length(mia_))}

### j-fb

    j-fb(mrt_) := {mrx_ in rw-univ: forsome([mrg_, mrh_, mrl_, mrb_], mrg_ in set and mrh_ in set and mrl_ in set and mrb_ in set and mrx_ = [1, mrg_, eapp(mrh_, mrl_), openl(mrb_, mrl_, length(mrl_))] and fun-def(mrh_, length(mrl_), mrb_) and eapp(mrh_, mrl_) in clt and openl(mrb_, mrl_, length(mrl_)) in clt)}

### j-idec

    j-idec(md14t_) := {md14y_ in rw-univ: forsome([md14g_, md14x2_, md14l2_, md14f2_], md14g_ in set and md14x2_ in set and md14l2_ in set and md14y_ = [2, md14g_, enc-in(md14x2_, enc-inter(md14l2_)), md14f2_] and [0, md14f2_] in rf-set(3, md14x2_, md14l2_) and enc-in(md14x2_, enc-inter(md14l2_)) in clf and md14f2_ in clf)}

### j-lb

    j-lb(mrt_) := {mrx_ in rw-univ: forsome([mrg_, mra_, mrb_, mre_], mrg_ in set and mra_ in set and mrb_ in set and mre_ in set and mrx_ = [1, mrg_, enc-apply(enc-lam(mra_, mrb_), mre_), expr-open(mrb_, 0, mre_)] and enc-apply(enc-lam(mra_, mrb_), mre_) in clt and expr-open(mrb_, 0, mre_) in clt and [mrg_, enc-in(mre_, mra_)] in derivable(mrt_))}

### j-len

    j-len(mrt_) := {mrx_ in rw-univ: forsome([mrg_, mrl_], mrg_ in set and mrl_ in set and mrx_ = [1, mrg_, enc-length(enc-list(mrl_)), enc-num(length(mrl_))] and enc-length(enc-list(mrl_)) in clt and enc-num(length(mrl_)) in clt and [mrg_, enc-eq(enc-list(mrl_), enc-list(mrl_))] in derivable(mrt_))}

### j-mac

    j-mac(mrt_) := {mrx_ in rw-univ: forsome([mrg_, mrc_, mra_, mrb_, mre_], mrg_ in set and mrc_ in set and mra_ in set and mrb_ in set and mre_ in clt and [mrg_, expr-open(mrc_, 0, mre_)] in derivable(mrt_) and [mrg_, enc-eq(mre_, mre_)] in derivable(mrt_) and (mrx_ = [1, mrg_, expr-open(mra_, 0, mre_), expr-open(mrb_, 0, mre_)] and enc-all(enc-imp(mrc_, enc-qeq(mra_, mrb_))) in mrt_ and enc-all(enc-imp(mrc_, enc-qeq(mra_, mrb_))) in clf and expr-open(mra_, 0, mre_) in clt and expr-open(mrb_, 0, mre_) in clt or mrx_ = [2, mrg_, expr-open(mra_, 0, mre_), expr-open(mrb_, 0, mre_)] and enc-all(enc-imp(mrc_, enc-iff(mra_, mrb_))) in mrt_ and enc-all(enc-imp(mrc_, enc-iff(mra_, mrb_))) in clf and expr-open(mra_, 0, mre_) in clf and expr-open(mrb_, 0, mre_) in clf))}

### j-macn

    j-macn(md13t_) := {md13x_ in rw-univ: forsome([md13g_, md13m_, md13l_, md13i_, md13r_, md13a_, md13b_], md13g_ in set and md13l_ in set and md13r_ in set and md13a_ in set and md13b_ in set and md13m_ in md13t_ and md13m_ in clf and md13i_ in inst-reach(md13m_, md13l_) and forall([md13j_ in nn], 2 <= md13j_ implies md13j_ <= length(md13l_) implies [md13g_, enc-eq(nth(md13j_, md13l_), nth(md13j_, md13l_))] in derivable(md13t_)) and (md13i_ = md13r_ or forsome([md13c_ in set], md13i_ = enc-imp(md13c_, md13r_) and [md13g_, md13c_] in derivable(md13t_))) and (md13r_ = enc-qeq(md13a_, md13b_) and md13x_ = [1, md13g_, md13a_, md13b_] and md13a_ in clt and md13b_ in clt or md13r_ = enc-iff(md13a_, md13b_) and md13x_ = [2, md13g_, md13a_, md13b_] and md13a_ in clf and md13b_ in clf))}

### j-nth

    j-nth(mrt_) := {mrx_ in rw-univ: forsome([mrg_, mrl_, mrk_], mrg_ in set and mrl_ in set and mrx_ = [1, mrg_, enc-nth(enc-num(mrk_), enc-list(mrl_)), nth(mrk_, mrl_)] and mrk_ in nn and 1 <= mrk_ and mrk_ <= length(mrl_) and enc-nth(enc-num(mrk_), enc-list(mrl_)) in clt and nth(mrk_, mrl_) in clt and [mrg_, enc-eq(enc-list(mrl_), enc-list(mrl_))] in derivable(mrt_))}

### j-teq

    j-teq(mit_) := {mix_ in rw-univ: forsome([mig_, mia_, mib_], mia_ in set and mib_ in set and mix_ = [2, mig_, enc-eq(enc-list(mia_), enc-list(mib_)), enc-tcj(mia_, mib_, length(mia_), length(mia_))] and mig_ in ctx-set and length(mia_) = length(mib_))}

### j-udec

    j-udec(md14t_) := {md14y_ in rw-univ: forsome([md14g_, md14x2_, md14l2_, md14f2_], md14g_ in set and md14x2_ in set and md14l2_ in set and md14y_ = [2, md14g_, enc-in(md14x2_, enc-union(md14l2_)), md14f2_] and [0, md14f2_] in rf-set(4, md14x2_, md14l2_) and enc-in(md14x2_, enc-union(md14l2_)) in clf and md14f2_ in clf)}

### juxta

    juxta(jxp_, jxq_, jxa_, jxb_, jxc_) := vnb-lambda(jxt_, ccint(jxa_, jxc_), if(jxt_ <= jxb_, jxp_(jxt_), jxq_(jxt_)))

### keep-set

    keep-set(phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha) := {y in grd: y = phi(alpha) and forall([z in kset], [z, y] in porel)}

### key-ideal-gen

    key-ideal-gen(sbgt_, sbgk_, sbgks_, sbgs_) := {sbgm_ in msg-set(sbgt_, sbgk_): forall([sbgq_ in power(msg-set(sbgt_, sbgk_))], is-key-ideal(sbgt_, sbgk_, sbgks_, sbgq_) implies forall([sbgz_ in sbgs_], sbgz_ in sbgq_) implies sbgm_ in sbgq_)}

### krylov

    krylov(gkr-md_, gkr-t_, gkr-v_, gkr-n_) := matof(gkr-n_, 1, vnb-lambda([gkri_, gkrj_], cartesian(interval(1, gkr-n_), interval(1, 1)), op-iter(gkr-md_, gkr-t_, gkr-v_, gkri_ - 1)))

### l1-pairing

    l1-pairing(omega, ca, mu, f) := vnb-lambda(lpg_, l1-set(omega, ca, mu), rintegral(omega, ca, mu, vnb-lambda(sx_, omega, f(sx_) * lpg_(sx_))))

### l1-set

    l1-set(omega, ca, mu) := {l1g_ in fun(omega, rr): is-integrable(omega, ca, mu, l1g_)}

### l2-act

    l2-act(omega, ca, mu) := vnb-lambda([lr_, lf_], cartesian(rr, l2-set(omega, ca, mu)), vnb-lambda(lx_, omega, lr_ * lf_(lx_)))

### l2-rip

    l2-rip(omega, ca, mu) := vnb-lambda([lf_, lg_], cartesian(l2-set(omega, ca, mu), l2-set(omega, ca, mu)), rintegral(omega, ca, mu, vnb-lambda(lx_, omega, lf_(lx_) * lg_(lx_))))

### l2-set

    l2-set(omega, ca, mu) := {lf_ in fun(omega, rr): is-real-measurable-fn(omega, ca, lf_) and is-integrable(omega, ca, mu, vnb-lambda(lx_, omega, lf_(lx_) * lf_(lx_)))}

### l2-space

    l2-space(omega, ca, mu) := [normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), l2-set(omega, ca, mu), l2-vadd(omega, ca, mu), l2-vzero(omega), l2-vneg(omega, ca, mu), l2-act(omega, ca, mu), l2-rip(omega, ca, mu)]

### l2-vadd

    l2-vadd(omega, ca, mu) := vnb-lambda([lf_, lg_], cartesian(l2-set(omega, ca, mu), l2-set(omega, ca, mu)), vnb-lambda(lx_, omega, lf_(lx_) + lg_(lx_)))

### l2-vneg

    l2-vneg(omega, ca, mu) := vnb-lambda(lf_, l2-set(omega, ca, mu), vnb-lambda(lx_, omega, -lf_(lx_)))

### l2-vzero

    l2-vzero(omega) := vnb-lambda(lx_, omega, 0)

### lastcoeff-set

    lastcoeff-set(md, p, u, sm) := {r_ in carr(scal(md)): forsome([c_ in mat(1, succ(p), carr(scal(md)))], entry(c_, 1, succ(p)) = r_ and lincomb(md, succ(p), c_, u) in sm)}

### laurent-coefs

    laurent-coefs(lcf_, lcc_, lcr_) := vnb-lambda(lck_, zz, if(0 <= lck_, recip((2 * pi) * 1i) * line-int(vnb-lambda(lcw_, trace(circle-path(lcc_, lcr_), 0, 2 * pi), lcf_(lcw_) * recip(lcw_ - lcc_) ^ succ(lck_)), circle-path(lcc_, lcr_), circle-deriv(lcc_, lcr_), 0, 2 * pi), recip((2 * pi) * 1i) * line-int(vnb-lambda(lcw_, trace(circle-path(lcc_, lcr_), 0, 2 * pi), lcf_(lcw_) * (lcw_ - lcc_) ^ (-(lck_ + 1))), circle-path(lcc_, lcr_), circle-deriv(lcc_, lcr_), 0, 2 * pi)))

### laurent-sum

    laurent-sum(lsc_, lsa_, lsz_) := cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(lsk_, nn, lsc_(lsk_) * (lsz_ - lsa_) ^ lsk_)) + recip(lsz_ - lsa_) * cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(lsk_, nn, lsc_(-(lsk_ + 1)) * recip(lsz_ - lsa_) ^ lsk_))

### ldir-deriv

    ldir-deriv(ldm_, ldf_, lda_, lde_) := iota(ldl_, has-dir-deriv(ldm_, ldf_, lda_, lde_, ldl_))

### leadcoef

    leadcoef(a, p) := p(deg(a, p))

### left-coset

    left-coset(gcg_, gca_, gch_) := {gcx_ in carr(gcg_): forsome([gcy_ in gch_], gcx_ = (opr(gcg_))(gca_, gcy_))}

### left-cosets

    left-cosets(gcg_, gch_) := {gcc_ in power(carr(gcg_)): forsome([gca_ in carr(gcg_)], gcc_ = left-coset(gcg_, gca_, gch_))}

### lft

    lft(a, b, c, d) := vnb-lambda(lfz_, cc-hat, if(lfz_ = cc-inf, if(c = 0, cc-inf, a * recip(c)), if(c * lfz_ + d = 0, cc-inf, (a * lfz_ + b) * recip(c * lfz_ + d))))

### lin-span

    lin-span(gls-md_, gls-n_, gls-u_) := {glsx_ in vec(gls-md_): forsome([glsc_ in mat(1, gls-n_, carr(scal(gls-md_)))], glsx_ = lincomb(gls-md_, gls-n_, glsc_, gls-u_))}

### lincomb

    lincomb(md, n, c, u) := finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (act(md))(entry(c, 1, j), entry(u, j, 1))), interval(1, n))

### line

    line(m, v) := {y_ in vec(m): forsome([r_ in rr], y_ = (act(m))(r_, v))}

### line-coef

    line-coef(m, v, u) := iota(lnt_, lnt_ in rr and u = (act(m))(lnt_, v))

### line-int

    line-int(pf, pgam, dgam, a, b) := cc-int(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), pf(pgam(pat_)) * dgam(pat_)), a, b)

### linf-norm

    linf-norm(omega, ca, mu, f) := einf({lim_ in rr-pos-star: mu({lix_ in omega: lim_ < abs(f(lix_))}) = 0})

### log

    log(x_) := c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), 1, x_)

### lp-norm

    lp-norm(omega, ca, mu, lpp_, lpf_) := ep-norm(omega, ca, mu, lpp_, abs-fn(omega, lpf_))

### lst

    lst(sml_) := [1, sml_]

### make-metric-group

    make-metric-group(mmm_, mmg_) := [carr(mmg_), opr(mmg_), iden(mmg_), inv(mmg_), dist(mmm_)]

### make-top-group

    make-top-group(mks_, mkg_) := [carr(mkg_), opr(mkg_), iden(mkg_), inv(mkg_), opens(mks_)]

### map-seq

    map-seq(h, n, u) := matof(n, 1, vnb-lambda([msa_, msb_], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, 1)), h(entry(u, msa_, msb_))))

### mapl

    mapl(mxf_, mxl_) := iota(mxr_, mxr_ in tuples(set) and length(mxr_) = length(mxl_) and forall([mxi_ in nn], 1 <= mxi_ implies mxi_ <= length(mxl_) implies nth(mxi_, mxr_) = mxf_(nth(mxi_, mxl_))))

### maplk

    maplk(mxf_, mxl_, mxk_) := iota(mxr_, mxr_ in tuples(set) and length(mxr_) = length(mxl_) and forall([mxi_ in nn], 1 <= mxi_ implies mxi_ <= length(mxl_) implies nth(mxi_, mxr_) = mxf_(nth(mxi_, mxl_), mxk_)))

### mat

    mat(m, n, x) := {p in matrix(x): n in nn and (length(p) = 0 implies m = 0) and (not(length(p) = 0) implies size(p) = [m, n])}

### mat-act

    mat-act(gma-md_, gma-n_, gma-p_, gma-u_) := matof(gma-n_, 1, vnb-lambda([gmai_, gmaj_], cartesian(interval(1, gma-n_), interval(1, 1)), finsum(module-vector-ag(gma-md_), vnb-lambda(gmak_, interval(1, gma-n_), (entry(gma-p_, gmai_, gmak_))(entry(gma-u_, gmak_, 1))), interval(1, gma-n_))))

### mat-lft

    mat-lft(s) := lft(entry(s, 1, 1), entry(s, 1, 2), entry(s, 2, 1), entry(s, 2, 2))

### mat-map

    mat-map(gmm-h_, gmm-m_, gmm-n_, gmm-a_) := matof(gmm-m_, gmm-n_, vnb-lambda([gmmi_, gmmj_], cartesian(interval(1, gmm-m_), interval(1, gmm-n_)), gmm-h_(entry(gmm-a_, gmmi_, gmmj_))))

### mat-ring

    mat-ring(a, n) := [mat(n, n, carr(a)), vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian(mat(n, n, carr(a)), mat(n, n, carr(a))), matadd(a, p, q)), vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian(mat(n, n, carr(a)), mat(n, n, carr(a))), matmul(a, p, q)), vnb-lambda(p, mat(n, n, carr(a)), matneg(a, p)), zeromat(a, n, n), identmat(a, n)]

### matact

    matact(md, p, u) := matof(nth(1, size(p)), nth(2, size(u)), vnb-lambda([i, c], cartesian(interval(1, nth(1, size(p))), interval(1, nth(2, size(u)))), finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, nth(2, size(p))), (act(md))(entry(p, i, j), entry(u, j, c))), interval(1, nth(2, size(p))))))

### matadd

    matadd(a, p, q) := matof(nth(1, size(p)), nth(2, size(p)), vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, nth(1, size(p))), interval(1, nth(2, size(p)))), (add(a))(entry(p, i, j), entry(q, i, j))))

### matmul

    matmul(a, p, q) := matof(nth(1, size(p)), nth(2, size(q)), vnb-lambda([i, k], cartesian(interval(1, nth(1, size(p))), interval(1, nth(2, size(q)))), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, nth(2, size(p))), (mul(a))(entry(p, i, j), entry(q, j, k))), interval(1, nth(2, size(p))))))

### matneg

    matneg(a, p) := matof(nth(1, size(p)), nth(2, size(p)), vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, nth(1, size(p))), interval(1, nth(2, size(p)))), (neg(a))(entry(p, i, j))))

### matof

    matof(m, n, g) := iota(p, p in mat(m, n, image(g, cartesian(interval(1, m), interval(1, n)))) and forall([i in interval(1, m), j in interval(1, n)], entry(p, i, j) = g(i, j)))

### matscale

    matscale(a, r, p) := matof(nth(1, size(p)), nth(2, size(p)), vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, nth(1, size(p))), interval(1, nth(2, size(p)))), (mul(a))(r, entry(p, i, j))))

### matunit

    matunit(a, n, k, l) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), if(i = k and j = l, one(a), zero(a))))

### method-i-outer

    method-i-outer(omega, cls, pm) := vnb-lambda(cve_, power(omega), einf(cover-sums(omega, cls, pm, cve_)))

### method-ii

    method-ii(mhm_, mhc_, mhp_) := vnb-lambda(mhe_, power(pts(mhm_)), esup({mhv_ in rr-pos-star: forsome([mhd_ in rr], pos-rr(mhd_) and mhv_ = (method-ii-delta(mhm_, mhc_, mhp_, mhd_))(mhe_))}))

### method-ii-delta

    method-ii-delta(mhm_, mhc_, mhp_, mhd_) := method-i-outer(pts(mhm_), {mha_ in mhc_: diam(mhm_, mha_) <= mhd_}, restrict(mhp_, {mha_ in mhc_: diam(mhm_, mha_) <= mhd_}))

### metric-group-group

    metric-group-group(mgr_) := [carr(mgr_), opr(mgr_), iden(mgr_), inv(mgr_)]

### metric-group-ms

    metric-group-ms(mgr_) := [carr(mgr_), gdist(mgr_)]

### metric-top

    metric-top(md) := [pts(md), {u in power(pts(md)): is-open(md, u)}]

### mi-add

    mi-add(x, u, v) := vnb-lambda(mia_, x, u(mia_) + v(mia_))

### mi-reindex

    mi-reindex(x, s, b) := vnb-lambda(mir_, x, b(s(mir_)))

### mi-zero

    mi-zero(x) := vnb-lambda(mia_, x, 0)

### mink-set

    mink-set(m, cs, x) := {mkr_ in rr: 0 < mkr_ and forsome([mky_ in cs], x = (act(m))(mkr_, mky_))}

### minkowski-functional

    minkowski-functional(m, cs) := vnb-lambda(mkx_, vec(m), inf(mink-set(m, cs, mkx_)))

### minor

    minor(s, r, c, n) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), entry(s, if(i < r, i, succ(i)), if(j < c, j, succ(j)))))

### monalg

    monalg(a, m) := [finsupp(a, m), vnb-lambda([f, g], cartesian(finsupp(a, m), finsupp(a, m)), monalg-add(a, m, f, g)), vnb-lambda([f, g], cartesian(finsupp(a, m), finsupp(a, m)), monalg-mul(a, m, f, g)), vnb-lambda(f, finsupp(a, m), monalg-neg(a, m, f)), monalg-zero(a, m), monalg-one(a, m)]

### monalg-add

    monalg-add(a, m, f, g) := vnb-lambda(x_, carr(m), (add(a))(f(x_), g(x_)))

### monalg-mul

    monalg-mul(a, m, f, g) := vnb-lambda(x_, carr(m), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(p, {p in cartesian(supp(a, m, f), supp(a, m, g)): (opr(m))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, (mul(a))(f(nth(1, p)), g(nth(2, p)))), {p in cartesian(supp(a, m, f), supp(a, m, g)): (opr(m))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}))

### monalg-neg

    monalg-neg(a, m, f) := vnb-lambda(x_, carr(m), (neg(a))(f(x_)))

### monalg-one

    monalg-one(a, m) := vnb-lambda(x_, carr(m), if(x_ = iden(m), one(a), zero(a)))

### monalg-pullback

    monalg-pullback(a, m, ph, f) := vnb-lambda(mpx_, carr(m), f(ph(mpx_)))

### monalg-zero

    monalg-zero(a, m) := vnb-lambda(x_, carr(m), zero(a))

### monic-poly

    monic-poly(f, a, n) := vnb-lambda(gmk_, nn, if(gmk_ = n, one(f), if(gmk_ < n, a(succ(gmk_)), zero(f))))

### monomial

    monomial(a, c, n) := vnb-lambda(x_, nn, if(x_ = n, c, zero(a)))

### monotone-class-generated

    monotone-class-generated(omega, ce) := {mca_ in power(omega): forall([mcb_], mcb_ in power(power(omega)) and is-monotone-class(omega, mcb_) and ce subset mcb_ implies mca_ in mcb_)}

### mpoly

    mpoly(r, n) := monalg(r, multi-index-monoid(interval(1, n)))

### msg-rank

    msg-rank(smt_, smk_, smm_) := iota(smr_, smr_ in nn and smm_ in msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smr_) and forall([smj_ in nn], smm_ in msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smj_) implies smr_ <= smj_))

### msg-set

    msg-set(smt_, smk_) := big-union(smn_, nn, msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smn_))

### mu-pinf

    mu-pinf(prp_) := {mpz_ in cc: forsome([mpk_ in nn], mpz_ ^ prp_ ^ mpk_ = 1)}

### multi-index-monoid

    multi-index-monoid(x) := [fun(x, nn), vnb-lambda([miu_, miv_], cartesian(fun(x, nn), fun(x, nn)), mi-add(x, miu_, miv_)), mi-zero(x)]

### nag-metric-space

    nag-metric-space(nag) := [carr(nag), vnb-lambda([u, v], cartesian(carr(nag), carr(nag)), (nrm(nag))((opr(nag))(u, (inv(nag))(v))))]

### neg-part

    neg-part(omega, f) := vnb-lambda(npx_, omega, if(0 <= f(npx_), 0, -f(npx_)))

### neg-variation

    neg-variation(ca, nu) := pos-variation(ca, signed-neg(ca, nu))

### nf-metric-space

    nf-metric-space(nf) := [carr(nf), vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(carr(nf), carr(nf)), (fnrm(nf))((add(nf))(x, (neg(nf))(y))))]

### nn-enum

    nn-enum(s) := choice({f in fun(nn, s): forall([m in nn, n in nn], m < n implies f(m) < f(n))})

### nnfst

    nnfst(n_) := iota(a_, a_ in nn and forsome([b_ in nn], nnpair(a_, b_) = n_))

### nnpair

    nnpair(i_, j_) := trinum(i_ + j_) + j_

### nnsnd

    nnsnd(n_) := iota(b_, b_ in nn and forsome([a_ in nn], nnpair(a_, b_) = n_))

### node-content

    node-content(ssp_, ssn_) := nth(2, nth(nth(1, ssn_), (strand-map(ssp_))(nth(2, ssn_))))

### node-dir

    node-dir(ssp_, ssn_) := nth(1, nth(nth(1, ssn_), (strand-map(ssp_))(nth(2, ssn_))))

### nodes

    nodes(ssp_) := {ssn_ in cartesian(nn, ids(ssp_)): 1 <= nth(1, ssn_) and nth(1, ssn_) <= length((strand-map(ssp_))(nth(2, ssn_)))}

### nv-ball

    nv-ball(cwk_, cwa_, cwr_) := {cwx_ in cwa_: (fnrm(cwk_))(cwx_) <= cwr_}

### nv-ideal2

    nv-ideal2(cwk_, cwa_, cwg_, cwh_) := {cwx_ in cwa_: forsome([cwm_ in cwa_, cwn_ in cwa_], cwx_ = (add(cwk_))((mul(cwk_))(cwm_, cwg_), (mul(cwk_))(cwn_, cwh_)))}

### nv-integers

    nv-integers(cwk_) := {cwx_ in carr(cwk_): (fnrm(cwk_))(cwx_) <= 1}

### nv-maximal

    nv-maximal(cwk_, cwa_) := {cwx_ in cwa_: (fnrm(cwk_))(cwx_) < 1}

### nv-multiples

    nv-multiples(cw3k_, cw3a_, cw3g_) := {cw3x_ in carr(cw3k_): forsome([cw3m_ in cw3a_], cw3x_ = (mul(cw3k_))(cw3m_, cw3g_))}

### nv-principal

    nv-principal(cwk_, cwa_, cwg_) := {cwx_ in cwa_: forsome([cwm_ in cwa_], cwx_ = (mul(cwk_))(cwm_, cwg_))}

### nv-res-ringoid

    nv-res-ringoid(rsk_, rsa_) := [rsa_, vnb-lambda([rsu_, rsv_], cartesian(rsa_, rsa_), (add(rsk_))(rsu_, rsv_)), vnb-lambda([rsu_, rsv_], cartesian(rsa_, rsa_), (mul(rsk_))(rsu_, rsv_)), vnb-lambda(rsu_, rsa_, (neg(rsk_))(rsu_)), zero(rsk_), one(rsk_), nv-maximal(rsk_, rsa_)]

### nv-residue

    nv-residue(rsk_, rsa_) := ringoid-quotient-ring(nv-res-ringoid(rsk_, rsa_))

### nv-units

    nv-units(cwk_, cwa_) := {cwx_ in cwa_: forsome([cwy_ in cwa_], (mul(cwk_))(cwx_, cwy_) = one(cwk_))}

### nvs-metric-space

    nvs-metric-space(m) := [vec(m), vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(vec(m), vec(m)), (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(x, (vneg(m))(y))))]

### ocint

    ocint(a, b) := {osq_ in rr: a < osq_ and osq_ <= b}

### ooint

    ooint(a, b) := {icx_ in rr: a < icx_ and icx_ < b}

### op-add

    op-add(goa-md_, goa-t_) := vnb-lambda([goaf_, goag_], cartesian(ops(goa-md_, goa-t_), ops(goa-md_, goa-t_)), vnb-lambda(goaw_, vec(goa-md_), (vadd(goa-md_))(goaf_(goaw_), goag_(goaw_))))

### op-alg

    op-alg(gal-md_, gal-t_) := [ops(gal-md_, gal-t_), op-add(gal-md_, gal-t_), op-mul(gal-md_, gal-t_), op-neg(gal-md_, gal-t_), op-zero(gal-md_), op-one(gal-md_)]

### op-mul

    op-mul(gom-md_, gom-t_) := vnb-lambda([gomf_, gomg_], cartesian(ops(gom-md_, gom-t_), ops(gom-md_, gom-t_)), vnb-lambda(gomw_, vec(gom-md_), gomf_(gomg_(gomw_))))

### op-neg

    op-neg(gon-md_, gon-t_) := vnb-lambda(gonf_, ops(gon-md_, gon-t_), vnb-lambda(gonw_, vec(gon-md_), (vneg(gon-md_))(gonf_(gonw_))))

### op-of

    op-of(gof-md_, gof-t_, gof-p_) := vnb-lambda(gofw_, vec(gof-md_), poly-op(gof-md_, gof-t_, gof-p_, gofw_))

### op-one

    op-one(goo-md_) := vnb-lambda(goow_, vec(goo-md_), goow_)

### op-zero

    op-zero(goz-md_) := vnb-lambda(gozw_, vec(goz-md_), vzero(goz-md_))

### open-fun

    open-fun(mxu_) := vnb-lambda([mxw_, mxj_], cartesian(expr-set, nn), expr-open(mxw_, mxj_, mxu_))

### opg-ext

    opg-ext(rpg_, rpa_, rpp_) := vnb-lambda(rvt_, rr, opg-val(rpg_, rpa_, rpp_, rvt_))

### opg-val

    opg-val(rpg_, rpa_, rpp_, rvt_) := iota(rvy_, rvy_ in carr(rpg_) and opg-rep(rpg_, rpa_, rpp_, rvt_, rvy_))

### ops

    ops(gos-md_, gos-t_) := {gosf_ in fun(vec(gos-md_), vec(gos-md_)): forsome([gosp_ in carr(poly(scal(gos-md_)))], gosf_ = op-of(gos-md_, gos-t_, gosp_))}

### pad-ones

    pad-ones(m, k, f) := vnb-lambda(poj_, interval(1, m), if(poj_ <= k, 1, f(nn-minus(poj_, k))))

### padic-abs

    padic-abs(cpp_) := vnb-lambda(cpx_, qq, iota(cpv_, cpv_ in rr and padic-rel(cpp_, cpx_, cpv_)))

### padic-char

    padic-char(cpp_) := vnb-lambda(cpx_, qq, iota(cpw_, cpw_ in cc and forsome([cpr_ in qq], padic-frac-rep(cpp_, cpx_, cpr_) and cpw_ = cc-exp(((2 * pi) * 1i) * cpr_))))

### permutations

    permutations(n) := injection(ord-segment(n), ord-segment(n))

### perp

    perp(v, ks) := {prp_ in vec(v): forall([prk_ in ks], (ip(v))(prp_, prk_) = 0)}

### phi-pm

    phi-pm(mhm_, mhf_) := vnb-lambda(mhh_, power(pts(mhm_)), if(diam(mhm_, mhh_) = pos-inf, pos-inf, mhf_(diam(mhm_, mhh_))))

### polar

    polar(m, t, a) := {plf_ in top-dual(m, t): forall([plx_ in a], plf_(plx_) <= 1)}

### pole-order

    pole-order(pof_, poa_) := iota(pon_, is-pole(pof_, poa_, pon_))

### poly

    poly(a) := monalg(a, nn-add-monoid)

### poly-eval

    poly-eval(a, p, t) := sum-ag(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(gek_, nn, (mul(a))(p(gek_), ring-power(a, t, gek_))), succ(deg(a, p)))

### poly-fn-deg

    poly-fn-deg(kk, a, f) := iota(pdgn_, pdgn_ in nn and is-poly-deg(kk, a, f, pdgn_))

### poly-le

    poly-le(a, m) := {gpl_ in carr(poly(a)): deg-bound(a, gpl_, m)}

### poly-op

    poly-op(gpo-md_, gpo-t_, gpo-p_, gpo-w_) := sum-ag(module-vector-ag(gpo-md_), vnb-lambda(gpok_, nn, (act(gpo-md_))(gpo-p_(gpok_), op-iter(gpo-md_, gpo-t_, gpo-w_, gpok_))), succ(deg(scal(gpo-md_), gpo-p_)))

### pos-part

    pos-part(omega, f) := vnb-lambda(ppx_, omega, if(0 <= f(ppx_), f(ppx_), 0))

### pos-variation

    pos-variation(ca, nu) := vnb-lambda(pva_, ca, esup({pvv_ in rr-pos-star: forsome([pve_ in ca], pve_ subset pva_ and nu(pve_) = pvv_)}))

### powers-union

    powers-union(pug_, puv_) := big-union(puk_, nn, set-power(pug_, puv_, puk_))

### prec-strict

    prec-strict(spc_, snd_, ssd_) := {ssp_ in cartesian(snd_, snd_): forall([ssq_ in power(cartesian(snd_, snd_))], is-prec-closed(spc_, snd_, ssd_, ssq_) implies ssp_ in ssq_)}

### precedes

    precedes(ssc_) := union(precedes-strict(ssc_), {ssp_ in cartesian(bnodes(ssc_), bnodes(ssc_)): nth(1, ssp_) = nth(2, ssp_)})

### precedes-strict

    precedes-strict(ssc_) := prec-strict(bspace(ssc_), bnodes(ssc_), bsend(ssc_))

### pred

    pred(n_) := iota(m_, m_ in nn and succ(m_) = n_)

### prefix-dist

    prefix-dist(ppa_, pdx_, pdy_) := if(pdx_ = pdy_, 0, recip(prefix-prod(ppa_, pdx_, agree-length(pdx_, pdy_))))

### preimage

    preimage(s, f, v) := {a in pts(s): f(a) in v}

### primitive-roots

    primitive-roots(prn_) := {prz_ in cc: prz_ ^ prn_ = 1 and forall([prm_ in nn], 1 <= prm_ implies prm_ < prn_ implies not(prz_ ^ prm_ = 1))}

### principal-ideal

    principal-ideal(s, a) := {x in carr(s): forsome([r in carr(s)], x = (mul(s))(r, a))}

### prod-fun

    prod-fun(pfu_, pff_) := vnb-lambda(pfx_, pfu_, prod-value(vnb-lambda(pfk_, nn, 1 + (pff_(pfk_))(pfx_))))

### prod-ring

    prod-ring(r, f, s) := finprod(commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm(r), f, s)

### prod-rmod

    prod-rmod(m) := [scal(m), cartesian(vec(m), rr), pv-vadd(m), [vzero(m), 0], pv-vneg(m), pv-act(m)]

### prod-set

    prod-set(cm, s, f) := finsum(cm, f, s)

### prod-top

    prod-top(t) := [cartesian(pts(t), rr), {ptu_ in power(cartesian(pts(t), rr)): is-pt-open(t, ptu_)}]

### prod-value

    prod-value(pvh_) := iota(pvl_, pvl_ in cc and prod-converges-to(pvh_, pvl_))

### product-carrier

    product-carrier(ms) := {x in fun(nn, big-union(n, nn, pts(ms(n)))): forall([n in nn], x(n) in pts(ms(n)))}

### product-measure

    product-measure(xs, ca, mu, ys, cb, nu) := vnb-lambda(pmc_, product-sigma(xs, ca, ys, cb), integral(xs, ca, mu, x-section-measure(xs, ys, nu, pmc_)))

### product-metric

    product-metric(ms) := product-metric-w(ms, vnb-lambda(n, nn, (/)(1, 2 ^ (n + 1))))

### product-metric-w

    product-metric-w(ms, w) := [product-carrier(ms), vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(product-carrier(ms), product-carrier(ms)), iota(l, l in rr and series-converges-to(vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n))), l)))]

### product-proj

    product-proj(ms, n) := vnb-lambda(x, product-carrier(ms), x(n))

### product-sigma

    product-sigma(xs, ca, ys, cb) := sigma-generated(cartesian(xs, ys), rectangles(xs, ca, ys, cb))

### product2-ms

    product2-ms(s, t) := [cartesian(pts(s), pts(t)), vnb-lambda([p2u_, p2v_], cartesian(cartesian(pts(s), pts(t)), cartesian(pts(s), pts(t))), max((dist(s))(nth(1, p2u_), nth(1, p2v_)), (dist(t))(nth(2, p2u_), nth(2, p2v_))))]

### proj

    proj(s) := vnb-lambda(a, pts(s), class(s, a))

### pruefer

    pruefer(prp_) := quotient-ag(normed-field-additive-ag(qp-field(prp_)), zp(prp_))

### ps-partial-sum

    ps-partial-sum(coef, x, k) := sum-ag(normed-field-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(n, nn, coef(n) * x ^ n), k)

### pseudo-gauge-top

    pseudo-gauge-top(fam, ground) := [ground, {u in power(ground): is-open-gauge(fam, ground, u)}]

### pt-box

    pt-box(t, v, c, e) := {ptz_ in cartesian(pts(t), rr): nth(1, ptz_) in v and abs(nth(2, ptz_) - c) < e}

### pt-set-dist

    pt-set-dist(m, x, es) := einf({mov_ in rr-pos-star: forsome([moz_ in es], mov_ = (dist(m))(x, moz_))})

### pts-left-cosets

    pts-left-cosets(lcs_, lcg_, lch_) := {lcu_ in power(pts(lcs_)): forsome([lcx_ in carr(lcg_)], lcu_ = {lcz_ in pts(lcs_): (opr(lcg_))((inv(lcg_))(lcx_), lcz_) in lch_})}

### ptwise-eplus

    ptwise-eplus(omega, f, g) := vnb-lambda(x_, omega, eplus(f(x_), g(x_)))

### ptwise-epow

    ptwise-epow(epo_, epf_, epp_) := vnb-lambda(epx_, epo_, epow(epf_(epx_), epp_))

### ptwise-etimes

    ptwise-etimes(omega, f, g) := vnb-lambda(x_, omega, etimes(f(x_), g(x_)))

### ptwise-liminf

    ptwise-liminf(omega, cf) := vnb-lambda(x_, omega, eliminf(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, (cf(n_))(x_))))

### ptwise-net-limits

    ptwise-net-limits(t, ee) := {pwf_ in fun(pts(t), rr): forsome([pwd_, pwp_], pwd_ in directed-set and is-directed-set(pwd_) and pwp_ in fun(pts(pwd_), ee) and forall([pwx_ in pts(t)], net-converges-to(metric-top(rr-ms), pwd_, vnb-lambda(pwl_, pts(pwd_), (pwp_(pwl_))(pwx_)), pwf_(pwx_))))}

### ptwise-scale

    ptwise-scale(omega, c, f) := vnb-lambda(x_, omega, etimes(c, f(x_)))

### ptwise-sup

    ptwise-sup(omega, cf) := vnb-lambda(x_, omega, esup(image(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, (cf(n_))(x_)), nn)))

### pullback-measure

    pullback-measure(omega, cb, f, nu) := vnb-lambda(mpm_, pullback-sigma(omega, cb, f), nu(image(f, mpm_)))

### pullback-ms

    pullback-ms(mt, ss, jj) := [ss, vnb-lambda([pbu_, pbv_], cartesian(ss, ss), (dist(mt))(jj(pbu_), jj(pbv_)))]

### pullback-sigma

    pullback-sigma(omega, cb, f) := {mpa_ in power(omega): forsome([mpb_ in cb], mpa_ = {mpx_ in omega: f(mpx_) in mpb_})}

### push-forward

    push-forward(omega, cb, mu, h) := vnb-lambda(pwb_, cb, mu({pwx_ in omega: h(pwx_) in pwb_}))

### pv-act

    pv-act(m) := vnb-lambda([pvr_, pvc_], cartesian(carr(scal(m)), cartesian(vec(m), rr)), [(act(m))(pvr_, nth(1, pvc_)), pvr_ * nth(2, pvc_)])

### pv-vadd

    pv-vadd(m) := vnb-lambda([pva_, pvb_], cartesian(cartesian(vec(m), rr), cartesian(vec(m), rr)), [(vadd(m))(nth(1, pva_), nth(1, pvb_)), nth(2, pva_) + nth(2, pvb_)])

### pv-vneg

    pv-vneg(m) := vnb-lambda(pvc_, cartesian(vec(m), rr), [(vneg(m))(nth(1, pvc_)), -nth(2, pvc_)])

### pw-int

    pw-int(pphi_, a, b) := iota(pav_, pav_ in rr and forsome([paw_], is-primitive(paw_, pphi_, a, b) and pav_ = paw_(b) - paw_(a)))

### qmod-act

    qmod-act(md, sm) := vnb-lambda([qmr_, qmc_], cartesian(carr(scal(md)), quotient(qmod-setoid(md, sm))), (qmod-scale(md, sm, qmr_))(qmc_))

### qmod-rel

    qmod-rel(md, sm) := {qmp_ in cartesian(vec(md), vec(md)): (vadd(md))(nth(1, qmp_), (vneg(md))(nth(2, qmp_))) in sm}

### qmod-scale

    qmod-scale(md, sm, r) := descend(qmod-setoid(md, sm), vnb-lambda(qma_, vec(md), class(qmod-setoid(md, sm), (act(md))(r, qma_))))

### qmod-setoid

    qmod-setoid(md, sm) := [vec(md), qmod-rel(md, sm)]

### qmod-vadd

    qmod-vadd(md, sm) := descend2(qmod-setoid(md, sm), vnb-lambda([qma_, qmb_], cartesian(vec(md), vec(md)), class(qmod-setoid(md, sm), (vadd(md))(qma_, qmb_))))

### qmod-vneg

    qmod-vneg(md, sm) := descend(qmod-setoid(md, sm), vnb-lambda(qma_, vec(md), class(qmod-setoid(md, sm), (vneg(md))(qma_))))

### qp

    qp(qsp_) := completion(nf-metric-space(qq-padic(qsp_)))

### qp-abs

    qp-abs(qsp_) := vnb-lambda(qsx_, quotient(cauchy-setoid(nf-metric-space(qq-padic(qsp_)))), (dist(completion(nf-metric-space(qq-padic(qsp_)))))(qsx_, class(cauchy-setoid(nf-metric-space(qq-padic(qsp_))), vnb-lambda(qsn_, nn, 0))))

### qp-cs

    qp-cs(qsp_) := cseq(nf-metric-space(qq-padic(qsp_)))

### qp-csr

    qp-csr(qsp_) := [qp-cs(qsp_), qp-sadd(qsp_), qp-smul(qsp_), qp-sneg(qsp_), vnb-lambda(qsn_, nn, 0), vnb-lambda(qsn_, nn, 1), qp-null(qsp_)]

### qp-digit

    qp-digit(qsp_, qdy_) := iota(qdd_, qdd_ in zz and 0 <= qdd_ and qdd_ < qsp_ and (qp-abs(qsp_))((add(qp-field(qsp_)))(qdy_, (neg(qp-field(qsp_)))((embed(nf-metric-space(qq-padic(qsp_))))(qdd_)))) < 1)

### qp-field

    qp-field(qsp_) := [carr(qp-ring(qsp_)), add(qp-ring(qsp_)), mul(qp-ring(qsp_)), neg(qp-ring(qsp_)), zero(qp-ring(qsp_)), one(qp-ring(qsp_)), qp-abs(qsp_)]

### qp-lambda

    qp-lambda(qsp_) := vnb-lambda(qly_, carr(qp-field(qsp_)), qp-lambda-at(qsp_, qly_))

### qp-lambda-at

    qp-lambda-at(qsp_, qlx_) := iota(qlw_, qlw_ in cc and forsome([qlu_ in qq], (qp-abs(qsp_))((add(qp-field(qsp_)))(qlx_, (neg(qp-field(qsp_)))((embed(nf-metric-space(qq-padic(qsp_))))(qlu_)))) <= 1 and qlw_ = (padic-char(qsp_))(qlu_)))

### qp-ms

    qp-ms(qsp_) := nf-metric-space(qq-padic(qsp_))

### qp-null

    qp-null(qsp_) := {qsu_ in qp-cs(qsp_): cseq-equiv(nf-metric-space(qq-padic(qsp_)), qsu_, vnb-lambda(qsn_, nn, 0))}

### qp-ord

    qp-ord(qsp_) := vnb-lambda(qox_, carr(qp-field(qsp_)), log((qp-abs(qsp_))(qox_)) * recip-star(log(recip-star(2))))

### qp-ppow

    qp-ppow(qsp_, qsk_) := iota(qpy_, qpy_ in carr(qp-field(qsp_)) and (mul(qp-field(qsp_)))(qpy_, (embed(nf-metric-space(qq-padic(qsp_))))(qsp_ ^ max(-qsk_, 0))) = (embed(nf-metric-space(qq-padic(qsp_))))(qsp_ ^ max(qsk_, 0)))

### qp-psum

    qp-psum(qsp_, qta_, qtn_, qtm_) := sum-ag(normed-field-additive-ag(qp-field(qsp_)), qp-term(qsp_, qta_, qtn_), qtm_)

### qp-ring

    qp-ring(qsp_) := ringoid-quotient-ring(qp-csr(qsp_))

### qp-sadd

    qp-sadd(qsp_) := vnb-lambda([qsu_, qsv_], cartesian(qp-cs(qsp_), qp-cs(qsp_)), vnb-lambda(qsn_, nn, qsu_(qsn_) + qsv_(qsn_)))

### qp-smul

    qp-smul(qsp_) := vnb-lambda([qsu_, qsv_], cartesian(qp-cs(qsp_), qp-cs(qsp_)), vnb-lambda(qsn_, nn, qsu_(qsn_) * qsv_(qsn_)))

### qp-sneg

    qp-sneg(qsp_) := vnb-lambda(qsu_, qp-cs(qsp_), vnb-lambda(qsn_, nn, -qsu_(qsn_)))

### qp-term

    qp-term(qsp_, qta_, qtn_) := vnb-lambda(qtj_, nn, (mul(qp-field(qsp_)))((embed(nf-metric-space(qq-padic(qsp_))))(qta_(qtn_ + qtj_)), qp-ppow(qsp_, qtn_ + qtj_)))

### qq-nv

    qq-nv(cpf_) := [qq, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_), 0, 1, cpf_]

### qq-padic

    qq-padic(cpp_) := [qq, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_), 0, 1, padic-abs(cpp_)]

### quotient

    quotient(s) := image(proj(s), pts(s))

### quotient-ag

    quotient-ag(qag_, qah_) := module-vector-ag(quotient-module(ag-zz-module(qag_), qah_))

### quotient-module

    quotient-module(md, sm) := [scal(md), quotient(qmod-setoid(md, sm)), qmod-vadd(md, sm), class(qmod-setoid(md, sm), vzero(md)), qmod-vneg(md, sm), qmod-act(md, sm)]

### r-exp

    r-exp(y_) := iota(x_, x_ in rr and 0 < x_ and log(x_) = y_)

### ran

    ran(f) := image(f, dom(f))

### recip-star

    recip-star(u_) := if(u_ = 0, 0, recip(u_))

### rectangles

    rectangles(xs, ca, ys, cb) := {rcr_ in power(cartesian(xs, ys)): forsome([rca_ in ca, rcb_ in cb], rcr_ = cartesian(rca_, rcb_))}

### related

    related(s, a, b) := [a, b] in rel(s)

### residue

    residue(mrf_, mra_) := iota(mrx_, mrx_ in cc and forsome([mrr_ in rr], 0 < mrr_ and forall([mrp_ in rr], 0 < mrp_ implies mrp_ < mrr_ implies mrx_ = recip((2 * pi) * 1i) * line-int(vnb-lambda(mrw_, trace(circle-path(mra_, mrp_), 0, 2 * pi), mrf_(mrw_)), circle-path(mra_, mrp_), circle-deriv(mra_, mrp_), 0, 2 * pi))))

### respects

    respects(s, f) := forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], related(s, a, b) implies f(a) = f(b))

### respects2

    respects2(s, f) := forall([a, b, a_, b_], a in pts(s) implies b in pts(s) implies a_ in pts(s) implies b_ in pts(s) implies related(s, a, a_) and related(s, b, b_) implies f(a, b) = f(a_, b_))

### restrict

    restrict(f, a) := vnb-lambda(rsx_, a, f(rsx_))

### restrict-sigma

    restrict-sigma(rsc_, rse_) := {rsa_ in rsc_: rsa_ subset rse_}

### rewr

    rewr(mrt_, mrk_) := {mrx_ in rw-univ: forall([mrs_ in power(rw-univ)], rw-closed(mrt_, mrk_, mrs_) implies mrx_ in mrs_)}

### rf-set

    rf-set(md14h_, md14x_, md14l_) := {md14p_ in cartesian(nn, expr-set): forall([md14w_ in power(cartesian(nn, expr-set))], rf-closed(md14h_, md14x_, md14l_, md14w_) implies md14p_ in md14w_)}

### ring-as-module

    ring-as-module(r) := [r, carr(r), add(r), zero(r), neg(r), mul(r)]

### ring-power

    ring-power(r, x, n) := mpow(commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm(r), x, n)

### ring-prod

    ring-prod(x, y) := [cartesian(carr(x), carr(y)), vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian(cartesian(carr(x), carr(y)), cartesian(carr(x), carr(y))), [(add(x))(nth(1, p), nth(1, q)), (add(y))(nth(2, p), nth(2, q))]), vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian(cartesian(carr(x), carr(y)), cartesian(carr(x), carr(y))), [(mul(x))(nth(1, p), nth(1, q)), (mul(y))(nth(2, p), nth(2, q))]), vnb-lambda(p, cartesian(carr(x), carr(y)), [(neg(x))(nth(1, p)), (neg(y))(nth(2, p))]), [zero(x), zero(y)], [one(x), one(y)]]

### ringoid-quotient

    ringoid-quotient(r) := quotient(ringoid-setoid(r))

### ringoid-quotient-ring

    ringoid-quotient-ring(r) := [quotient(ringoid-setoid(r)), descend2(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (add(r))(a, b)))), descend2(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (mul(r))(a, b)))), descend(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda(a, carr(r), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (neg(r))(a)))), class(ringoid-setoid(r), zero(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), one(r))]

### ringoid-rel

    ringoid-rel(r) := {p in cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)): (add(r))(nth(1, p), (neg(r))(nth(2, p))) in idl(r)}

### ringoid-setoid

    ringoid-setoid(r) := [carr(r), ringoid-rel(r)]

### rintegral

    rintegral(omega, ca, mu, f) := integral(omega, ca, mu, pos-part(omega, f)) - integral(omega, ca, mu, neg-part(omega, f))

### rm-ball

    rm-ball(s, dseq, n) := closed-ball(s, dseq(nnfst(n)), recip(nnsnd(n) + 1))

### rm-code

    rm-code(s, dseq, x) := vnb-lambda(rox_, nn, if(x in rm-ball(s, dseq, rox_), 1, 0))

### rm-cyl

    rm-cyl(s, dseq, a, nb) := {rce_ in rm-k(s, dseq): forall([rci_ in nn], rci_ < nb implies rce_(rci_) = a(rci_))}

### rm-k

    rm-k(s, dseq) := {rke_ in pts(cantor-ms): forall([rkn_ in nn], forsome([rky_ in pts(s)], rm-realizes(s, dseq, rke_, rkn_, rky_)))}

### rm-kms

    rm-kms(s, dseq) := subspace-ms(cantor-ms, rm-k(s, dseq))

### rm-pi

    rm-pi(s, dseq) := vnb-lambda(rpe_, rm-k(s, dseq), iota(rpy_, rpy_ in pts(s) and rm-address(s, dseq, rpe_, rpy_)))

### rm-shell

    rm-shell(s, dseq, n) := {rsy_ in pts(s): recip(nnsnd(n) + 1) <= (dist(s))(dseq(nnfst(n)), rsy_)}

### rn

    rn(n) := [normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), fun(interval(1, n), rr), vnb-lambda([rnax_, rnay_], cartesian(fun(interval(1, n), rr), fun(interval(1, n), rr)), vnb-lambda(rnai_, interval(1, n), rnax_(rnai_) + rnay_(rnai_))), vnb-lambda(rnzi_, interval(1, n), 0), vnb-lambda(rnnx_, fun(interval(1, n), rr), vnb-lambda(rnni_, interval(1, n), -rnnx_(rnni_))), vnb-lambda([rnsr_, rnsx_], cartesian(rr, fun(interval(1, n), rr)), vnb-lambda(rnsi_, interval(1, n), rnsr_ * rnsx_(rnsi_))), vnb-lambda(rnvx_, fun(interval(1, n), rr), rn-maxabs(rnvx_, n))]

### rn-box

    rn-box(n, jj) := {mrx_ in fun(interval(1, n), rr): forall([mri_ in interval(1, n)], mrx_(mri_) in jj(mri_))}

### rn-rectangles

    rn-rectangles(n) := {mrr_ in power(fun(interval(1, n), rr)): forsome([mrj_ in fun(interval(1, n), power(rr))], forall([mri_ in interval(1, n)], is-interval(mrj_(mri_))) and mrr_ = rn-box(n, mrj_))}

### rn-trunc

    rn-trunc(n, x, k) := vnb-lambda(rntj_, interval(1, n), if(rntj_ <= k, x(rntj_), 0))

### rn-unit

    rn-unit(n, i) := vnb-lambda(rnuj_, interval(1, n), if(rnuj_ = i, 1, 0))

### road-length

    road-length(rld_, rla_, rlb_) := pw-int(vnb-lambda(rlt_, ccint(rla_, rlb_), magnitude(rld_(rlt_))), rla_, rlb_)

### rpow-star

    rpow-star(x_, s_) := if(0 < x_, r-exp(s_ * log(x_)), if(0 < s_, 0, 1))

### rr-fn-space

    rr-fn-space(rfv_) := [field-as-integral-domain(rr-field), rfv_, vnb-lambda([rff_, rfg_], cartesian(rfv_, rfv_), vnb-lambda(rfx_, rr, rff_(rfx_) + rfg_(rfx_))), vnb-lambda(rfx_, rr, 0), vnb-lambda(rff_, rfv_, vnb-lambda(rfx_, rr, -rff_(rfx_))), vnb-lambda([rfc_, rff_], cartesian(rr, rfv_), vnb-lambda(rfx_, rr, rfc_ * rff_(rfx_)))]

### schanuel-map

    schanuel-map(m, n, la, lb) := vnb-lambda(scz_, vec(zz-pow(m)), seq-join(m, n, (vadd(zz-pow(n)))(seq-shift(n, scz_), (vneg(zz-pow(n)))(lb(seq-head(n, scz_)))), (vadd(zz-pow(n)))(seq-head(n, scz_), la((vadd(zz-pow(n)))(seq-shift(n, scz_), (vneg(zz-pow(n)))(lb(seq-head(n, scz_))))))))

### sdet

    sdet(r, m, n, a, s) := {sdx_ in carr(r): forsome([sdp_, sdq_], sdp_ in injection(interval(1, s), interval(1, m)) and sdq_ in injection(interval(1, s), interval(1, n)) and sdx_ = det(r, s, submatrix(a, s, sdp_, sdq_)))}

### seg-curve

    seg-curve(m, f, a, eta) := vnb-lambda(u_, rr, f((vadd(m))(a, (act(m))(u_, eta))))

### seg-deriv

    seg-deriv(sgp_, sgq_) := vnb-lambda(sgs_, ccint(0, 1), sgq_ - sgp_)

### seg-int

    seg-int(sgf_, sgp_, sgq_) := line-int(sgf_, seg-path(sgp_, sgq_), seg-deriv(sgp_, sgq_), 0, 1)

### seg-path

    seg-path(sgp_, sgq_) := vnb-lambda(sgs_, ccint(0, 1), sgp_ + sgs_ * (sgq_ - sgp_))

### seminorm-ext

    seminorm-ext(m, s, p, q) := vnb-lambda(sxy_, vec(m), inf(seminorm-ext-set(m, s, p, q, sxy_)))

### seminorm-ext-set

    seminorm-ext-set(m, s, p, q, y) := {sxt_ in rr: forsome([sev_ in s, sew_ in vec(m)], y = (vadd(m))(sev_, sew_) and sxt_ = p(sev_) + q(sew_))}

### seminorm-top

    seminorm-top(m, ix, pf) := [vec(m), {snu_ in power(vec(m)): is-sn-open(m, ix, pf, snu_)}]

### seq-cons

    seq-cons(sck_, scv_) := vnb-lambda(scj_, nn, if(scj_ = 0, sck_, scv_(nn-minus(scj_, 1))))

### seq-head

    seq-head(n, z) := vnb-lambda(shi_, interval(1, n), z(shi_))

### seq-join

    seq-join(m, n, hd, tl) := vnb-lambda(jnj_, interval(1, m), if(jnj_ <= n, hd(jnj_), tl(nn-minus(jnj_, n))))

### seq-limit

    seq-limit(f) := if(converges(rr-ms, f), iota(lm_, converges-to(rr-ms, f, lm_)), 0)

### seq-proj

    seq-proj(spk_, spz_) := vnb-lambda(spj_, nn, spz_(nnpair(spk_, spj_)))

### seq-shift

    seq-shift(n, z) := vnb-lambda(sfi_, interval(1, n), z(n + sfi_))

### seq-space-ms

    seq-space-ms(sqx_) := product-metric-w(vnb-lambda(sqk_, nn, discrete-ms(sqx_)), vnb-lambda(sqk_, nn, (/)(1, 2 ^ sqk_)))

### seq-tail

    seq-tail(stz_) := vnb-lambda(stj_, nn, stz_(succ(stj_)))

### series-limit

    series-limit(f) := iota(lm_, lm_ in rr and series-converges-to(f, lm_))

### series-partial-sum

    series-partial-sum(f, k) := sum-ag(normed-field-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), f, k)

### set-dist

    set-dist(m, aa, bb) := einf({mow_ in rr-pos-star: forsome([mox_ in aa, moy_ in bb], mow_ = (dist(m))(mox_, moy_))})

### set-prod

    set-prod(spg_, spa_, spb_) := {spz_ in carr(spg_): forsome([spx_ in spa_, spy_ in spb_], spz_ = (opr(spg_))(spx_, spy_))}

### sigma-generated

    sigma-generated(omega, ce) := {a_ in power(omega): forall([cb_], cb_ in power(power(omega)) and is-sigma-algebra(omega, cb_) and ce subset cb_ implies a_ in cb_)}

### signed-neg

    signed-neg(ca, nu) := vnb-lambda(sna_, ca, ext-neg(nu(sna_)))

### simple-integral

    simple-integral(omega, mu, s) := finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, vnb-lambda(siy_, image(s, omega), etimes(siy_, mu({six_ in omega: s(six_) = siy_}))), image(s, omega))

### singleton

    singleton(y_) := {y_}

### sip-metric-space

    sip-metric-space(v) := nag-metric-space(sip-normed-ag(v))

### sip-normed-ag

    sip-normed-ag(v) := [vec(v), vadd(v), vzero(v), vneg(v), vnb-lambda(shn_, vec(v), sqrt((rip(v))(shn_, shn_)))]

### sip-null

    sip-null(v) := {sqn_ in vec(v): (rip(v))(sqn_, sqn_) = 0}

### sip-perp

    sip-perp(v, ks) := {spp_ in vec(v): forall([spk_ in ks], (rip(v))(spp_, spk_) = 0)}

### sip-qact

    sip-qact(v) := vnb-lambda([sqr_, sqc_], cartesian(rr, quotient(sip-setoid(v))), (sip-qscale(v, sqr_))(sqc_))

### sip-qrip

    sip-qrip(v) := descend2(sip-setoid(v), vnb-lambda([sqa_, sqb_], cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), (rip(v))(sqa_, sqb_)))

### sip-qscale

    sip-qscale(v, r) := descend(sip-setoid(v), vnb-lambda(sqa_, vec(v), class(sip-setoid(v), (act(v))(r, sqa_))))

### sip-quotient

    sip-quotient(v) := [scal(v), quotient(sip-setoid(v)), sip-qvadd(v), class(sip-setoid(v), vzero(v)), sip-qvneg(v), sip-qact(v), sip-qrip(v)]

### sip-qvadd

    sip-qvadd(v) := descend2(sip-setoid(v), vnb-lambda([sqa_, sqb_], cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), class(sip-setoid(v), (vadd(v))(sqa_, sqb_))))

### sip-qvneg

    sip-qvneg(v) := descend(sip-setoid(v), vnb-lambda(sqa_, vec(v), class(sip-setoid(v), (vneg(v))(sqa_))))

### sip-rel

    sip-rel(v) := {sqp_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)): (rip(v))((vadd(v))(nth(1, sqp_), (vneg(v))(nth(2, sqp_))), (vadd(v))(nth(1, sqp_), (vneg(v))(nth(2, sqp_)))) = 0}

### sip-setoid

    sip-setoid(v) := [vec(v), sip-rel(v)]

### size

    size(m) := [length(m), if(length(m) = 0, 0, length(nth(1, m)))]

### sn-ball

    sn-ball(m, pf, ff, x, eps) := {snby_ in vec(m): forall([sni_ in ff], (pf(sni_))((vadd(m))(snby_, (vneg(m))(x))) <= eps)}

### sn-oball

    sn-oball(m, pf, ff, x, r) := {snoy_ in vec(m): forsome([snoe_ in rr], pos-rr(snoe_) and forall([sni_ in ff], (pf(sni_))((vadd(m))(snoy_, (vneg(m))(x))) + snoe_ <= r))}

### snoc-col

    snoc-col(w, n, x) := matof(succ(n), 1, vnb-lambda([i_, j_], cartesian(interval(1, succ(n)), interval(1, 1)), if(i_ = succ(n), x, entry(w, i_, 1))))

### snoc-row

    snoc-row(c, n, r) := matof(1, succ(n), vnb-lambda([i_, j_], cartesian(interval(1, 1), interval(1, succ(n))), if(j_ = succ(n), r, entry(c, 1, j_))))

### snowflake

    snowflake(snfs_, snfe_) := [pts(snfs_), vnb-lambda([snfu_, snfv_], cartesian(pts(snfs_), pts(snfs_)), rpow-star((dist(snfs_))(snfu_, snfv_), snfe_))]

### souslin-set

    souslin-set(s, sv) := {sxp_ in pts(s): forsome([sxz_ in fun(nn, nn)], forall([sxn_ in nn], sxp_ in sv(restrict(sxz_, ord-segment(sxn_)))))}

### span

    span(md, n, u) := {x_ in vec(md): forsome([c_ in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md)))], x_ = lincomb(md, n, c_, u))}

### span-add-one

    span-add-one(m, s, v) := {y_ in vec(m): forsome([x_ in s, r_ in rr], y_ = (vadd(m))(x_, (act(m))(r_, v)))}

### span2

    span2(s2m_, s2e_, s2x_) := {s2v_ in vec(s2m_): forsome([s2r_ in rr, s2s_ in rr], s2v_ = (vadd(s2m_))((act(s2m_))(s2r_, s2e_), (act(s2m_))(s2s_, s2x_)))}

### sphere

    sphere(cuvs_, cuvc_, cuvr_) := {cuvy_ in pts(cuvs_): (dist(cuvs_))(cuvc_, cuvy_) = cuvr_}

### sqn

    sqn(a) := fun(nn, a)

### sqrt

    sqrt(a_) := iota(x_, x_ in rr and 0 <= x_ and x_ * x_ = a_)

### strands

    strands(sst_, ssk_) := tuples(directed(sst_, ssk_))

### strict-epigraph

    strict-epigraph(m, p) := {sepw_ in cartesian(vec(m), rr): p(nth(1, sepw_)) < nth(2, sepw_)}

### strict-reach

    strict-reach(md7e_) := {md7z_ in expr-set: forall([md7w_ in power(expr-set)], md7e_ in md7w_ implies strict-closed(md7w_) implies md7z_ in md7w_)}

### subadd-reg

    subadd-reg(srm_, srg_, srx_) := einf(fact-sums(srm_, srg_, srx_))

### submat

    submat(s, p, q) := matof(p, q, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, p), interval(1, q)), entry(s, succ(i), succ(j))))

### submatrix

    submatrix(a, s, p, q) := matof(s, s, vnb-lambda([smi_, smj_], cartesian(interval(1, s), interval(1, s)), entry(a, p(smi_), q(smj_))))

### submodule-norm

    submodule-norm(md, sm) := if(forsome([nmn_, nmx_, nmy_, nma_], is-norm-data(md, sm, nmn_, nmx_, nmy_, nma_)), iota(nmd_, forsome([nmn_, nmx_, nmy_, nma_], is-norm-data(md, sm, nmn_, nmx_, nmy_, nma_) and nmd_ = abs(det(zz-ring, nmn_, nma_)))), 0)

### subseq

    subseq(f, phi) := vnb-lambda(k, nn, f(phi(k)))

### subspace-ms

    subspace-ms(s, a) := [a, vnb-lambda([sbu_, sbv_], cartesian(a, a), (dist(s))(sbu_, sbv_))]

### subspace-top

    subspace-top(t, a) := [a, {stw_ in power(a): forsome([stu_ in opens(t)], stw_ = intersection(stu_, a))}]

### subst-fun

    subst-fun(mxm_, mxu_) := vnb-lambda(mxw_, expr-set, expr-subst(mxw_, mxm_, mxu_))

### subterm-rel

    subterm-rel(smt_, smk_) := {smp_ in cartesian(msg-set(smt_, smk_), msg-set(smt_, smk_)): forall([smq_ in power(cartesian(msg-set(smt_, smk_), msg-set(smt_, smk_)))], is-subterm-closed(smt_, smk_, smq_) implies smp_ in smq_)}

### sum-ms

    sum-ms(smt_) := [sum-pts(smt_), vnb-lambda([smu_, smv_], cartesian(sum-pts(smt_), sum-pts(smt_)), if(nth(1, smu_) = nth(1, smv_), (dist(bdd-metric(smt_(nth(1, smu_)))))(nth(2, smu_), nth(2, smv_)), 1))]

### sum-pts

    sum-pts(smt_) := {smp_ in cartesian(nn, big-union(smk_, nn, pts(smt_(smk_)))): nth(2, smp_) in pts(smt_(nth(1, smp_)))}

### sum-set

    sum-set(r, s, f) := finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s)

### sum-sigma

    sum-sigma(sgx_, sgc_) := {sga_ in power(disjoint-sum-set(sgx_)): forall([sgk_ in nn], {sgy_ in sgx_(sgk_): [sgk_, sgy_] in sga_} in sgc_(sgk_))}

### sumset

    sumset(m, a, p) := {smx_ in vec(m): forsome([smz_ in p], (vadd(m))(smx_, (act(m))(-1, smz_)) in a)}

### sup-dist

    sup-dist(sds_, sdt_, sdf_, sdg_) := esup(image(vnb-lambda(sdx_, pts(sds_), (dist(sdt_))(sdf_(sdx_), sdg_(sdx_))), pts(sds_)))

### supp

    supp(a, m, f) := {x_ in carr(m): not(f(x_) = zero(a))}

### sym-difference

    sym-difference(sda_, sdb_) := union(complement-in(sda_, sdb_), complement-in(sdb_, sda_))

### taylor-aux

    taylor-aux(d, n, a, b) := vnb-lambda(tu_, ccint(a, b), taylor-sum(d, n, b, tu_))

### taylor-poly

    taylor-poly(f, a, n, x) := series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, ((nth-deriv(f, k))(a) * (x - a) ^ k) * recip(factorial(k))), succ(n))

### taylor-rem

    taylor-rem(d, n, a, b) := (d(0))(b) - taylor-sum(d, n, b, a)

### taylor-sum

    taylor-sum(d, n, b, tv_) := series-partial-sum(taylor-term(d, b, tv_), n)

### taylor-term

    taylor-term(d, b, tv_) := vnb-lambda(tk_, nn, ((d(tk_))(tv_) * (b - tv_) ^ tk_) * recip(factorial(tk_)))

### top-dual

    top-dual(m, t) := {tdf_ in fun(vec(m), rr): is-linear-functional(m, tdf_) and is-hom-top-space(t, seminorm-top(normed-vector-space-as-module(rr-nvs), pair(0, 0), vnb-lambda(nvk_, pair(0, 0), vnrm(rr-nvs))), tdf_)}

### top-group-group

    top-group-group(tgr_) := [carr(tgr_), opr(tgr_), iden(tgr_), inv(tgr_)]

### top-group-space

    top-group-space(tgr_) := [carr(tgr_), topology(tgr_)]

### total-variation

    total-variation(ca, nu) := vnb-lambda(tva_, ca, eplus((pos-variation(ca, nu))(tva_), (neg-variation(ca, nu))(tva_)))

### total-variation-norm

    total-variation-norm(omega, ca, nu) := (total-variation(ca, nu))(omega)

### tprod-box

    tprod-box(ix, tf, bb) := {tpy_ in tprod-pts(ix, tf): forall([tpc_ in bb], tpy_(nth(1, tpc_)) in nth(2, tpc_))}

### tprod-constr

    tprod-constr(ix, tf) := {tpb_ in cartesian(ix, big-union(tpj_, ix, opens(tf(tpj_)))): nth(2, tpb_) in opens(tf(nth(1, tpb_)))}

### tprod-pts

    tprod-pts(ix, tf) := {tpx_ in fun(ix, big-union(tpj_, ix, pts(tf(tpj_)))): forall([tpi_ in ix], tpx_(tpi_) in pts(tf(tpi_)))}

### tproduct-top

    tproduct-top(ix, tf) := [tprod-pts(ix, tf), {tpu_ in power(tprod-pts(ix, tf)): is-tprod-open(ix, tf, tpu_)}]

### tproj

    tproj(ix, tf, i) := vnb-lambda(tpz_, tprod-pts(ix, tf), tpz_(i))

### trace

    trace(pgam, a, b) := image(pgam, ccint(a, b))

### translate

    translate(es, c) := {ltz_ in rr: ltz_ - c in es}

### transpose

    transpose(a, m, n) := matof(n, m, vnb-lambda([tri_, trj_], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, m)), entry(a, trj_, tri_)))

### tri-int

    tri-int(sgf_, sga_, sgb_, sgc_) := (seg-int(sgf_, sga_, sgb_) + seg-int(sgf_, sgb_, sgc_)) + seg-int(sgf_, sgc_, sga_)

### tri-road

    tri-road(tra_, trb_, trc_) := juxta(juxta(seg-path(tra_, trb_), vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(1, 2), (seg-path(trb_, trc_))(trs_ - 1)), 0, 1, 2), vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(2, 3), (seg-path(trc_, tra_))(trs_ - 2)), 0, 2, 3)

### tri-road-deriv

    tri-road-deriv(tra_, trb_, trc_) := juxta(juxta(seg-deriv(tra_, trb_), vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(1, 2), (seg-deriv(trb_, trc_))(trs_ - 1)), 0, 1, 2), vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(2, 3), (seg-deriv(trc_, tra_))(trs_ - 2)), 0, 2, 3)

### true-reach

    true-reach(md7j_) := {md7zz_ in expr-set: forall([md7ww_ in power(expr-set)], md7j_ in md7ww_ implies true-closed(md7ww_) implies md7zz_ in md7ww_)}

### trunc-metric

    trunc-metric(s) := [pts(s), vnb-lambda([u, v], cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), min(1, (dist(s))(u, v)))]

### ultra-seq-ms

    ultra-seq-ms(usx_, usa_) := [fun(nn, usx_), vnb-lambda([usu_, usv_], cartesian(fun(nn, usx_), fun(nn, usx_)), prefix-dist(usa_, usu_, usv_))]

### unitrow

    unitrow(a, n, i) := matof(1, n, vnb-lambda([rw, cl], cartesian(interval(1, 1), interval(1, n)), if(cl = i, one(a), zero(a))))

### upd

    upd(upw_, upn_, upm_) := vnb-lambda(upj_, nn, if(upj_ < upn_, upw_(upj_), upm_))

### var-permute

    var-permute(r, n, s, p) := monalg-pullback(r, multi-index-monoid(interval(1, n)), vnb-lambda(mib_, fun(interval(1, n), nn), mi-reindex(interval(1, n), s, mib_)), p)

### weak-star-top

    weak-star-top(m, t) := subspace-top(tproduct-top(vec(m), vnb-lambda(b6n_, vec(m), metric-top(rr-ms))), top-dual(m, t))

### winding

    winding(wnp_, wnd_, wna_, wnb_, wnz_) := recip((2 * pi) * 1i) * line-int(vnb-lambda(wnw_, difference(cc, singleton(wnz_)), recip(wnw_ - wnz_)), wnp_, wnd_, wna_, wnb_)

### x-minus

    x-minus(a, c) := (add(poly(a)))(monomial(a, one(a), 1), monomial(a, (neg(a))(c), 0))

### x-section

    x-section(ys, cs, x) := {xsy_ in ys: [x, xsy_] in cs}

### x-section-integral

    x-section-integral(xs, ys, cb, nu, f) := vnb-lambda(six_, xs, integral(ys, cb, nu, fn-x-section(ys, f, six_)))

### x-section-measure

    x-section-measure(xs, ys, nu, cs) := vnb-lambda(smx_, xs, nu(x-section(ys, cs, smx_)))

### y-section

    y-section(xs, cs, y) := {ysx_ in xs: [ysx_, y] in cs}

### y-section-integral

    y-section-integral(xs, ca, mu, ys, f) := vnb-lambda(siy_, ys, integral(xs, ca, mu, fn-y-section(xs, f, siy_)))

### y-section-measure

    y-section-measure(xs, ys, mu, cs) := vnb-lambda(smy_, ys, mu(y-section(xs, cs, smy_)))

### zero-order

    zero-order(zou_, zof_, zoa_) := iota(zon_, has-zero-of-order(zou_, zof_, zoa_, zon_))

### zeromat

    zeromat(a, m, n) := matof(m, n, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, m), interval(1, n)), zero(a)))

### zp

    zp(qsp_) := nv-integers(qp-field(qsp_))

### zz-act

    zz-act(g_, k_, a_) := if(k_ in nn, mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g_), a_, k_), (inv(g_))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g_), a_, -k_)))

### zz-bezout-set

    zz-bezout-set(a, b) := {z in zz: forsome([x_ in zz, y_ in zz], z = x_ * a + y_ * b)}

### zz-mod

    zz-mod(a) := quotient-module(ring-as-module(zz-ring), principal-ideal(zz-ring, a))

### zz-pow

    zz-pow(n) := direct-sum(zz-ring, n, vnb-lambda(zpi_, interval(1, n), ring-as-module(zz-ring)))

### zz-residue

    zz-residue(k, m) := choice({zrr_ in interval(1, m): zz-divides(m, k - zrr_)})

### zz-row

    zz-row(n, x) := matof(1, n, vnb-lambda([zra_, zrb_], cartesian(interval(1, 1), interval(1, n)), x(zrb_)))

### zz-units

    zz-units(n) := matof(n, 1, vnb-lambda([zua_, zub_], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, 1)), vnb-lambda(zut_, interval(1, n), entry(unitrow(zz-ring, n, zua_), 1, zut_))))

## Other definitional axioms

Conservative defining `iff`/`==` axioms installed via `add-axiom!`, so absent from the `def-*` registry above but stamped `definitional` in the provenance ledger: property predicates, membership characterisations, `bin*` apply laws, ordinal order, slot reads.  Unfold with `(mac 'NAME)` / `(mac-h 'NAME k)` under the name shown.

### abelian-group-assoc-module-vector-ag

    forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r), c in vec(r)], (vadd(r))((vadd(r))(a, b), c) = (vadd(r))(a, (vadd(r))(b, c))))

### abelian-group-assoc-module-vector-ag-rev-sip-as-module

    forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r), c in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, (vadd(r))(b, c)) = (vadd(r))((vadd(r))(a, b), c)))

### abelian-group-assoc-module-vector-ag-rev-sip-as-module-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r), c in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, (vadd(r))(b, c)) = (vadd(r))((vadd(r))(a, b), c)))

### abelian-group-assoc-module-vector-ag-rev-sip-as-module-rev-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r), c in vec(r)], (vadd(r))((vadd(r))(a, b), c) = (vadd(r))(a, (vadd(r))(b, c))))

### abelian-group-assoc-module-vector-ag-sip-as-module

    forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r), c in vec(r)], (vadd(r))((vadd(r))(a, b), c) = (vadd(r))(a, (vadd(r))(b, c))))

### abelian-group-assoc-module-vector-ag-sip-as-module-csip-as-sip

### abelian-group-assoc-module-vector-ag-sip-as-module-rev-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r), c in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, (vadd(r))(b, c)) = (vadd(r))((vadd(r))(a, b), c)))

### abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-module-vector-ag

    forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, a) = a implies a = vzero(r)))

### abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-module-vector-ag-rev-sip-as-module

    forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, a) = a implies vzero(r) = a))

### abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-module-vector-ag-rev-sip-as-module-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, a) = a implies vzero(r) = a))

### abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-module-vector-ag-rev-sip-as-module-rev-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, a) = a implies a = vzero(r)))

    forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, a) = a implies a = vzero(r)))

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, a) = a implies a = vzero(r)))

### abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-rev-ring-additive-ag

    forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies zero(r) = a))

### abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-ring-additive-ag

    forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies a = zero(r)))

### abelian-group-inverse-unique-module-vector-ag

    forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, b) = vzero(r) implies b = (vneg(r))(a)))

### abelian-group-inverse-unique-module-vector-ag-rev-sip-as-module

    forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, b) = vzero(r) implies (vneg(r))(a) = b))

### abelian-group-inverse-unique-module-vector-ag-rev-sip-as-module-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, b) = vzero(r) implies (vneg(r))(a) = b))

### abelian-group-inverse-unique-module-vector-ag-rev-sip-as-module-rev-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, b) = vzero(r) implies b = (vneg(r))(a)))

### abelian-group-inverse-unique-module-vector-ag-sip-as-module

    forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, b) = vzero(r) implies b = (vneg(r))(a)))

### abelian-group-inverse-unique-module-vector-ag-sip-as-module-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, b) = vzero(r) implies b = (vneg(r))(a)))

### abelian-group-inverse-unique-module-vector-ag-sip-as-module-rev-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, b) = vzero(r) implies (vneg(r))(a) = b))

### abelian-group-opr-comm-rev-ring-additive-ag

    forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(b, a) = (add(r))(a, b)))

### abelian-group-opr-comm-ring-additive-ag

    forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, b) = (add(r))(b, a)))

### abelian-group-right-id-module-vector-ag

    forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, vzero(r)) = a))

### abelian-group-right-id-module-vector-ag-rev-sip-as-module

    forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], a = (vadd(r))(a, vzero(r))))

### abelian-group-right-id-module-vector-ag-rev-sip-as-module-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], a = (vadd(r))(a, vzero(r))))

### abelian-group-right-id-module-vector-ag-rev-sip-as-module-rev-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, vzero(r)) = a))

### abelian-group-right-id-module-vector-ag-sip-as-module

    forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, vzero(r)) = a))

### abelian-group-right-id-module-vector-ag-sip-as-module-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, vzero(r)) = a))

### abelian-group-right-id-module-vector-ag-sip-as-module-rev-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], a = (vadd(r))(a, vzero(r))))

### ag-cancel-right-rev-ring-additive-ag

    forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, c) = (add(r))(b, c) implies b = a))

### ag-cancel-right-ring-additive-ag

    forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, c) = (add(r))(b, c) implies a = b))

### ax-class-ext-def

    ax-class-ext == enc-all(enc-all(enc-imp(enc-all(enc-iff(enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(2)), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(1)))), enc-eq(ebvar(1), ebvar(0)))))

### ax-fun-app-def

    ax-fun-app == enc-all(enc-all(enc-all(enc-imp(enc-in(ebvar(1), enc-fun1(ebvar(2))), enc-iff(enc-eq(enc-apply(ebvar(1), ebvar(0)), enc-apply(ebvar(1), ebvar(0))), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(2)))))))

### ax-limit-iff-def

    ax-limit-iff == enc-all(enc-iff(enc-limit(ebvar(0)), enc-and(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-ordc), enc-and(enc-not(enc-eq(ebvar(0), enc-num(0))), enc-not(enc-ex(enc-and(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-ordc), enc-eq(ebvar(1), enc-succo(ebvar(0))))))))))

### ax-mem-set-def

    ax-mem-set == enc-all(enc-all(enc-imp(enc-in(ebvar(1), ebvar(0)), enc-in(ebvar(1), enc-setc))))

### ax-nn-ind-def

    ax-nn-ind == enc-all(enc-imp(enc-and(enc-in(enc-num(0), ebvar(0)), enc-all(enc-imp(enc-and(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-nnc), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(1))), enc-in(enc-succ(ebvar(0)), ebvar(1))))), enc-all(enc-imp(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-nnc), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(1))))))

### ax-ord-succ-above-def

    ax-ord-succ-above == enc-all(enc-imp(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-ordc), enc-ordlt(ebvar(0), enc-succo(ebvar(0)))))

### ax-succ-nn-def

    ax-succ-nn == enc-all(enc-imp(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-nnc), enc-in(enc-succ(ebvar(0)), enc-nnc)))

### ax-tfi-def

    ax-tfi == enc-all(enc-imp(enc-all(enc-imp(enc-and(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-ordc), enc-all(enc-imp(enc-ordlt(ebvar(0), ebvar(1)), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(2))))), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(1)))), enc-all(enc-imp(enc-in(ebvar(0), enc-ordc), enc-in(ebvar(0), ebvar(1))))))

### ax-zero-nn-def

    ax-zero-nn == enc-in(enc-num(0), enc-nnc)

### baire-ms-def

    baire-ms == ultra-seq-ms(nn, vnb-lambda(bwk_, nn, 2))

### bijection-membership-iff

    forall([x, y, phi], phi in bijection(x, y) iff phi in fun(x, y) and forall([a in x, b in x], phi(a) = phi(b) implies a = b) and forall([w in y], forsome([z in x], phi(z) = w)))

### binary-divide-def

    forall([a, b], (/)(a, b) == a * recip(b))

### binary-minus-def

    forall([a, b], a - b == a + -b)

### binneg-apply

    forall([x], binneg(x) == -x)

### binplus-apply

    forall([x, y], binplus(x, y) == x + y)

### bintimes-apply

    forall([x, y], bintimes(x, y) == x * y)

### cantor-ms-def

    cantor-ms == product-metric(vnb-lambda(ctk_, nn, rm-two-ms))

### caratheodory-sets-membership

    forall([omega, mu, es_], es_ in caratheodory-sets(omega, mu) iff es_ in power(omega) and is-caratheodory-measurable(omega, mu, es_))

### cayley-def

    cayley == lft(1, -1i, 1, 1i)

### cc-coords-def

    cc-coords == vnb-lambda(zv_, cc, [real-part(zv_), imag-part(zv_)])

### cc-hat-def

    cc-hat == union(cc, singleton(cc-inf))

### cc-hat-opens-def

    cc-hat-opens == {uh_ in power(cc-hat): uh_ subset cc and is-open(cc-ms, uh_) or forsome([kh_ in power(cc)], kh_ subset cc and is-compact(subspace-ms(cc-ms, kh_)) and uh_ = difference(cc-hat, kh_))}

### cc-hat-top-def

    cc-hat-top = [cc-hat, cc-hat-opens]

### cc-hat-top@opens

    opens(cc-hat-top) == cc-hat-opens

### cc-hat-top@pts

    pts(cc-hat-top) == cc-hat

### cc-matrices-def

    cc-matrices == {cmq_ in mat(2, 2, rr): forsome([cma_ in rr, cmb_ in rr], cmq_ = cc-matrix-of(cma_, cmb_))}

### cc-matrix-rep-def

    cc-matrix-rep == vnb-lambda(cmz_, cc, cc-matrix-of(real-part(cmz_), imag-part(cmz_)))

### cc-ms-def

    cc-ms = [cc, vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(cc, cc), magnitude(x - y))]

### cc-ms@dist

    dist(cc-ms) == vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(cc, cc), magnitude(x - y))

### cc-ms@pts

    pts(cc-ms) == cc

### cc-normed-field-def

    cc-normed-field = [cc, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, cc, -x_), 0, 1, vnb-lambda(x_, cc, magnitude(x_))]

### cc-normed-field@add

    add(cc-normed-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ + y_)

### cc-normed-field@carr

    carr(cc-normed-field) == cc

### cc-normed-field@fnrm

    fnrm(cc-normed-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, cc, magnitude(x_))

### cc-normed-field@mul

    mul(cc-normed-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ * y_)

### cc-normed-field@neg

    neg(cc-normed-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, cc, -x_)

### cc-normed-field@one

    one(cc-normed-field) == 1

### cc-normed-field@zero

    zero(cc-normed-field) == 0

### cc-of-pair-def

    cc-of-pair == vnb-lambda(pv_, cartesian(rr, rr), nth(1, pv_) + nth(2, pv_) * 1i)

### cc-partial-prod-succ

    forall([cpf_, cpn_ in nn], cc-partial-prod(cpf_, succ(cpn_)) == cc-partial-prod(cpf_, cpn_) * cpf_(cpn_))

### cc-partial-prod-zero

    forall([cpf_], cc-partial-prod(cpf_, 0) == 1)

### cc2-sphere-def

    cc2-sphere == {hsw_ in cartesian(cc, cc): magnitude(nth(1, hsw_)) * magnitude(nth(1, hsw_)) + magnitude(nth(2, hsw_)) * magnitude(nth(2, hsw_)) = 1}

### change-of-basis-det-unit-sip-as-module

    forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies is-commutative-ring(scal(r)) implies forall([n, xs, xs2, ys, ys2, sm, am, am2], xs in mat(n, 1, vec(r)) implies xs2 in mat(n, 1, vec(r)) implies ys in mat(n, 1, vec(r)) implies ys2 in mat(n, 1, vec(r)) implies generates(sip-as-module(r), n, xs) implies rel-free(sip-as-module(r), n, xs) implies generates(sip-as-module(r), n, xs2) implies rel-free(sip-as-module(r), n, xs2) implies spans(sip-as-module(r), n, ys, sm) implies rel-free(sip-as-module(r), n, ys) implies spans(sip-as-module(r), n, ys2, sm) implies rel-free(sip-as-module(r), n, ys2) implies am in mat(n, n, carr(scal(r))) implies am2 in mat(n, n, carr(scal(r))) implies ys = matact(sip-as-module(r), am, xs) implies ys2 = matact(sip-as-module(r), am2, xs2) implies forsome([cbw_ in carr(scal(r))], forsome([cbv_ in carr(scal(r))], (mul(scal(r)))(cbw_, cbv_) = one(scal(r))) and det(scal(r), n, am2) = (mul(scal(r)))(cbw_, det(scal(r), n, am)))))

### change-of-basis-det-unit-sip-as-module-csip-as-sip

    forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies is-commutative-ring(scal(r)) implies forall([n, xs, xs2, ys, ys2, sm, am, am2], xs in mat(n, 1, vec(r)) implies xs2 in mat(n, 1, vec(r)) implies ys in mat(n, 1, vec(r)) implies ys2 in mat(n, 1, vec(r)) implies generates(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), n, xs) implies rel-free(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), n, xs) implies generates(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), n, xs2) implies rel-free(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), n, xs2) implies spans(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), n, ys, sm) implies rel-free(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), n, ys) implies spans(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), n, ys2, sm) implies rel-free(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), n, ys2) implies am in mat(n, n, carr(scal(r))) implies am2 in mat(n, n, carr(scal(r))) implies ys = matact(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), am, xs) implies ys2 = matact(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), am2, xs2) implies forsome([cbw_ in carr(scal(r))], forsome([cbv_ in carr(scal(r))], (mul(scal(r)))(cbw_, cbv_) = one(scal(r))) and det(scal(r), n, am2) = (mul(scal(r)))(cbw_, det(scal(r), n, am)))))

### cips-ip-add-left

    forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v), z_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))((vadd(v))(x_, y_), z_) = (ip(v))(x_, z_) + (ip(v))(y_, z_)))

### cips-ip-conj-sym

    forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(y_, x_) = conjugate((ip(v))(x_, y_))))

### cips-ip-definite

    forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, x_) = 0 iff x_ = vzero(v)))

### cips-ip-homog-left

    forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))((act(v))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * (ip(v))(x_, y_)))

### cips-ip-zero-vector

    forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, x_) = 0 implies x_ = vzero(v)))

### cips-scal-carrier

    forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies carr(scal(v)) = cc)

### cips-vadd-vzero

    forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (vadd(v))(vzero(v), x_) = x_))

### clf-def

    clf == {mce_ in expr-set: wf-fml(mce_) and expr-lc(mce_)}

### closed-intervals-def

    closed-intervals == {oci_ in power(rr): oci_ = empty-set or forsome([oca_ in rr, ocb_ in rr], oca_ <= ocb_ and oci_ = ccint(oca_, ocb_))}

### clt-def

    clt == {mce_ in expr-set: wf-term(mce_) and expr-lc(mce_)}

### comb-kk-succ

    forall([r, x, y, m in nn], comb-kk(r, x, y, succ(m)) == vnb-lambda(k, zz, (add(r))((mul(r))(x, (comb-kk(r, x, y, m))(k - 1)), (mul(r))(y, (comb-kk(r, x, y, m))(k)))))

### comb-kk-zero

    forall([r, x, y], comb-kk(r, x, y, 0) == vnb-lambda(k, zz, if(k = 0, one(r), zero(r))))

### conv-level-succ

    forall([cvm_, cve_, cvn_ in nn], conv-level(cvm_, cve_, succ(cvn_)) == {cvz_ in vec(cvm_): forsome([cvx_ in conv-level(cvm_, cve_, cvn_), cvy_ in cve_, cvt_ in rr], 0 <= cvt_ and cvt_ <= 1 and cvz_ = (vadd(cvm_))((act(cvm_))(1 - cvt_, cvx_), (act(cvm_))(cvt_, cvy_)))})

### ideal-stage-succ

    forall([sbrt_, sbrk_, sbrks_, sbrs_, sbrn_ in nn], ideal-stage(sbrt_, sbrk_, sbrks_, sbrs_, succ(sbrn_)) == {sbrm_ in msg-set(sbrt_, sbrk_): forsome([sbrg_ in key-ideal-gen(sbrt_, sbrk_, empty-set, ideal-stage(sbrt_, sbrk_, sbrks_, sbrs_, sbrn_)), sbry_ in sbrks_], sbrm_ = encr(sbrg_, sbry_))})

### imag-part-def

    forall([z], imag-part(z) == (z - conjugate(z)) * recip(2 * 1i))

### image-membership-iff

    forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-field(a, b, f) iff is-normed-field(a) and is-normed-field(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((add(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (add(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((mul(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (mul(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((neg(a))(x1_)) = (neg(b))(f(x1_))) and f(zero(a)) = zero(b) and f(one(a)) = one(b) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], (fnrm(a))(x1_) = (fnrm(b))(f(x1_))))

### is-hom-ringoid-def

### is-hom-semi-inner-product-space-def

### is-hom-setoid-def

### keep-set-membership

    forall([phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha, y_], y_ in keep-set(phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha) iff y_ in grd and y_ = phi(alpha) and forall([z in kset], [z, y_] in porel))

### lastcoeff-set-membership

    forall([md, p, u, sm, r_], r_ in lastcoeff-set(md, p, u, sm) iff r_ in carr(scal(md)) and forsome([c_ in mat(1, succ(p), carr(scal(md)))], entry(c_, 1, succ(p)) = r_ and lincomb(md, succ(p), c_, u) in sm))

### lcd-stage-succ

    forall([mxn_ in nn], lcd-stage(succ(mxn_)) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(succ(mxn_)), if(mxe_ in expr-stage(mxn_), (lcd-stage(mxn_))(mxe_), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 3, {mxd_ in nn: forall([mxw_ in make-set(nth(3, mxe_))], mxd_ in (lcd-stage(mxn_))(mxw_))}, {mxd_ in nn: forall([mxw_ in make-set(nth(4, mxe_))], mxd_ in (lcd-stage(mxn_))(mxw_)) and mxd_ + nth(3, mxe_) in (lcd-stage(mxn_))(nth(5, mxe_))}))))

### lcd-stage-zero

    lcd-stage(0) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(0), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 1, {mxd_ in nn: nth(2, mxe_) < mxd_}, nn))

### length-cons

    forall([x, l], x in set implies length(cons(x, l)) = succ(length(l)))

### lft-group-def

    lft-group = [lfts, vnb-lambda([lfg_, lfh_], cartesian(lfts, lfts), compose(lfg_, lfh_)), id-fun(cc-hat), vnb-lambda(lfg_, lfts, inverse-bij(lfg_, cc-hat, cc-hat))]

### lft-group@carr

    carr(lft-group) == lfts

### lft-group@iden

    iden(lft-group) == id-fun(cc-hat)

### lft-group@inv

    inv(lft-group) == vnb-lambda(lfg_, lfts, inverse-bij(lfg_, cc-hat, cc-hat))

### lft-group@opr

    opr(lft-group) == vnb-lambda([lfg_, lfh_], cartesian(lfts, lfts), compose(lfg_, lfh_))

### lfts-def

    lfts == {lff_ in fun(cc-hat, cc-hat): forsome([lfa_ in cc, lfb_ in cc, lfc_ in cc, lfd_ in cc], not(lfa_ * lfd_ - lfb_ * lfc_ = 0) and lff_ = lft(lfa_, lfb_, lfc_, lfd_))}

### limit-ord-iff

    forall([lambda], limit-ord(lambda) iff lambda in ord and not(lambda = 0) and not(forsome([alpha in ord], lambda = succ_ord(alpha))))

    forall([r], is-abelian-group(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), j in nn, k in nn], mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, j + k) = (opr(r))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, j), mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, k))))
